L4092: | 2 | **Transmit return current** | Up to 1 A (and 13 A at the polarizer) must return to J802 / J905; if any of it shares copper with the receiver pour it appears as a differential signal in front of a gain of 1000 | TX out and TX return both on GND, terminal on the front edge directly under the power stage; receiver entirely on AGND; single tie at NT1 | TX and its return routed as a **close pair, loop area < 1 cm²**; the coil return never touches the AGND pour; polarizer pair likewise, ≥ 2 mm wide | T-17, T-22 |
L4093: | 3 | **Coupling-capacitor recovery** | After transmit leakage saturates the LNA, an innocent 1 µF / 100 k interstage coupling (1.6 Hz) takes 300 ms to recover — four T2* periods, and the classic reason a first NMR build shows nothing | Interstage corner deliberately at **1.59 kHz** (C720 10 nF / R721 10 k): 0.3 ms recovery for 0.016 % of amplitude and 1.0° of phase at 89.4 kHz (D-39) | Do not "improve" the corner by enlarging C720; keep C720/R721 adjacent to the switch | T-19 |
L4094: | 4 | **Switch charge injection** | The DG419 injects ~60 pC.  On the 1.25 nF tank that is a 48 mV kick ringing for 36 µs at full gain; the TS5A23157 injects 3 pC LO-synchronously, which is a DC offset after the mixer | Blanking placed **between the stages**, where 60 pC lands on 10 nF as 6 mV (τ = 100 µs); mixer differential with 0.1 % difference amps (CMRR 54 dB) so the LO-synchronous injection cancels (D-38, D-39) | The two IF paths must be **mirror images** (R914–R917 as matched pairs, placed symmetrically) or the cancellation is lost | T-19, T-20 |
L4095: | 5 | **PowerPAD thermal paths** | OPA564 (0.70 W quiescent, 4.1 W in the worst continuous case) and DRV8871 (3.6 A) have no other heat path than their exposed pads | OPA564 is the **pad-down DWP part** (C188648) with the pad at V− = GND (θ_JA 33 °C/W on a high-K board); duty cycle held to ≤ 1 % by TX_EN (D-35); DRV8871 datasheet §10.1 | OPA564 pad soldered onto the GND pour with a via array; DRV8871 pad with a **3 × 3 array of 0.3 mm vias on a 1.0 mm grid** to a bottom-layer GND pour; OUT traces ≥ 2 mm; no precision part within 15 mm | T-17 thermal, T-21 |

L4207: 217:      <p class="foot">Built from <code>instrument.scad</code>, <code>probe.scad</code>, <code>housing.scad</code> and <code>cut-housing.scad</code> (<code>mechanical/</code> in this repository), 2 Oct 2026. The pulse experiment solves the Bloch equations for every voxel of the water (2.5 mm grid, 3555 voxels in the tube) in exact fields computed in Mathematica (<code>build/nmr_exact.wls</code>): Biot–Savart for the B0 pair with its real winding cross-section and for each of the class coil's 400 turns, including the direction of B1 at every voxel. With identical inputs it matches SpinDynamica (Bengs and Levitt, Magn. Reson. Chem. 2017, doi:10.1002/mrc.4642) to 0.1 % in signal, 1 Hz in line position and 1 % in T2*. Then reciprocity pickup, the Q = 10 tank and the receiver noise (15.6 nV/√Hz over 15 kHz). Shown in the frame rotating at f_tx: in the lab the magnetization turns 89 400 times a second.</p>

L4272: NMR sequence used by T-19 / T-20 (circuits doc §9.3): RX_BLANK low ≥ 20 µs before TX_EN high; TX_EN high for the pulse

L4390:   "message": "## Firmware audit: class-board-2026 (branch w1-yi-tsai), NMR path\n\nEverything was read-only. I built both environments in a copy under `C:\\Users\\Liang\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\claude\\audit-firmware\\fw\\` and changed nothing in the repo. Paths below are relative to `class-board-2026/`. Anything marked **[est]** or **[analysis]** has not been measured on hardware; no hardware run is recorded anywhere.\n\n### 1. How one scan runs today\n- **Task:** `xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(\"nmrseq\", stack 12288, priority 5, core 1)` (`Sequencer.cpp:47-49, 235`). It sleeps on `ulTaskNotifyTake` (`:249`) until `start()` (`:254-273`) calls `xTaskNotifyGive`. No timer interrupt and no ISR is used anywhere in the NMR path.\n- **Timing primitive:** `waitUntil()` (`Sequencer.cpp:71-77`) polls `esp_timer_get_time()`. It busy-spins below 1.5 ms and calls `vTaskDelay` above that.\n- **Set preparation** (`prepareHardware`, `:294-312`), once per scan set over I2C:\n  - Si5351 CLK0 = 50 MHz with a PLLA reset.\n  - `setLo` (`:314-321`): CLK1 = 4·f_lo, PLLB reset, Johnson-counter /CLR pulse through two TCA9535 I2C writes (`Tca9535.cpp:117-122`).\n  - DDS: RESET, FREQ0, PHASE0 = 0, PHASE1 = 180, RESET cleared.\n- **One scan** (`runOneScan`, `:386-500`):\n  1. Optional polarize: digitalWrite FET/H-bridge, then `waitMs`.\n  2. Phase: `dds.setPhase(0, φ)` and `setPhase(1, φ+90)`, PSEL = 0. Each is an SPI write that takes the SPI mutex (`:414-416`).\n  3. RX_BLANK low, wait `t_blank_pre_us` (`:419-420`).\n  4. Pulse: `on = esp_timer_get_time(); digitalWrite(TX_EN, HIGH); waitUntil(on + t90); digitalWrite(TX_EN, LOW)` (`:427-430`). This is a software-timed gate.\n  5. Echo only: `waitUntil(pulseEnd + tau)`, then `dds.selectPhase(true)` (SPI write under the mutex), then the 180° gate (`:436-451`).\n  6. Wait `t_dead_us`, unblank (`:456-457`). Wait `t_acq_start_us`, take `spibus::Guard g(200)` (`:472`), then `adc.burst()` (`:478`).\n  7. Blank again, `readFlags()` (`:489-490`). This is a no-op on v0.7 because `EXP_BIT_IFLAG/TFLAG = -1` (`pins.h:96-97`).\n- **ADC pacing** (`Ads8688.cpp:163-225`):\n  - Reprograms AUTO_SEQ (an extra SPI transaction), opens one Arduino `SPI.beginTransaction`, sends AUTO_RST.\n  - Per sample: `paceUntil(t0 + s·period)` busy-poll, then per channel CS low by register write, blocking `SPI.transfer32(NO_OP)`, CS high, flip the top bit, store to PSRAM.\n  - If it falls behind it catches up back-to-back with no overrun flag. It reports only the average `achieved_hz` (`:220-223`).\n- **Buffers:** `heap_caps_malloc(MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM)` at `config` time, grow-only (`Sequencer.cpp:95-155`). Defaults: raw 800 kB, frame 400 kB, FFT 2×64 kB. Nothing is allocated per scan in the sequencer.\n- **DSP per scan** (`processScan`, `:502-539`, in `Dsp.cpp`):\n  - DC offset: mean of the last 10 % of the raw record if `t_acq_ms ≥ 1000`, else the whole record (`Dsp.cpp:36-40`).\n  - Boxcar decimation, which also does code-to-volts (`:42-61`).\n  - **No digital mixing:** the record stays at the IF.\n  - CYCLOPS: rotate by −φ (`:63-74`), φ = 90·(s mod 4) (`Sequencer.cpp:573`).\n  - Running mean in the frame payload under `frameLock_` (`:522-531`).\n  - Status-only spectrum `dsp::analyse` (`:536`): first ≤16384 samples, Hann window, radix-2 FFT, parabolic peak, median/1.17741 noise (`Dsp.cpp:137-212`).\n- **Network:**\n  - The sequencer sets `recordReady_`. The main loop's `NmrBlock::loop` calls `consumeRecord` → `ws.binaryAll` (`NmrBlock.cpp:86-91`, `main.cpp:74-78`). That is the whole averaged record, a 28-byte header plus n×8 bytes (400 kB at defaults), after **every** scan.\n  - JSON status goes out at 20 Hz. `get_record` resends the last record.\n  - Header `block_id` is hard-coded to 9 (`Sequencer.cpp:547`); nothing reads it.\n\n### 2. ADS8688 driver compared with the docs\n**What the code does:** Arduino `SPI` (the global SPIClass on SPI2), `SPISettings(17 MHz, MSBFIRST, SPI_MODE1)` (`Ads8688.cpp:34, 181`), one 32-bit `transfer32` per conversion, CS by GPIO register writes (`:40-41`).\n\n**Discrepancies:**\n\n| # | Doc / comment (quote) | Code |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | NMR-FIRMWARE.md:139 \"Uses `spi_device_polling_transmit` on a dedicated device handle at 17 MHz\" | Arduino `SPI.transfer32` on the shared global object, no ESP-IDF device handle (`Ads8688.cpp:181-200`). |\n| 2 | NMR-FIRMWARE.md:133 `begin()` \"…NO_OP, AUTO_RST\" | `begin` sends 2×NO_OP, 8 range writes and an AUTO_SEQ write. No AUTO_RST; that happens in `burst` (`:94-106, 185`). |\n| 3 | NMR-FIRMWARE.md:138 \"as fast as the loop allows … returns samples per channel achieved\" | Paced to `rate_hz` and always returns `n_per_channel` (`:176, 225`). |\n| 4 | Ads8688.h:33 \"17 MHz\" | **[est]** The ESP32 APB divider gives 80/5 = **16 MHz** effective. |\n| 5 | NMR-FIRMWARE.md:192 \"busy-waits inside a `portDISABLE_INTERRUPTS()`-free critical region no longer than the pulse itself\" | There is no critical region at all. The task spins for the whole acquisition (up to 4 s) at priority 5. |\n| 6 | NMR-FIRMWARE.md:179 \"priority high\"; Sequencer.h:13-14 \"at priority 5 on core 1 nothing here gets preempted except by interrupts\" | False. sdkconfig has `CONFIG_ARDUINO_EVENT_RUNNING_CORE 1` (arduino_events task, priority about 19). AsyncTCP is **priority 10, unpinned** (`AsyncTCP.h:37,50`). Both can preempt it. |\n| 7 | Sequencer.cpp:410-411 \"the PSEL bit, about 2 us of SPI\" | The write first takes the SPI mutex (`Ad9834.cpp:41`, Guard 50 ms), then beginTransaction and digitalWrite FSYNC. **[est]** 5–15 µs normally, up to 50 ms worst case. |\n| 8 | Sequencer.cpp:571 \"the app offers multiples of four\" (n_avg) | `nmr.js:77` uses `step=\"1\"`. |\n| 9 | Si5351.cpp:33-35 \"well under a millihertz\" | **[analysis]** About 9 mHz at CLK1 and about 2.2 mHz at the LO (23.8 Hz PLL step / (668·4) / 4). Harmless, but the comment is wrong. |\n| 10 | Ad9834.cpp:82 sets PHASE1 = 90; Sequencer.cpp:309 sets 180; NMR-FIRMWARE §3.7 says 180, then each scan overwrites it with φ+90 | Three different claims. |\n\n**Per-sample cost and maximum rate [est]:**\n- One 32-bit frame at 16 MHz is 2.0 µs. Arduino `spiTransferLongNL` overhead is about 0.5–1 µs. So one channel costs about 2.7–3.2 µs and a pair about 6–7 µs, plus about 0.3 µs for `esp_timer_get_time`.\n- That gives roughly **140–165 kS/s per channel sustained** for 2 channels.\n- The default 100 kS/s (10 µs budget) is achievable. The accepted maximum of 250 kS/s (`Sequencer.cpp:176`) is not.\n- When the rate is not met, the header still says `cfg.rate_hz/decim` (`Sequencer.h:81`, `:551`), so **the frequency axis is silently scaled wrong**. Only the status `rate_hz` shows the achieved value.\n- **VERIFY:** CS-high time between back-to-back frames is only tens of ns (`:197-199`). Check this against the ADS8688 CS-high/conversion timing and the AUTO_RST data latency.\n\n### 3. AD9834 and Si5351\n- **AD9834** (`Ad9834.cpp`):\n  - SPI mode 2 at 10 MHz (exact: 80/8), FSYNC through digitalWrite. Every word takes the mutex and opens its own transaction.\n  - Control word: B28 = 1, PIN/SW = 0.\n  - Frequency: control word, then FREQ0 LSB `0x4000|w&0x3FFF`, then MSB (`:85-107`). Resolution 50 MHz/2²⁸ = **0.186 Hz/LSB**.\n  - Phase: `0xC000/0xE000 | round(deg·4096/360)`, which is **0.088°/LSB** (`:109-120`).\n  - PSEL is a control-word bit (`:127-130`).\n  - **Phase switching inside a sequence works** with 2 registers via PSEL: echo already does it, and CYCLOPS sets the phase per scan. It costs one locked SPI write each time.\n  - Faster, deterministic switching would need PIN/SW = 1 and GPIO42 (`PIN_DDS_PSEL`). That loses the RESET and SLEEP bits, because those pins are tied low.\n- **Si5351** (`Si5351.cpp`), 25 MHz crystal, I2C at 400 kHz:\n  - CLK0: integer search gives PLLA 900 MHz / 18 = 50 MHz exact (`:147-161`).\n  - CLK1: R-divider doubling up to ≥1 MHz, largest even integer MS, fractional PLLB with denominator 1048575 (`:197-238`).\n  - The register encoding matches AN619. Control bytes 0x4F / 0x6F are correct.\n  - Bug: if `setClk1` fails (f_lo below about 1.3 kHz), `config` does not check the return value. `actualLo()` then falls back to the *requested* value (`NmrBlock.cpp:39-42, 160`).\n- **Core coherence problem [analysis, high impact]:**\n  - TX (DDS on PLLA) and LO (PLLB, plus a Johnson counter cleared by I2C once per set) free-run at different frequencies. The demodulated IF phase is therefore set by **absolute time** modulo 1/f_IF (185 µs).\n  - Nothing ties the pulse or the first ADC sample to that beat. Scan start comes from `vTaskDelay`/`esp_timer` on the ESP32's own crystal.\n  - **Each scan's record should carry a random phase, so coherent averaging and CYCLOPS cannot work on hardware.**\n  - SIM hides this: `simBurst` starts every record at phase φ_pulse (`Sequencer.cpp:627-629`).\n  - The redesign needs a hardware-synchronised start (gate triggered from a TX/LO-derived edge, a reference channel, or a homodyne LO). **VERIFY first on hardware.**\n\n### 4. Hard-real-time hazards\n- **Software TX gate:** if anything preempts between `digitalWrite HIGH` and `LOW`, the transmitter stays on for the length of that preemption, possibly milliseconds. AsyncTCP (prio 10), arduino_events (core 1) and ISRs can all do this. I_FLAG/T_FLAG are not connected, so nothing catches an over-pulse. The bench `pulse` command does the same on the **loop task at prio 1** (`NmrBlock.cpp:189-191`, `Sequencer.cpp:343-357`), which is worse.\n- **Mutex waits inside the sequence:** `spibus::Guard(200)` at acquisition start (`:472`), and the DDS Guard(50) inside tau for echo. b1's `readAll(0)` can hold the bus when the sequencer preempts it. **[est]** About 50–100 µs delay with priority inheritance.\n- **Sample-time jitter:** FreeRTOS tick (1 kHz) and other ISRs on core 1, plus higher-priority tasks. Late samples are taken back-to-back with no detection. Flash writes stall both cores' cache: LittleFS alarm saves (`AlarmEngine.cpp:143`) and OTA, neither refused during a scan.\n- **No heap, logging, JSON, String or WiFi** in the pulse or acquisition path. The 400 kB `ws.binaryAll` copy runs under `frameLock_` on the main task. `processScan` waits on it with `portMAX_DELAY` (`:522`), which jitters the repetition time but not the pulse.\n- **Loop task starved:** the loop task shares core 1 and is blocked for the whole 2–4 s spin, so the status broadcast stalls every scan. The CPU1 idle task is not watchdogged (sdkconfig only checks CPU0), so it does not crash.\n- **b1 `set_range`** is not refused during a scan. It can change the AI7/AI8 scaling between scans of an average (`InputsBlock.cpp:57-69`).\n- **Validation gaps:** `t_acq_start_us < t_dead_us` is accepted (sampling while still blanked). `status()` reads `spec_` non-atomically.\n\n### 5. Host side\n- **instrument.py:**\n  - `nmr` (`:323-369`): sends only f_tx, f_lo, sequence, n_avg; polls status; calls `get_record`.\n  - **Bug:** `next_binary` (`:104-118`) returns the *oldest* parked kind-3 frame from the `deque(maxlen=8)` (`:82`). Per-scan frames are parked while waiting, so the CLI typically returns the **scan-1 record**, not the final average.\n  - `nmr_spectrum` (`:287-302`): `np.hanning(n)` over the **entire** record (50 000 pts at defaults), no zero-padding, scale `2/sum(w)`.\n  - **[est]** For T2* = 0.4 s over 2 s, a full Hann window gives about **10 dB less SNR** than a matched exponential (relative 22 vs 71). Firmware and JS window only the first 0.655 s and lose about 3 dB.\n  - `nmr_peak`: 2 Hz guard, `skip = n/100`, `/1.177`, no interpolation. CSV time starts at the first sample, not the pulse.\n- **nmr.js:** its own radix-2 FFT (`:266-306`), Hann with `(nUse-1)` (`:321`), `MAX_FFT = 16384` (`:37`), scale `2/wsum` (`:329`), guard `max(2, 2/df)` bins, `skip = nfft/100`, `/1.177`. It exposes only 8 settings, with defaults duplicated (`:26-35`).\n- **Three different spectrum implementations:**\n  - Firmware: periodic Hann, `1/(sumW·√2)` scaling (V rms per quadrature), skirt `nfft/64+4`, parabolic peak.\n  - JS: symmetric Hann, `2/sum`, 16k points.\n  - Python: symmetric Hann, `2/sum`, full record.\n  - `2/sum` is the real-signal convention, so peak amplitudes differ by 2√2 (9 dB) between the board and the clients, and SNRs differ because of the window lengths.\n- **Four simulators:** `simBurst`, the `Ads8688` SIM burst (`:204-211`), `dev-nmr.html:39-105`, and the doc.\n\n### 6. I re-ran the Biot–Savart part of `nmr_exact.wls` at 5 voxels and it reproduces `fieldmap-tube.json` (df, b1, phi) to every printed digit.\n- `physics.js` reproduces its README table exactly. Its Bloch rotation and precession sign are correct (checked against an RK4 integration of dM/dt = γM×B).\n- **The reference script `nmr_exact.wls` has a handedness bug.** The SpinDynamica pulse rotates about +H, but the free precession afterwards uses e^{-iΩt}. These are opposite senses.\n  - Evidence: if I flip `physics.js`'s pulse axis to match SpinDynamica, its T2* moves from 79.88 to exactly 80.52 ms (tube) and from 40.96 to exactly 41.52 ms (bottle). Those are the SpinDynamica numbers.\n  - So the 0.6 ms \"agreement\" gap in the README is this convention mismatch, not numerical error. `physics.js` is the self-consistent one.\n- **The \"FWHM 6 Hz\" linewidth is not physical.** It is the spectral width set by the 0.2 s record (rectangular window, 1.207/T). With the exact tube map, record lengths of 0.1 / 0.2 / 0.4 / 0.8 s give 12 / 6 / 3 / 1 Hz. Do not use it as golden data.\n- **Three models put the line at different frequencies for the same 1.5 A:**\n\n| Model | Line at 1.5 A |\n|---|---|\n| `physics.js` approximate fields (B0nom = 89400/γ̄ fixed, `physics.js:20`) | 89 400 Hz |\n| Mathematica, finite cross-section winding (2.10347 mT) | 89 560 Hz |\n| `spectrum.py`, thin-wire Helmholtz with N = 312 (2.10407 mT, `spectrum.py:74-75`) | 89 585 Hz |\n\n  - The README's \"thin-wire formula 2.0997 mT\" (`README.md:48`, `nmr_exact.wls:67`) is wrong. 2.0997 mT is the design target before N was rounded up from 311.36 to 312. Thin-wire with 312 turns gives 2.10407 mT.\n  - The `probe.scad:296` echo prints the target field, not the field the coils produce.\n  - The \"retune to the tank\" current (`instrument.scad:107`; the `test-physics` \"1.484 A\" case) assumes the line is at 89 400 Hz. With the exact field it should be about 1.5×88469/89560 ≈ **1.4817 A** (estimate).\n- **The four models do not agree on the signal chain:**\n\n| Model | Signal | T2* | Gain | Noise |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Firmware SIM | \"20 µV at coil\", 18 mV at ADC | 0.4 s | ×900 | 3 mV uniform |\n| `physics.js` | 0.90 µV emf, 8.8 µV at tank, about 0.2 V at ADC | 72–80 ms | ×25 500 | 49 mV rms |\n| `spectrum.py` | firmware SIM constants (0.018 / 0.003 / 0.002 / DC) | intrinsic T2 = 2 s, plus 4th-order field spread | — | — |\n\n- **`spectrum.py` uses a different sample model:**\n  - It uses r = 15 mm, the tube's outer radius (`spectrum.py:87`); `physics.js` and Mathematica use 14 mm.\n  - It keeps only x from −50 to +25 mm, the water inside the winding. The others use −58 to +45 mm.\n  - So its field spread is 193 Hz, against 298 Hz in Mathematica.\n  - Its \"decay\" T2* (1.03 s, from sliding-window peak height) is a different quantity from `physics.js`'s 1/e envelope T2*.\n- **`nmr_exact.wls` cannot run in full here.** SpinDynamica is not installed (no `SPINDYNAMICA` variable, no `sd371` folder, none under the user profile or the Wolfram install). The Biot–Savart part runs at about 0.03 s per voxel, so a full field map takes about 2 min (estimate). The README says about 5 min per run including SpinDynamica.\n\n## 1. The models\n\n**`simulator/physics.js`** (SI units; M in A/m; emf in V)\n- **Field, approximate mode:** Helmholtz 4th-order term −1.152(y⁴−3y²ρ²+⅜ρ⁴)/R⁴, with y along the pair axis (L56-62). Earth's field is added along B0 and in quadrature across it.\n- **Field, exact mode:** from the maps, B = (89400+df)/γ̄·(I/1.5) + B_earth (L96-98).\n- **B1, approximate mode:** on-axis finite solenoid μ0N/L·½[(x+h)/√… − (x−h)/√…] (L32-35, L101). It ignores the radial variation and the real two-layer winding, which is why it gives t90 = 451 µs.\n- **Pulse:** the rotating component is half the linear field (L104). The rotation is exact Rodrigues about −B_eff in the f_tx frame, including the B1 direction φ (L64-70, L103-108).\n- **Coil current:** iTx = 7.95/|6.7 + jωL| (L91). It ignores R813 (4.7 Ω) and the tank capacitor on the shared coil.\n- **Magnetization:** Curie law M0 = Nγ²ħ²B/(4kT) (L107).\n- **Pickup:** reciprocity, emf = ω·B1⊥/I·M⊥·dV (L120). It uses ω_tx rather than the local Larmor frequency (about 0.2 % error).\n- **Free precession:** M⊥·e^{−iΔωt}·e^{−t/T2}, with received phase e^{−iφ} (L122-131).\n- **Relaxation:** T1 recovery appears only in `state()` (L196). Averaging uses a steady-state Mz from the centre voxel's flip, (1−E1)/(1−E1cosθ) (L111-114).\n- **Tank:** a scalar Lorentzian gain Q/√(1+(2QΔf/f0)²) at the line frequency (L136). No phase shift and no noise shaping.\n- **Detection:** the signal is conjugated so that a line above f_tx lands above the 5.4 kHz IF; I = Re, Q = Im of e^{+i2πf_IF t} (L142-143).\n- **Noise and blanking:** Gaussian, σ = 15.6 nV/√Hz·√15 kHz/√n per channel (L137). That is about 10 % above en·√(12.5 kHz) at 25 kS/s complex (estimate; needs checking). The first 1.2 ms are zeroed (L145).\n- **Line position:** phase slope over the first 1 ms (L177-185).\n- **Linewidth and spectrum:** naive DFTs (L156-160, L203-216).\n- **Not modelled:** spin echo, pulse ringdown, the tank's transient.\n\n**`mechanical/spectrum.py`**\n- Relative field offsets from the 4th-order Helmholtz term over a uniform disk sample (L89-100).\n- Signal amplitude scales as f² times the tank response (L133). `f_tank` defaults to f_tx, not 88 469 Hz (L70).\n- Lines are placed on 0.5 Hz FFT bins, then shifted by the t0 dead time and multiplied by e^{−t/T2} (L106-120).\n- Hum, noise and DC offsets are the firmware SIM constants (L118-120).\n- Voltage-drive heating: dT += I²R0(1+αdT)·t_rep/(m·c), with I = I_set/(1+αdT) (L123-136). This is self-consistent.\n- Its own `spectrum()` uses a half-Hann window with truncation (L146-163).\n- It reads `.scad` constants with a regex (L49-55).\n\n**`simulator/build/nmr_exact.wls`**\n- Exact single-loop Biot–Savart using elliptic integrals (L37-43).\n- B0 pair: 5×5 filaments per coil, 312 turns, R = 200 mm, cross-section 28×27 mm (L46-47).\n- Class coil: 222 turns at r = 20.225 mm plus 178 turns at r = 20.675 mm, 0.45 mm pitch, from x = −50 mm (L50-51).\n- B1⊥ and φ in a frame built from the local B0 (L70-76).\n- SpinDynamica pulse (L80-82), then FID with e^{−iΩt} (L97). This is the handedness inconsistency described above.\n- Same tank and FWHM definitions as `physics.js` (L101-107).\n- Voxel grid starts at x0+g/2 (L59), so it is off-centre: tube y and z run −12.75 to +12.25 mm; bottle y and z run −16 to +17 mm. As a result, tube volume is 55.55 mL against 55.88 analytic, and bottle is 121.77 mL against 119.3 (+2 %).\n\n**Firmware SIM (`Sequencer.cpp:51-63`, `:611-685`)**\n- A single complex exponential at the IF (sim_larmor − f_lo) with the scan phase, a fixed 18 mV amplitude, and e^{−t/0.4 s} starting at the first sample.\n- Uniform noise of 3 mV rms from an LCG, 2 mV of 50 Hz hum as a complex tone, and DC offsets.\n- Generated at the raw 100 kS/s and boxcar-decimated by 4 (`Sequencer.h:45-46`).\n- It ignores t90, flip angle, echo (`:436-451` waits but simulates nothing), field and tank.\n- The I/Q convention matches everywhere else: Larmor = f_lo + peak (`NmrBlock.cpp:286`, `instrument.py:290`).\n\n**I/Q sign:** all four models agree that a positive IF offset means a line above f_lo. None of them has been checked against the real mixer wiring.\n\n## 2. Analytic checks\n- **`test-physics.js`:** has no assertions; it only prints. In Node the field maps are never loaded, so it runs the approximate model. Default tube: emf 0.83 µV, t90 451 µs, T2* 72.4 ms, FWHM 6 Hz, about 0.4–0.6 s per case, 3.6 s in total.\n- **Helmholtz 4th order (numeric loop integration):**\n\n| Offset | Axial error | 1.152(s/R)⁴ | Radial error | ⅜·1.152(s/R)⁴ |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 10 mm | −7.18e-6 | −7.20e-6 | −2.706e-6 | −2.700e-6 |\n| 30 mm | −5.69e-4 | −5.83e-4 | −2.23e-4 | −2.19e-4 |\n\n  The coefficients are correct. The long-solenoid formula gives 417.64 µs, so the workbook's 417 is the infinite-solenoid number and matches the exact value partly by coincidence. The approximate model gives 451.22 µs.\n- **Curie law:** M0 = 6.909e-6 A/m at 2.0997 mT and 293 K. The formula and N_H = 6.69e28 m⁻³ are correct.\n- **Bloch rotation:** Rodrigues and RK4 agree to 5 decimals at Δf = 0, +160 Hz, and −300 Hz with φ = 0.4. Example: Δf = 160 Hz, 417.38 µs, B1rot = 14.068 µT gives (0.26306, 0.96466, 0.01522).\n- **Precession sign:** at 100 Hz for 1 ms, M⊥ goes to e^{−i0.2π}, which is clockwise and correct for γ > 0.\n- **Hahn echo (90x–τ–180y–τ, Δf spread ±150 Hz):** hard pulses refocus to 1.0000. The real 14.07 µT B1 gives 0.968 at the echo (0.990 right after the 90°). This is my script, not a project feature.\n- **Relaxation:** T2 decay and the T1 recovery formula in `state()` are correct.\n\n## 3. Most connectivity below comes from the netlist exported from the current schematic. Where the generator scripts or docs disagree with it, the netlist wins and the conflict is listed under §7.\n\n## 1. ERC, DRC and netlist (KiCad 10)\nNo library variable was needed: the lib tables use `${KIPRJMOD}`. All runs succeeded.\n\n| Run | Errors | Warnings | Detail |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| ERC main | 0 | 2 | Two `pin_to_pin` warnings: J13 (Unspecified pin type) to R933 / GND. TX chain (sheet `/nmr_tx/`; generator `hardware/scripts/sheet_nmr_tx.py`)\n- **DDS:** U801 AD9834BRUZ.\n  - MCLK is the Si5351 CLK0 at 50 MHz through R703 33 Ω.\n  - R801 6.8k sets 3.18 mA full scale. R802/R803 200 Ω loads give 0.636 Vpp. C807 20 pF.\n  - FSYNC = GPIO41, PSELECT = GPIO42, SDATA/SCLK on the shared SPI bus.\n  - **RESET and SLEEP only have 10k pull-downs (R817/R818) and are not driven by anything.** Firmware must reset the part through its control register.\n- **Reconstruction filter:** 3rd-order Butterworth at 3 MHz: C808 390 pF, L801 15 µH, C809 130 pF. C810 1 µF couples into TX_IN.\n- **Bias:** R820 47k from TX_VMID. The 2026-10-01 fix is in the netlist.\n- **Power amp:** U802 OPA564AIDWPR on a single supply, +VEXT_TX through FB802; V− = GND.\n  - Gain: R807 6.49k / R808 270 Ω gives G = 25 when header J10 1-2 is fitted. With no shunt, G = 1.\n  - R808 returns to TX_VMID. C813 was removed, and R805/R806 are now 10k (they were 100k).\n  - ISET: R809 11k, so I_LIM = 1.5 A.\n  - E/S = TX_EN on GPIO40, with R810 10k pull-down.\n  - **I_FLAG and T_FLAG are unconnected**: they end at the DNP pull-ups R811/R812. `pins.h:96-97` sets both to −1.\n- **Output path:** TX_A goes to D801/D802 SS54 rail clamps, the R814 10 Ω + C817 10 nF snubber, then **R813 4.7 Ω 1 W** and **C818 10 µF**, into header J11. J11 1-2 gives full output; 2-3 selects the −20 dB pad (R815 910 Ω / R816 100 Ω). J11 pin 2 is net TX.\n- **Route to the coil:** TX goes to link J6 pins 10/12, then panel J1 pins 10/12, then the TX SMA J20 and the **TX COIL terminal J32** pin 1 (pin 2 = GND).\n- **Output limits:** design swing is 15.9 Vpp (±7.95 V) at +VEXT = 18 V. Current is limited to 1.5 A. At lower +VEXT (the input range goes down to 7 V) the stage will clip; I did not quantify where (VERIFY).\n\n## 3. RX chain (sheet `/nmr_rx/`)\n- **Input:** RX SMA (panel J13) goes to panel J1 pin 20, then main J6 pin 20, then net RX.\n- **On the RX node:** crossed diodes D703/D704 1N4148W to GND, tank C710 1.2 nF + C711 100 pF + C712 22 pF (all fitted, 1.322 nF total), R711 1M to GND, then R710 100 Ω in series to the LNA input.\n- **LNA stage 1:** U703A OPA1656 on ±12 V. R712 10k / R713 100 Ω gives G = 101; R714 1k is the alternative.\n  - **Header J3: pin 1 = R712, pin 2 = LNA_FB1, pin 3 = R714. Without a shunt, stage 1 has no feedback.** See §7.\n- **Blanking:** C720 10 nF and R721 10k (high-pass at 1.59 kHz) feed U704 DG419. S2 = signal and S1 = GND, so RX_BLANK low means blanked. RX_BLANK is GPIO8 with R724 10k pull-down.\n- **LNA stage 2:** U703B, R722 9.1k / R723 1k gives G ≈ 10.1 via header J9 (no shunt gives G = 1). C721 100 pF.\n- **Mixer:**\n  - U705A unity-gain inverter (R901/R902 10k) makes S−.\n  - C901-C904 10 nF couple the signal in. R906-R909 10k bias to V_MID = 1.65 V (R903/R904 divider, U705B buffer, R905 100 Ω). D905-D908 BAV99 clamp to 0 / +3V3A.\n  - U901/U902 TS5A23157 form a double-balanced commutator.\n  - The LO comes from Si5351 CLK1 (= 4 × f_LO) through R704/C706 into U702 74HC74, wired as a Johnson counter, giving LO_I and LO_Q. /CLR is on expander P1.4.\n- **IF filtering and gain:**\n  - Passive RC: R910-R913 1k with C910-C913 10 nF to V_MID, pole at 15.9 kHz.\n  - Difference amplifiers U706 OPA1612: R914/R916/R918/R923 1k, R915/R917/R919/R924 20k, C914/C915 560 pF (pole 14.2 kHz). Outputs are COND_OUT1/COND_OUT2.\n- **ADC channels:** COND_OUT1 goes through header J14 2-3, then R117 1k / C117 1 nF / D117 BAV99 to ±12 V, into ADS8688 **AIN_3P (pin 23, chip channel 3)**. COND_OUT2 goes through J15 and R118 into **AIN_2P (chip channel 2)**. This matches `pins.h:32` (kAinOfAi). The docs' \"ch 7/8\" refers to panel numbering AI7/AI8.\n\n## 4. Shared coil, T/R switching, B0 drive, polarizer, power, grounding, clocks, ADC\n\n**Is one coil shared for TX and RX?**\n- TX (J20 / J32) and RX (J13) are separate connectors and separate nets.\n- **There is no T/R switch or duplexer.** DG419 blanking sits after LNA stage 1 and does not isolate the coil.\n- The docs never say \"one coil\" or \"two coils\". However, the TX level plan assumes \"5.6 mA into the 1419 Ω coil\" (`sheet_nmr_tx.py:145`, `design-decisions.md:405`), which is the same 2.53 mH coil the RX tank is designed for (`sheet_nmr_rx.py:253`).\n- `docs/students/yi-tsai/PROGRESS.md:29` lists \"TX/RX on one coil (RX clamp diodes across the transmitter, scan aborts)\" as an open blocker.\n\n**What happens if one coil is wired to both TX and RX (my calculation):**\n- D703/D704 then clamp the TX node to about ±1-1.3 V. The OPA564 (±7.95 V pk) drives them through R813 4.7 Ω + C818 (0.18 Ω).\n- Diode current ≈ (7.95 − ~1.2) / 4.9 ≈ **1.4 A peak**, which is essentially the 1.5 A I_LIM.\n- Consequences:\n  - The OPA564 runs in current limit, and the flag that would report it is not wired.\n  - R813 dissipates about 4-5 W during the pulse (it is a 1 W part).\n  - The 1N4148W diodes carry several times their typical repetitive rating. Check the vendor datasheet; I did not.\n  - Coil current falls to about 1.2 V / 1419 Ω ≈ 0.85 mA instead of 5.6 mA. That makes B1 about 6-7× weaker, so a 90° pulse would take about 3 ms instead of 417 µs.\n\n**B0 drive (J903):**\n- U903 DRV8871DDAR with VM = +VEXT (7-18 V input), IN1 = GPIO9, IN2 = GPIO14, R921/R922 10k pull-downs. C921 100 µF + C920 100 nF, D920 SMBJ26A on VM.\n- R920 32k on ILIM gives I_TRIP = 64 / 32 = **2.0 A** (`requirements.md:75`).\n- **No current sensing of the B0 coil exists anywhere.**\n- From memory of the datasheet (fairly confident, not re-checked): the DRV8871 regulates by fixed off-time chopping (about 25 µs) once current reaches I_TRIP. That is a current limit, not a settable closed-loop current source.\n- The operating point is about 1.50 A. That figure comes from the student simulator's 59.7 kHz/A coil calibration, not a measurement. It is below I_TRIP, so B0 is effectively voltage/PWM-driven and drifts with coil resistance. PROGRESS.md reports 138 Hz/min.\n\n**Polarizer (J905):**\n- Pin 1 is +VCOIL, an external supply that only reaches D930. Pin 2 is COIL, going to Q904 AOD4184A drain. Pin 3 is GND.\n- Q904 source goes to ISENSE_COIL, then R933 0 Ω to GND. The J13 sense header is DNP on the PCB, and **ISENSE_COIL does not reach the ADC**.\n- Gate drive: U904 UCC27517 from GPIO47 via R931 100 Ω and R932 10 Ω; R930 / R950 10k pull-downs.\n- **The driver's VDD comes only from a shunt on header J12** (+VEXT or +5V_RAW). Without the shunt the driver is unpowered.\n- D930 SS54 freewheel is fitted; D934 SMBJ20A is DNP.\n- There are **no relays** on either board; the b4_switching sheet was removed.\n\n**Power rails:**\n- J901 → F901 5 A fuse → D931 SMBJ26A → Q901 AOD4185 reverse-polarity P-FET (D932 12 V gate clamp) → +VEXT.\n- USB-C J201 and DC jack J202 → 1.5 A polyfuses → SMF5.0A TVS → LM66100 ideal-diode OR → +5V_RAW. From there:\n  - AMS1117 → +3V3.\n  - Two B0512S-2WR3 2 W isolated modules → ±12 V.\n  - 78L05 on +12 V → +5VA (ADS8688 AVDD, DG419 VL, DAC).\n  - FB901 → +3V3A (mixer and divider).\n\n**Grounding:**\n- **There is one GND net on both boards and no AGND net or NT1 net-tie.** This was a deliberate change in commit 147f9cc (\"one GND net on both boards\", Decision #73).\n- The AD9834 AGND/DGND, the receiver, the OPA564, the H-bridge and the 13 A polarizer return all share it.\n\n**Clocks:**\n- Y701 25 MHz crystal → U701 Si5351A at I²C 0x60, powered from +3V3 rather than +3V3A.\n- CLK0 = 50 MHz to the DDS. CLK1 = 336 kHz (4 × 84 kHz LO). CLK2 goes to TP701.\n- The DDS and the LO share one reference.\n\n**ADC (ADS8688):**\n- SPI2 on the IO_MUX pins: SCLK GPIO12 (via R1 33 Ω), MOSI GPIO11 (via R2 33 Ω), MISO GPIO13 (via R101 33 Ω), /CS GPIO10.\n- The bus is shared with the AD9834 (SPI mode 2) and the DAC8563 (via 74HCT125). The ADC uses mode 1.\n- /RST is pulled up with 10k and not driven. REFSEL = GND selects the internal reference; REFIO has 10 µF.\n- The input range is set in software: firmware defaults ±5.12 V, code 1 (`NMR-FIRMWARE.md:147`).\n- Sustained rate:\n  - The ADS8688 tops out at 500 kSPS aggregate (from memory of the datasheet). Two channels at 250 kS/s would be the absolute limit.\n  - Firmware targets 100 kS/s per channel with per-sample polling at 17 MHz (`NMR-FIRMWARE.md:139-147`). Per-transaction overhead on the ESP32 will likely set the real ceiling; this is speculation and needs measuring.\n  - The ADC's ~15 kHz input filter acts as the IF anti-alias filter.\n\n## 5. Critical parameters\n\n| Name | Value | Unit | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| B0 / Larmor / IF / LO | 2.1 mT / 89.4 / 5.4 / 84.0 | – / kHz / kHz / kHz | design-decisions.md:343-346; bring-up.md:52 |\n| Si5351 crystal / CLK0 / CLK1 | 25 / 50.000 / 0.336 | MHz | netlist Y701; Classification\n\n| Feature | Class | Why |\n|---|---|---|\n| Si5351 + Johnson-counter quadrature LO, one reference shared with the DDS | RETAIN | Sound, coherent architecture. |\n| AD9834 + 3 MHz reconstruction filter | RETAIN | Values are consistent. |\n| Wire AD9834 RESET/SLEEP to GPIOs | REIMPLEMENT | Currently not driven at all. |\n| OPA564 power stage | RETAIN topology, REIMPLEMENT flags | IFLAG/TFLAG are unwired; no sequencing interlock. |\n| Heterodyne I/Q receiver, blanking between LNA stages | RETAIN | |\n| IF difference-amplifier resistor network | VERIFY / REIMPLEMENT | Source resistance gives G ≈ 10, not 20, and the P and N legs have unequal poles. |\n| Crossed diodes directly on the RX node, no T/R switch | REIMPLEMENT | Single-coil use loads TX at about 1.4 A. Needs a proper T/R network (series crossed diodes plus a quarter-wave/lumped duplexer, or an analog switch). |\n| Jumper-header gain selection (J3, J9, J10) | REIMPLEMENT | J3 without a shunt leaves the LNA open-loop. |\n| Fixed 1.322 nF on-board tank | VERIFY | Off-tune as fitted (~87 kHz); tune at the coil. |\n| DRV8871 voltage-mode B0 drive | REIMPLEMENT | Needs closed-loop constant current with a shunt plus ADC/feedback. |\n| Polarizer AOD4184A + UCC27517 + SS54 | RETAIN, VERIFY | Make J12 a fixed connection; route the current sense to the ADC. |\n| Single GND net, no AGND | VERIFY / REIMPLEMENT | Polarizer, H-bridge and TX returns share copper with a ×1000 receiver. |\n| +VEXT input protection | REIMPLEMENT | The TVS clamp is above the OPA564's 26 V absolute max. |\n| ADS8688 as the NMR digitizer | VERIFY | Sustained 2-channel rate on shared SPI is not demonstrated. |\n| Relays / mains | REMOVE | Already absent from the design. |\n| Stale documentation | REMOVE / UPDATE | See §7. |\n\n## 7. Known problems and risks (with evidence)\n1. **The LNA has no feedback unless J3 is shunted.** The netlist shows J3 pin 1 (R712) and pin 2 (LNA_FB1) on different nets. The schematic note \"R712 permanent — J3 pin 1 tied to pin 2\" (`sheets/nmr_rx.kicad_sch:7336`) is not reflected in the connectivity.\n2. **A shared TX/RX coil drives the RX clamp diodes at about 1.4 A**, and there is no T/R switch (§4).\n3. **The OPA564 I_FLAG/T_FLAG are unconnected**, so the firmware abort on current limit (`NMR-FIRMWARE.md:188`, `bring-up.md:66`) cannot work.\n4. **No B0 current sensing**, and the DRV8871 only limits at 2.0 A. B0 drifts with coil temperature.\n5. **Power sequencing:** VDIG must come up before V+ (`design-decisions.md` D-35). Only a silkscreen note and procedure enforce this. Also, the SMBJ26A (~42 V clamp) does not protect the OPA564 (26 V absolute max).\n6. **The docs are stale versus the netlist:**\n   - AGENTS.md:158, README.md:133 and design-decisions D-10/D-21 claim an AGND/NT1 split; there is one GND net.\n   - D-35 claims R805/R806 100k and C813 present.\n   - bring-up T-17 refers to J802/JP801, and T-18 to JP702.\n   - `bring-up.md:61` channel table (AI7→4, AI8→5) contradicts the netlist and `pins.h` (AI7→3, AI8→2).\n   - The TX link pins are 10/12 in the netlist, not 19/21 or 39.\n7. **IF gain is roughly half the documented value** (≈10 vs 20, my calculation). The expected ~1.0 V pk at the ADC would be about 0.5 V.\n8. **Tank fully stuffed at 1.322 nF** puts resonance about 2.4 kHz low (my calculation).\n9. **Speculative:** the free-running DDS phase relative to the LO at pulse time may vary from shot to shot. Coherent averaging may need phase referencing. VERIFY in firmware.\n10. **Minor:** U204 AMS1117 tab pad 4 has no net, and the J12 shunt is needed to power U904.\n"

L4476: # Milestone 0 — audit of the legacy sources
L4477: 
L4478: Sources audited (read-only):
L4479: 
L4480: | Archive | Repository | Commit |
L4481: |---|---|---|
L4482: | `class-board-2026 (2).zip` | TIGP-Experimental-Methods/class-board-2026, branch `w1-yi-tsai` | `da56d2b` (hardware/firmware identical to `main` @ `eef00d2`) |
L4483: | `nmr-instrument.zip` | tojestspacja/nmr-instrument, `main` | `0914edb` (now under `legacy/` on this branch) |
L4484: 
L4485: The audit ran KiCad 10 ERC/DRC, exported the netlist, built both firmware environments with PlatformIO, re-ran the Biot–Savart
L4486: field-map computation in Mathematica 13.3 and checked the Bloch code against an RK4 integration. Nothing in the legacy
L4487: sources has ever run on real hardware. Raw audit outputs: `validation/legacy-audit/`.
L4488: 
L4489: ## 1. Feature inventory
L4490: 
L4491: ### Electronics (class-board-2026, KiCad authority)
L4492: 
L4493: | Feature | Class | Evidence / reason |
L4494: |---|---|---|
L4495: | Si5351A (25 MHz crystal): CLK0 50 MHz DDS clock, CLK1 = 4·f_LO into a 74HC74 Johnson counter (I/Q LO) | RETAIN | Sound; one reference for TX and LO |
L4496: | AD9834 DDS + 3 MHz 3rd-order reconstruction filter | RETAIN | Values consistent (R801 6.8 k, 0.636 Vpp) |
L4497: | AD9834 RESET/SLEEP pins only pulled down, never driven | REIMPLEMENT | Reset only via control word; no hardware phase reset |
L4498: | OPA564 power stage, G = 25 (R807/R808), I_LIM 1.5 A (R809 11 k) | RETAIN topology | — |
L4499: | OPA564 I_FLAG/T_FLAG unconnected (DNP pull-ups, `pins.h:96-97` = −1) | REIMPLEMENT | Over-current abort documented but impossible |
L4500: | TX output snubber R814 10 Ω + C817 10 nF to GND | VERIFY | Loads a shared coil's tank heavily during receive (179 Ω at 89 kHz) |
L4501: | Crossed diodes D703/D704 directly on the RX node; **no T/R switch** | REIMPLEMENT | With one coil on TX and RX the diodes carry ≈1.4 A from the OPA564 (at I_LIM) and B1 drops ~6× — `docs/tx-rx.md` |
L4502: | Tank C710+C711+C712 all fitted = 1.322 nF on the board's RX node | VERIFY | Resonance ≈87 kHz, not 89.4 kHz; a tank on the board side of the cable is cable-dependent |
L4503: | OPA1656 LNA stage 1, gain set via header J3 | REIMPLEMENT | **Without a J3 shunt stage 1 has no feedback** (netlist: J3-1 and J3-2 on different nets) |
L4504: | DG419 blanking between LNA stages (RX_BLANK, GPIO8) | RETAIN | Correct place for post-LNA blanking; does not isolate the coil |
L4505: | Double-balanced TS5A23157 commutating mixer + OPA1612 difference amplifiers | RETAIN / VERIFY | IF gain computes to ≈10, documented 20; unequal P/N poles |
L4506: | ADS8688 on shared SPI2 (with AD9834 and DAC8563), ±5.12 V range, channels AIN3 (I) / AIN2 (Q) | VERIFY | Sustained two-channel rate on a shared bus not demonstrated |
L4507: | DRV8871 H-bridge as B0 driver, I_TRIP 2.0 A (R920 32 k), **no current sense** | REIMPLEMENT | Chopping limiter, not a regulated current source; B0 drifts with coil temperature; chopping ripple modulates B0 |
L4508: | Polarizer FET AOD4184A + UCC27517 | RETAIN / VERIFY | Driver VDD only via J12 shunt; ISENSE_COIL not routed to the ADC |
L4509: | One GND net on both boards (Decision #73) | VERIFY | 1000× receiver shares copper with the polarizer (13 A), H-bridge and TX returns |
L4510: | +VEXT TVS SMBJ26A (clamps ~42 V) | REIMPLEMENT | Above the OPA564's 26 V absolute maximum |
L4511: | ERC/DRC | RETAIN | ERC 0 errors (2 cosmetic warnings), DRC 0 violations, 0 unconnected |
L4512: 
L4513: ### Firmware (class-board-2026 `firmware/`, Arduino-ESP32 2.0.17)
L4514: 
L4515: | Feature | Class | Evidence / reason |
L4516: |---|---|---|
L4517: | TX gate = `digitalWrite` + `esp_timer` busy-wait (`Sequencer.cpp:427-430`) | REIMPLEMENT | Pre-emptible (AsyncTCP prio 10, arduino_events on core 1): an over-pulse lasts as long as the pre-emption; no flag to catch it |
L4518: | ADC burst: Arduino `SPI.transfer32`, `esp_timer` polling per sample (`Ads8688.cpp:163-225`) | REIMPLEMENT | Jitter; late samples taken back-to-back silently; header reports requested, not achieved, rate |
L4519: | NMR-FIRMWARE.md ↔ code (ADS8688 "spi_device_polling_transmit", "critical region", "priority high", AD9834 PHASE1 90/180) | REMOVE | 10 documented discrepancies (firmware audit §2) |
L4520: | **Phase coherence: TX (PLLA/DDS) and LO (PLLB) free-run; scan start is ESP32-timed** | REIMPLEMENT (missing) | IF phase at the pulse is random per scan → averaging and CYCLOPS incoherent on hardware; SIM hides it (`Sequencer.cpp:627-629`) |
L4521: | Grow-only PSRAM buffers at `config` | RETAIN | No per-scan allocation |
L4522: | `Dsp.cpp` pure functions (offset, boxcar decimation, CYCLOPS rotation, radix-2 FFT, parabolic peak) | RETAIN concept, REIMPLEMENT | Correct but untested; no digital mixing; three incompatible spectrum implementations (firmware, JS, Python; 2√2 scale differences) |
L4523: | Si5351/AD9834 drivers | RETAIN / VERIFY | AN619-correct; `setClk1` return unchecked; SPI mutex inside the timed path |
L4524: | Per-scan 400 kB WebSocket broadcast under a lock | REIMPLEMENT | Heavy; jitters TR |
L4525: | `instrument.py next_binary` returns the oldest parked record | REMOVE | Returns scan 1, not the average |
L4526: | Full-record Hann window (`instrument.py:287-302`) | REMOVE | ≈10 dB SNR loss for a T2* ≪ record |
L4527: | Four simulators (firmware SIM, ADS8688 SIM, dev-nmr.html, docs) | REMOVE | Replace with the shared physics core |
L4528: | CI: build only, ruff; **no unit tests** | REIMPLEMENT | — |
L4529: 
L4530: ### Physics and simulation (nmr-instrument `legacy/`)
L4531: 
L4532: | Feature | Class | Evidence / reason |
L4533: |---|---|---|
L4534: | Rodrigues rotation about −B_eff in the f_tx frame; precession e^{−iΔωt} (`physics.js`) | RETAIN | Matches RK4 of dM/dt = γ M×B to 5 decimals |
L4535: | Curie M0, reciprocity pickup, rotating B1 = ½ linear field | RETAIN | Analytically correct |
L4536: | Exact Biot–Savart field maps (`fieldmap-*.json`), elliptic-integral loop kernel | RETAIN as golden data; port the kernel | Recomputed voxels match every printed digit |
L4537: | Approximate 4th-order Helmholtz + on-axis solenoid | REIMPLEMENT | B0 ignores N rounding (311.4→312); B1 has no radial/two-layer model → t90 451 µs vs exact 417.4 µs |
L4538: | `nmr_exact.wls` SpinDynamica comparison | REIMPLEMENT | Handedness mismatch (pulse about +H, FID e^{−iΩt}); explains the 0.6 ms "agreement" gap |
L4539: | Reported "FWHM 6 Hz" | REMOVE | Window-limited by the 0.2 s record (1.207/T) |
L4540: | Scalar tank gain, no transient, unshaped noise | REIMPLEMENT | Needs complex response |
L4541: | `spectrum.py` sample model (r 15 mm, x −50…25 mm) | REIMPLEMENT | Disagrees with the other models (r 14 mm, −58…+25 mm + cone) |
L4542: | Coil-heating drift model | RETAIN | Self-consistent |
L4543: | `test-physics.js` | REIMPLEMENT | Prints, asserts nothing |
L4544: | Line at 1.5 A: 89 400 / 89 560 / 89 585 Hz in three models | REIMPLEMENT | One field model, one source of geometry |

L4553: 
L4554: ## 2. Known problems that block real NMR (ordered)
L4555: 
L4556: 1. **Phase coherence between TX, LO and acquisition** — averaging cannot work (ADR-0006).
L4557: 2. **No T/R switch on a shared coil** — the transmitter is shorted into the RX clamp diodes (ADR-0007).
L4558: 3. **B0 is not current-regulated and not measured** — 23 W heating moves the line ≈138 Hz/min; DRV8871 chopping modulates B0 (ADR-0008).
L4559: 4. **LNA stage 1 open-loop without the J3 shunt** — bring-up step, then a schematic fix.
L4560: 5. **Software-timed TX gate** — over-pulse risk with no hardware flag (ADR-0005).
L4561: 6. Tank as fitted ≈87 kHz; IF gain ≈half the documented value; OPA564 flags unwired; TVS above the OPA564's absolute maximum.
L4562: 
L4563: ## 3. Dependency map (legacy)
L4564: 
L4565: ```text
L4566: nmr-params.scad ──regex──► spectrum.py           (constants copied by hand into:)
L4567:                           physics.js P{}         Sequencer.h defaults, nmr.js defaults,
L4568:                           nmr_exact.wls          instrument.py NMR_DEFAULTS, dev-nmr.html,
L4569:                                                  NMR-FIRMWARE.md, PROTOCOL.md
L4570: KiCad (class-board) ──export──► release/*.stl/.step ──► OpenSCAD housing, simulator meshes
L4571: firmware Sequencer ──WebSocket──► PWA nmr.js (own FFT) / instrument.py (own FFT)
L4572: ```
L4573: 
L4574: Every arrow labelled "by hand" is replaced in v2 by generation from `design/instrument.yaml`.

L4600: # The canonical definition of the instrument. The ONLY hand-edited copy of these numbers.
L4601: #
L4602: # Every quantity is {value, unit, kind, source}:
L4603: #   unit   - SI (see design/schema/instrument.schema.json for the allowed set)
L4604: #   kind   - target     : what we aim for (a set-point)
L4605: #            design     : chosen in the design (a dimension, a component value)
L4606: #            datasheet  : a manufacturer's figure
L4607: #            calculated : derived by a documented calculation (source says which)
L4608: #            estimate   : a judgement, to be replaced by a measurement
L4609: #            measured   : measured on this instrument (source names the record in validation/)
L4610: #            constant   : a physical constant
L4611: #   source - where the value comes from (file:line, document, calculation)
L4612: # Quantities that follow from others (IF, tank capacitance, coil resistance, ...) are NOT stored here:
L4613: # tools/gen_config.py computes them into generated/ so they cannot disagree.
L4614: # Edit this file, then run `py tools/gen_config.py`; CI fails if generated/ is stale.

L4798: """Generate language-specific instrument configs from design/instrument.yaml (the only hand-edited copy).
L4799: 
L4800:     py tools/gen_config.py            # write generated/
L4801:     py tools/gen_config.py --check    # exit 1 if generated/ is stale (CI)
L4802: 
L4803: What it does:
L4804:   1. validates design/instrument.yaml against design/schema/instrument.schema.json;
L4805:   2. flattens it to unit-carrying identifiers (tx.frequency [Hz] -> TX_FREQUENCY_HZ);
L4806:   3. computes the derived quantities (each with its formula) so nothing derived is ever typed twice;
L4807:   4. checks physical consistency (Nyquist, ADC rate, t90 vs. B1, ...) and fails on violations;
L4808:   5. writes generated/instrument_config.{json,hpp,py} with a SHA-256 of the canonical content.
L4809: """

L4830:     "J*s": "J_S", "J/K": "J_PER_K", "H/m": "H_PER_M", "ohm*m": "OHM_M", "1/K": "PER_K", "J/(kg*K)": "J_PER_KG_K",
L4831:     "kg/m^3": "KG_PER_M3", "Hz/T": "HZ_PER_T", "1/m^3": "PER_M3", "T/A": "T_PER_A", "V/sqrt(Hz)": "V_PER_RTHZ",

L4836: def ident(path: str, unit: str) -> str:
L4837:     base = path.replace(".", "_").upper()
L4838:     suf = UNIT_SUFFIX[unit]
L4839:     return f"{base}_{suf}" if suf else base
L4840: 
L4841: 
L4842: def flatten(doc: dict) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
L4843:     """-> (quantities {path: {value, unit, kind, source}}, strings {path: str})"""
L4844:     q, s = {}, {}
L4845:     for group, body in doc.items():
L4846:         if group in ("schema_version",):
L4847:             continue
L4848:         for key, v in body.items():
L4849:             path = f"{group}.{key}"
L4850:             if isinstance(v, dict):
L4851:                 q[path] = v
L4852:             else:
L4853:                 s[path] = v
L4854:     return q, s
L4855: 
L4856: 
L4857: def derive(q: dict) -> dict:
L4858:     """Derived quantities. Each: (value, unit, formula). Inputs by path."""
L4859:     v = {p: x["value"] for p, x in q.items()}
L4860:     d = {}
L4861: 
L4862:     def put(path, value, unit, formula):
L4863:         d[path] = {"value": value, "unit": unit, "kind": "calculated", "source": formula}
L4864: 
L4865:     gbar = v["nucleus.gamma_bar"]
L4866:     pi = math.pi
L4867:     mu0 = v["constants.mu0"]
L4868:     put("rf.if_frequency", v["tx.frequency"] - v["lo.frequency"], "Hz", "tx.frequency - lo.frequency")
L4869:     put("lo.clk1_frequency", v["lo.frequency"] * v["lo.multiplier"], "Hz", "lo.frequency * lo.multiplier")
L4870:     put("acquisition.decimated_rate", v["acquisition.raw_rate"] / v["acquisition.decimation"], "Hz",
L4871:         "acquisition.raw_rate / acquisition.decimation")
L4872:     put("acquisition.samples", round(v["acquisition.duration"] * v["acquisition.raw_rate"] / v["acquisition.decimation"]),
L4873:         "1", "round(duration * raw_rate / decimation) complex samples")
L4874:     # B0 pair (thin-wire Helmholtz; the finite-section value comes from the physics core / field map)
L4875:     b0_thin = (4 / 5) ** 1.5 * mu0 * v["b0.turns_per_coil"] * v["b0.current"] / v["b0.radius"]
L4876:     put("b0.field_thin_wire", b0_thin, "T", "(4/5)^1.5 mu0 N I / R")
L4877:     put("b0.larmor_thin_wire", gbar * b0_thin, "Hz", "gamma_bar * b0.field_thin_wire")
L4878:     put("b0.field_per_amp_thin_wire", b0_thin / v["b0.current"], "T/A", "(4/5)^1.5 mu0 N / R")
L4879:     wire_len = 2 * v["b0.turns_per_coil"] * 2 * pi * v["b0.radius"]
L4880:     area = pi * (v["b0.wire_copper_diameter"] / 2) ** 2
L4881:     r_b0 = v["constants.copper_resistivity_20c"] * wire_len / area
L4882:     mass = wire_len * area * v["constants.copper_density"]
L4883:     put("b0.resistance_20c", r_b0, "ohm", "rho_Cu * 2 N 2 pi R / (pi d^2/4)")
L4884:     put("b0.copper_mass", mass, "kg", "2 N 2 pi R * pi d^2/4 * rho_mass")
L4885:     put("b0.voltage_20c", r_b0 * v["b0.current"], "V", "I * R_20C")
L4886:     put("b0.power_20c", r_b0 * v["b0.current"] ** 2, "W", "I^2 R_20C")
L4887:     heat = r_b0 * v["b0.current"] ** 2 / (mass * v["constants.copper_heat_capacity"])
L4888:     put("b0.heating_rate_adiabatic", heat, "K/s", "P / (m c), no cooling: an upper bound")
L4889:     put("b0.voltage_drive_drift_rate", -gbar * b0_thin * v["constants.copper_tempco"] * heat, "Hz/s",
L4890:         "voltage drive: df/dt = -f alpha_Cu dT/dt")
L4891:     put("b0.current_drive_drift_rate", -gbar * b0_thin * v["constants.copper_expansion"] * heat, "Hz/s",
L4892:         "constant current: B ~ 1/R_geometric -> df/dt = -f alpha_expansion dT/dt")
L4893:     # RF coil and tank
L4894:     w = 2 * pi * v["tx.frequency"]
L4895:     put("rf_coil.reactance", w * v["rf_coil.inductance"], "ohm", "2 pi f_tx L")
L4896:     put("rf_coil.tank_capacitance", 1 / (w ** 2 * v["rf_coil.inductance"]), "F", "1 / ((2 pi f_tx)^2 L)")
L4897:     put("rf_coil.tank_parallel_resistance", v["rf_coil.quality_factor"] * w * v["rf_coil.inductance"], "ohm", "Q omega L")
L4898:     put("rf_coil.ringdown_time_constant", v["rf_coil.quality_factor"] / (pi * v["tx.frequency"]), "s",
L4899:         "tau = 2Q/omega = Q/(pi f) (energy decays at 2/tau)")
L4900:     i_tx = (v["tx.amplitude"] - v["tx.series_diode_drop"]) / math.hypot(v["rf_coil.resistance"], w * v["rf_coil.inductance"])
L4901:     put("tx.coil_current", i_tx, "A", "(V_tx - V_diode) / |R + j omega L| (untuned coil during TX, ADR-0007)")
L4902:     b1rot = 0.5 * v["rf_coil.b1_per_amp_centre"] * i_tx
L4903:     put("tx.b1_rotating_centre", b1rot, "T", "1/2 * b1_per_amp_centre * coil_current")
L4904:     put("tx.t90_calculated", 1 / (4 * gbar * b1rot), "s", "pi/2 = 2 pi gamma_bar B1rot t  ->  t = 1/(4 gamma_bar B1rot)")
L4905:     put("tx.excitation_bandwidth", 1 / (4 * v["tx.t90"]), "Hz", "~1/(4 t90): +-half-width of a hard 90 pulse's flat region")
L4906:     # Curie magnetization at the thin-wire field
L4907:     m0 = (v["sample.proton_density"] * (2 * pi * gbar) ** 2 * v["constants.hbar"] ** 2 * b0_thin
L4908:           / (4 * v["constants.k_boltzmann"] * v["sample.temperature"]))
L4909:     put("sample.curie_magnetization", m0, "A/m", "N gamma^2 hbar^2 B0 / (4 k T)")
L4910:     put("timing.tick_period", 1 / v["timing.tick"], "s", "1 / timing.tick")
L4911:     put("timing.capture_resolution", 1 / v["timing.capture_clock"], "s", "1 / timing.capture_clock")
L4912:     return d
L4913: 
L4914: 
L4915: def check(q: dict, d: dict) -> list[str]:
L4916:     """Physical/engineering consistency. Returns a list of violations (errors)."""
L4917:     v = {p: x["value"] for p, x in {**q, **d}.items()}
L4918:     errs = []
L4919:     if v["acquisition.decimated_rate"] <= 2 * abs(v["rf.if_frequency"]):
L4920:         errs.append("decimated rate must exceed 2|IF| (complex IF would still fold for real sampling)")
L4921:     if 2 * v["acquisition.raw_rate"] > v["receiver.adc_max_aggregate_rate"]:
L4922:         errs.append("two channels at raw_rate exceed the ADC's aggregate maximum")
L4923:     if abs(v["tx.t90_calculated"] / v["tx.t90"] - 1) > 0.02:
L4924:         errs.append(f"t90 target {v['tx.t90']:.6g} s disagrees with the B1 calculation {v['tx.t90_calculated']:.6g} s by >2 %")
L4925:     if abs(v["tx.t180"] / v["tx.t90"] - 2) > 1e-9:
L4926:         errs.append("t180 must be 2 t90")
L4927:     if v["acquisition.start_delay"] < v["receiver.dead_time"]:
L4928:         errs.append("acquisition must not start before the receiver dead time has elapsed")
L4929:     if v["receiver.dead_time"] < 10 * v["rf_coil.ringdown_time_constant"]:
L4930:         errs.append("dead time shorter than 10 ring-down time constants")
L4931:     if v["b0.current"] > v["limits.b0_current_max"]:
L4932:         errs.append("B0 current above its limit")
L4933:     if v["b0.voltage_20c"] * (1 + v["constants.copper_tempco"] * 20) > v["b0.supply_compliance"] - 1.0:
L4934:         errs.append("B0 compliance: hot coil (+20 K) plus 1 V regulator headroom exceeds the supply")
L4935:     if v["tx.t90"] > v["limits.tx_max_pulse"] or v["tx.t180"] > v["limits.tx_max_pulse"]:
L4936:         errs.append("pulse longer than the TX limit")
L4937:     if v["limits.vext_max"] > v["limits.opa564_supply_abs_max"]:
L4938:         errs.append("VEXT max above OPA564 absolute maximum")
L4939:     if v["tx.frequency"] > v["dds.max_output_frequency"]:
L4940:         errs.append("TX frequency above the DDS limit")
L4941:     if int(v["acquisition.averages"]) % 4:
L4942:         errs.append("averages must be a multiple of 4 (CYCLOPS)")
L4943:     return errs
L4944: 
L4945: 
L4946: def canonical(obj) -> str:
L4947:     return json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True)
L4948: 
L4949: 
L4950: def build() -> dict[str, str]:
L4951:     doc = yaml.safe_load(SRC.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
L4952:     jsonschema.validate(doc, json.loads(SCHEMA.read_text(encoding="utf-8")))
L4953:     q, s = flatten(doc)
L4954:     d = derive(q)
L4955:     errs = check(q, d)
L4956:     if errs:
L4957:         raise SystemExit("instrument.yaml is inconsistent:\n  " + "\n  ".join(errs))
L4958:     params = {ident(p, x["unit"]): {"path": p, **x} for p, x in {**q, **d}.items()}
L4959:     if len(params) != len(q) + len(d):
L4960:         raise SystemExit("identifier collision between parameters")
L4961:     strings = {p.replace(".", "_").upper(): x for p, x in s.items()}
L4962:     strings.update({"INSTRUMENT_" + k.upper(): x for k, x in doc["instrument"].items()})
L4963:     body = {"params": params, "strings": strings}
L4964:     digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical(body).encode()).hexdigest()
L4965:     meta = {"generator": "tools/gen_config.py", "source": "design/instrument.yaml", "config_sha256": digest}
L4966: 
L4967:     out = {}
L4968:     out["instrument_config.json"] = json.dumps({"meta": meta, **body}, indent=1, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
L4969: 
L4970:     def cnum(x):
L4971:         if isinstance(x, int) or float(x).is_integer() and abs(x) < 2 ** 53 and not isinstance(x, float):
L4972:             return f"{x}"
L4973:         return repr(float(x))
L4974: 
L4975:     h = ["// GENERATED by tools/gen_config.py from design/instrument.yaml - do not edit.",
L4976:          f"// config sha256 {digest}", "#pragma once", "", "namespace nmr::cfg {", "",
L4977:          f'inline constexpr const char* CONFIG_SHA256 = "{digest}";']
L4978:     for k, x in sorted(strings.items()):
L4979:         h.append(f'inline constexpr const char* {k} = "{x}";')
L4980:     h.append("")
L4981:     for k, x in sorted(params.items()):
L4982:         h.append(f"/// {x['path']} [{x['unit']}] ({x['kind']}) - {x['source'].replace('*/', '* /')}")
L4983:         h.append(f"inline constexpr double {k} = {cnum(x['value'])};")
L4984:     h += ["", "}  // namespace nmr::cfg", ""]
L4985:     out["instrument_config.hpp"] = "\n".join(h)
L4986: 
L4987:     p = ['"""GENERATED by tools/gen_config.py from design/instrument.yaml - do not edit."""', "",
L4988:          f'CONFIG_SHA256 = "{digest}"', ""]
L4989:     for k, x in sorted(strings.items()):
L4990:         p.append(f"{k} = {x!r}")
L4991:     p.append("")
L4992:     for k, x in sorted(params.items()):
L4993:         p.append(f"{k} = {cnum(x['value'])}  # {x['path']} [{x['unit']}] ({x['kind']})")
L4994:     p += ["", "META = " + repr({k: {kk: vv for kk, vv in x.items() if kk != 'value'} for k, x in sorted(params.items())}), ""]
L4995:     out["instrument_config.py"] = "\n".join(p)
L4996:     return out

L4999: def main() -> int:
L5000:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
L5001:     ap.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="fail if generated/ differs from a fresh generation")
L5002:     a = ap.parse_args()

L5004:     if a.check:
L5005:         stale = [n for n, c in files.items() if not (OUT / n).exists() or (OUT / n).read_text(encoding="utf-8") != c]
L5006:         if stale:
L5007:             print("generated/ is stale: " + ", ".join(stale) + "  (run py tools/gen_config.py)")
L5008:             return 1

L5013:         (OUT / n).write_text(c, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")

L5249:         base = ROOT / top
L5250:         if not base.exists():
L5251:             continue

L5254:                 continue
L5255:             if f.suffix not in {".cpp", ".hpp", ".h", ".c", ".py", ".js", ".ts", ".html", ".scad", ".yaml", ".json", ".ini"}:
L5256:                 continue
L5257:             if f.name == "gen_config.py":
L5258:                 continue
L5259:             text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
L5260:             for pat in CRITICAL:
L5261:                 for m in re.finditer(pat, text):
L5262:                     line = text.count("\n", 0, m.start()) + 1
L5263:                     hits.append(f"{f.relative_to(ROOT)}:{line}: {m.group(0)}")
L5264:     assert not hits, "critical constants typed by hand (use generated/):\n" + "\n".join(hits)

L5313: // Strongly typed SI quantities for public APIs. Internally everything is double in SI units; at the API
L5314: // boundary a frequency cannot be passed where a time is expected. Construct explicitly: Hz{89.4e3}, Seconds{1e-3}.
L5315: #pragma once
L5316: 
L5317: namespace nmr {
L5318: 
L5319: template <class Tag>
L5320: struct Quantity {
L5321:     double v = 0.0;
L5322:     constexpr Quantity() = default;
L5323:     constexpr explicit Quantity(double x) : v(x) {}
L5324:     constexpr double value() const { return v; }
L5325:     constexpr Quantity operator+(Quantity o) const { return Quantity{v + o.v}; }
L5326:     constexpr Quantity operator-(Quantity o) const { return Quantity{v - o.v}; }
L5327:     constexpr Quantity operator*(double k) const { return Quantity{v * k}; }
L5328:     constexpr Quantity operator/(double k) const { return Quantity{v / k}; }
L5329:     constexpr double operator/(Quantity o) const { return v / o.v; }
L5330:     constexpr bool operator<(Quantity o) const { return v < o.v; }
L5331:     constexpr bool operator>(Quantity o) const { return v > o.v; }
L5332: };
L5333: 
L5334: struct HzTag {};
L5335: struct SecondsTag {};
L5336: struct TeslaTag {};
L5337: struct RadTag {};
L5338: struct AmpTag {};
L5339: struct VoltTag {};

L5352: // the only physically meaningful products are spelled out
L5353: constexpr Radians phase(Hz f, Seconds t) { return Radians{2.0 * kPi * f.v * t.v}; }
L5354: constexpr Hz larmor(double gamma_bar_hz_per_t, Tesla b) { return Hz{gamma_bar_hz_per_t * b.v}; }

L5357: Write C:\Users\Liang\tigp-2026\nmr-instrument\physics\include\nmr\bloch.hpp
L5358: 62 lines
L5359: // Bloch dynamics of one isochromat, analytic (no time stepping).
L5360: //
L5361: // Conventions (validated against an RK4 integration of dM/dt = gamma M x B in tests/test_bloch.cpp):
L5362: //   - gamma > 0 (1H). In the lab, M precesses clockwise seen from +z: dM/dt = gamma M x B.
L5363: //   - Rotating frame at the transmitter frequency f_tx: the residual field along z is dB = (f_L - f_tx)/gamma_bar,
L5364: //     i.e. an offset df = f_L - f_tx in Hz. The RF field B1 lies in the transverse plane at angle phi from x'.
L5365: //   - Effective field B_eff = (B1 cos phi, B1 sin phi, df/gamma_bar). M rotates about -B_eff (left-handed for gamma>0)
L5366: //     at |omega_eff| = 2 pi gamma_bar |B_eff|.
L5367: //   - Free precession: M_xy(t) = M_xy(0) exp(-i 2 pi df t) exp(-t/T2); M_z(t) = M0 + (M_z(0) - M0) exp(-t/T1).
L5368: // Units: SI (Hz, s, T); M is in units of M0 (dimensionless) unless stated.
L5369: #pragma once
L5370: 
L5371: #include <cmath>
L5372: 
L5373: namespace nmr {
L5374: 
L5375: struct Vec3 {
L5376:     double x = 0, y = 0, z = 0;
L5377:     constexpr Vec3 operator+(Vec3 o) const { return {x + o.x, y + o.y, z + o.z}; }
L5378:     constexpr Vec3 operator-(Vec3 o) const { return {x - o.x, y - o.y, z - o.z}; }
L5379:     constexpr Vec3 operator*(double k) const { return {x * k, y * k, z * k}; }
L5380:     constexpr double dot(Vec3 o) const { return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z; }
L5381:     constexpr Vec3 cross(Vec3 o) const { return {y * o.z - z * o.y, z * o.x - x * o.z, x * o.y - y * o.x}; }
L5382:     double norm() const { return std::sqrt(dot(*this)); }
L5383: };
L5384: 
L5385: // Rotate v about the unit axis n by angle theta (right-handed, Rodrigues).
L5386: inline Vec3 rotate(Vec3 v, Vec3 n, double theta) {
L5387:     const double c = std::cos(theta), s = std::sin(theta);
L5388:     return v * c + n.cross(v) * s + n * (n.dot(v) * (1.0 - c));
L5389: }
L5390: 
L5391: // A hard pulse in the frame rotating at f_tx: rotation about -B_eff for duration t.
L5392: //   b1      : rotating-frame RF amplitude [T]
L5393: //   phase   : RF phase phi [rad] (0 = along +x')
L5394: //   offset  : f_L - f_tx [Hz]
L5395: inline Vec3 pulse(Vec3 m, double gamma_bar, double b1, double phase, double offset_hz, double t) {
L5396:     const double bx = b1 * std::cos(phase), by = b1 * std::sin(phase), bz = offset_hz / gamma_bar;
L5397:     const double b = std::sqrt(bx * bx + by * by + bz * bz);
L5398:     if (b == 0.0) return m;
L5399:     const Vec3 n{-bx / b, -by / b, -bz / b};
L5400:     return rotate(m, n, 2.0 * 3.14159265358979323846 * gamma_bar * b * t);
L5401: }
L5402: 
L5403: // Free precession with relaxation for time t (exact). m0 is the equilibrium Mz (1 for normalised M).
L5404: inline Vec3 precess(Vec3 m, double offset_hz, double t, double t1, double t2, double m0 = 1.0) {
L5405:     const double ph = -2.0 * 3.14159265358979323846 * offset_hz * t;
L5406:     const double c = std::cos(ph), s = std::sin(ph);
L5407:     const double e2 = t2 > 0 ? std::exp(-t / t2) : 1.0;
L5408:     const double e1 = t1 > 0 ? std::exp(-t / t1) : 1.0;
L5409:     return {(m.x * c - m.y * s) * e2, (m.x * s + m.y * c) * e2, m0 + (m.z - m0) * e1};
L5410: }
L5411: 
L5412: // Curie-law equilibrium magnetization M0 = N gamma^2 hbar^2 B0 / (4 k T) for spin-1/2 [A/m].
L5413: inline double curie_magnetization(double n_per_m3, double gamma_bar, double b0, double temperature,
L5414:                                   double hbar, double k_b) {
L5415:     const double g = 2.0 * 3.14159265358979323846 * gamma_bar;
L5416:     return n_per_m3 * g * g * hbar * hbar * b0 / (4.0 * k_b * temperature);
L5417: }
L5418: 
L5419: }  // namespace nmr

L5461: #include "nmr/field.hpp"
L5462: 
L5463: #include <cmath>
L5464: 
L5465: namespace nmr {
L5466: 
L5467: void ellipke(double m, double& K, double& E) {
L5468:     // AGM: K = pi / (2 AGM(1, sqrt(1-m))); E from the same iteration (Abramowitz & Stegun 17.6)
L5469:     double a = 1.0, b = std::sqrt(1.0 - m), c2sum = m / 2.0, pow2 = 0.5;
L5470:     for (int i = 0; i < 40; ++i) {
L5471:         const double an = 0.5 * (a + b), bn = std::sqrt(a * b), cn = 0.5 * (a - b);
L5472:         pow2 *= 2.0;
L5473:         c2sum += pow2 * cn * cn;
L5474:         a = an;
L5475:         b = bn;
L5476:         if (std::fabs(cn) < 1e-17 * a) break;
L5477:     }
L5478:     K = kPi / (2.0 * a);
L5479:     E = K * (1.0 - c2sum);
L5480: }
L5481: 
L5482: static constexpr double kPi = 3.14159265358979323846;
L5483: 
L5484: Vec3 loop_field(double a, double i, int axis, double axis_offset, Vec3 p, double mu0) {
L5485:     // cylindrical coordinates about the loop axis
L5486:     double ax, r1, r2;  // axial coordinate, two transverse coordinates
L5487:     if (axis == 0) { ax = p.x - axis_offset; r1 = p.y; r2 = p.z; }
L5488:     else if (axis == 1) { ax = p.y - axis_offset; r1 = p.z; r2 = p.x; }
L5489:     else { ax = p.z - axis_offset; r1 = p.x; r2 = p.y; }
L5490:     const double rho = std::hypot(r1, r2);
L5491:     const double q = (a + rho) * (a + rho) + ax * ax;
L5492:     const double m = 4.0 * a * rho / q;
L5493:     double K, E;
L5494:     ellipke(m, K, E);
L5495:     const double c = mu0 * i / (2.0 * kPi * std::sqrt(q));
L5496:     const double d = (a - rho) * (a - rho) + ax * ax;
L5497:     const double b_ax = c * (K + (a * a - rho * rho - ax * ax) / d * E);
L5498:     double b_rho = 0.0;
L5499:     if (rho > 1e-12) b_rho = c * ax / rho * (-K + (a * a + rho * rho + ax * ax) / d * E);
L5500:     const double b1 = rho > 1e-12 ? b_rho * r1 / rho : 0.0, b2 = rho > 1e-12 ? b_rho * r2 / rho : 0.0;
L5501:     if (axis == 0) return {b_ax, b1, b2};
L5502:     if (axis == 1) return {b2, b_ax, b1};
L5503:     return {b1, b2, b_ax};
L5504: }
L5505: 
L5506: Vec3 Coil::field_per_amp(Vec3 p, double mu0) const {
L5507:     Vec3 b;
L5508:     for (const auto& f : filaments) b = b + loop_field(f.radius, f.turns, axis, f.position, p, mu0);
L5509:     return b;
L5510: }
L5511: 
L5512: Coil helmholtz_pair(int axis, double radius, double spacing, double turns, double width, double depth, int nw, int nd) {
L5513:     Coil c;
L5514:     c.axis = axis;
L5515:     const double per = turns / (nw * nd);
L5516:     for (int s = -1; s <= 1; s += 2)
L5517:         for (int iw = 0; iw < nw; ++iw)
L5518:             for (int id = 0; id < nd; ++id) {
L5519:                 const double pos = s * spacing / 2.0 + width * ((iw + 0.5) / nw - 0.5);
L5520:                 const double r = radius + depth * ((id + 0.5) / nd - 0.5);
L5521:                 c.filaments.push_back({r, pos, per});
L5522:             }
L5523:     return c;
L5524: }
L5525: 
L5526: Coil layered_solenoid(int axis, const std::vector<double>& radii, const std::vector<int>& turns, double pitch,
L5527:                       double x_start) {
L5528:     Coil c;
L5529:     c.axis = axis;
L5530:     for (size_t k = 0; k < radii.size(); ++k)
L5531:         for (int n = 0; n < turns[k]; ++n) c.filaments.push_back({radii[k], x_start + (n + 0.5) * pitch, 1.0});
L5532:     return c;
L5533: }

L5538: 77 lines
L5539: // The pulse-program intermediate representation (ADR-0004).
L5540: //
L5541: // A program is a flat array of 8-byte instructions executed by a deterministic timing engine. Time only advances
L5542: // in WAIT; every other instruction takes effect at the current time (tick). The IR is hardware-neutral: frequency in
L5543: // millihertz, phase in 1/65536 turn, amplitude as a Q16 fraction, time in engine ticks (design: timing.tick).
L5544: // The same program drives the ESP32 RMT backend, the simulator, and a future FPGA backend.
L5545: #pragma once
L5546: 
L5547: #include <cstdint>
L5548: 
L5549: namespace pulse {
L5550: 
L5551: enum class Op : uint8_t {
L5552:     END = 0,           // end of program
L5553:     WAIT = 1,          // advance time by arg ticks (arg > 0)
L5554:     SET_FREQ = 2,      // TX channel chan: frequency = arg mHz
L5555:     SET_PHASE = 3,     // TX channel chan: RF phase = arg (low 16 bits) / 65536 turn
L5556:     SET_AMPLITUDE = 4, // TX channel chan: amplitude = arg / 65536 of full scale (<= 65536)
L5557:     TX_GATE_ON = 5,    // TX channel chan: transmitter output enabled
L5558:     TX_GATE_OFF = 6,
L5559:     RX_BLANK = 7,      // receiver chan: protected / blanked
L5560:     RX_ENABLE = 8,     // receiver chan: un-blanked
L5561:     ACQ_ON = 9,        // receiver chan: start keeping samples (a window); arg = window id
L5562:     ACQ_OFF = 10,
L5563:     RX_PHASE = 11,     // receiver chan: phase the DSP must rotate this scan's data by (arg/65536 turn); metadata
L5564:     SCAN_BEGIN = 12,   // arg = scan index (for averaging / phase cycling bookkeeping)
L5565:     SCAN_END = 13,
L5566:     LOOP_BEGIN = 14,   // repeat the body up to the matching LOOP_END arg times (arg >= 1)
L5567:     LOOP_END = 15,
L5568:     MARKER = 16,       // arg = marker id; backends timestamp it (e.g. 1 = excitation pulse start)
L5569:     SYNC_WAIT = 17,    // wait for the external reference edge (chan = source); backends that cannot, reject it
L5570: };
L5571: 
L5572: constexpr const char* op_name(Op op) {
L5573:     switch (op) {
L5574:         case Op::END: return "END";
L5575:         case Op::WAIT: return "WAIT";
L5576:         case Op::SET_FREQ: return "SET_FREQ";
L5577:         case Op::SET_PHASE: return "SET_PHASE";
L5578:         case Op::SET_AMPLITUDE: return "SET_AMPLITUDE";
L5579:         case Op::TX_GATE_ON: return "TX_GATE_ON";
L5580:         case Op::TX_GATE_OFF: return "TX_GATE_OFF";
L5581:         case Op::RX_BLANK: return "RX_BLANK";
L5582:         case Op::RX_ENABLE: return "RX_ENABLE";
L5583:         case Op::ACQ_ON: return "ACQ_ON";
L5584:         case Op::ACQ_OFF: return "ACQ_OFF";
L5585:         case Op::RX_PHASE: return "RX_PHASE";
L5586:         case Op::SCAN_BEGIN: return "SCAN_BEGIN";
L5587:         case Op::SCAN_END: return "SCAN_END";
L5588:         case Op::LOOP_BEGIN: return "LOOP_BEGIN";
L5589:         case Op::LOOP_END: return "LOOP_END";
L5590:         case Op::MARKER: return "MARKER";
L5591:         case Op::SYNC_WAIT: return "SYNC_WAIT";
L5592:     }
L5593:     return "?";
L5594: }

L5680: // An experiment is a list of elements (pulse, delay, acquire). The compiler owns receiver protection: it blanks the
L5681: // receiver pre_blank before every pulse, keeps it blanked for dead_time after, and only opens acquisition windows on
