L5745: uint32_t crc32(const uint8_t* d, size_t n) {
L5746:     uint32_t c = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
L5747:     for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
L5748:         c ^= d[i];
L5749:         for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) c = (c >> 1) ^ (0xEDB88320u & (0u - (c & 1u)));
L5750:     }
L5751:     return ~c;
L5752: }
L5753: 
L5754: bool expand(const Program& p, std::vector<Event>& out, Diagnostic& diag, uint64_t max_events) {
L5755:     out.clear();
L5756:     struct Frame { size_t body; uint32_t left; };
L5757:     Frame stack[kMaxLoopDepth];
L5758:     int depth = 0;
L5759:     uint64_t t = 0;
L5760:     size_t pc = 0, steps = 0;
L5761:     // structural pass: balanced loops, END present
L5762:     {
L5763:         int d = 0;
L5764:         bool end = false;
L5765:         for (size_t i = 0; i < p.code.size(); ++i) {
L5766:             const Op op = p.code[i].op;
L5767:             if (op == Op::LOOP_BEGIN) {
L5768:                 if (++d > kMaxLoopDepth) { diag = {i, "loop nesting deeper than kMaxLoopDepth"}; return false; }
L5769:                 if (p.code[i].arg == 0) { diag = {i, "LOOP_BEGIN with zero count"}; return false; }
L5770:             } else if (op == Op::LOOP_END) {
L5771:                 if (--d < 0) { diag = {i, "LOOP_END without LOOP_BEGIN"}; return false; }
L5772:             } else if (op == Op::WAIT && p.code[i].arg == 0) {
L5773:                 diag = {i, "WAIT 0"};
L5774:                 return false;
L5775:             } else if (op == Op::END) {
L5776:                 if (d != 0) { diag = {i, "END inside a loop"}; return false; }
L5777:                 end = true;
L5778:                 break;
L5779:             }
L5780:         }
L5781:         if (!end) { diag = {p.code.size(), "program has no END"}; return false; }
L5782:         if (p.code.size() > kMaxInstructions) { diag = {kMaxInstructions, "program exceeds kMaxInstructions"}; return false; }
L5783:     }
L5784:     while (pc < p.code.size()) {
L5785:         if (++steps > max_events * 4 + 1000) { diag = {pc, "execution step budget exceeded"}; return false; }
L5786:         const Instr& in = p.code[pc];
L5787:         switch (in.op) {
L5788:             case Op::END:
L5789:                 out.push_back({t, Op::END, 0, 0});
L5790:                 return true;
L5791:             case Op::WAIT:
L5792:                 t += in.arg;
L5793:                 ++pc;
L5794:                 break;
L5795:             case Op::LOOP_BEGIN:
L5796:                 stack[depth++] = {pc + 1, in.arg};
L5797:                 ++pc;
L5798:                 break;
L5799:             case Op::LOOP_END:
L5800:                 if (--stack[depth - 1].left > 0) {
L5801:                     pc = stack[depth - 1].body;
L5802:                 } else {
L5803:                     --depth;
L5804:                     ++pc;
L5805:                 }
L5806:                 break;
L5807:             default:
L5808:                 if (out.size() >= max_events) { diag = {pc, "unrolled program exceeds the event budget"}; return false; }
L5809:                 out.push_back({t, in.op, in.chan, in.arg});
L5810:                 ++pc;
L5811:         }
L5812:     }
L5813:     diag = {pc, "fell off the end of the program"};
L5814:     return false;
L5815: }
L5816: 
L5817: std::vector<Diagnostic> validate(const Program& p, const Limits& lim) {
L5818:     std::vector<Diagnostic> d;
L5819:     if (std::fabs(p.tick_hz - lim.tick_hz) > 1e-9 * lim.tick_hz) d.push_back({0, "program tick differs from the engine tick"});
L5820:     std::vector<Event> ev;
L5821:     Diagnostic ed;
L5822:     if (!expand(p, ev, ed, lim.max_events)) {
L5823:         d.push_back(ed);
L5824:         return d;
L5825:     }
L5826:     const double tick = lim.tick_hz;
L5827:     const auto ticks = [&](double s) { return static_cast<uint64_t>(std::llround(s * tick)); };
L5828:     bool tx_on[kTxChannels] = {}, rx_blanked[kRxChannels], acq[kRxChannels] = {};
L5829:     uint64_t tx_since[kTxChannels] = {}, blank_since[kRxChannels] = {}, last_tx_off = 0;
L5830:     bool any_tx_yet = false;
L5831:     for (int i = 0; i < kRxChannels; ++i) rx_blanked[i] = true;   // engines start blanked
L5832:     uint64_t tx_total = 0;
L5833:     for (size_t i = 0; i < ev.size(); ++i) {
L5834:         const Event& e = ev[i];
L5835:         const auto bad = [&](const std::string& m) { d.push_back({i, m}); };
L5836:         const bool txc = e.chan < kTxChannels, rxc = e.chan < kRxChannels;
L5837:         switch (e.op) {
L5838:             case Op::SET_FREQ: {
L5839:                 if (!txc) { bad("TX channel out of range"); break; }
L5840:                 const double f = e.arg / 1000.0;
L5841:                 if (f < lim.tx_min_freq_hz || f > lim.tx_max_freq_hz) bad("frequency outside the supported range");
L5842:                 break;
L5843:             }
L5844:             case Op::SET_AMPLITUDE:
L5845:                 if (!txc) bad("TX channel out of range");
L5846:                 if (e.arg > kTurn) bad("amplitude above full scale");
L5847:                 break;
L5848:             case Op::SET_PHASE:
L5849:                 if (!txc) bad("TX channel out of range");
L5850:                 if (e.arg >= kTurn) bad("phase operand must be < 65536");
L5851:                 break;
L5852:             case Op::TX_GATE_ON:
L5853:                 if (!txc) { bad("TX channel out of range"); break; }
L5854:                 if (tx_on[e.chan]) bad("TX gate already on");
L5855:                 for (int r = 0; r < kRxChannels; ++r) {
L5856:                     if (!rx_blanked[r]) bad("TX gate on while a receiver is enabled");
L5857:                     else if (e.t - blank_since[r] < ticks(lim.pre_blank_s)) bad("TX gate on before the pre-blank interval");
L5858:                 }
L5859:                 tx_on[e.chan] = true;
L5860:                 tx_since[e.chan] = e.t;
L5861:                 break;
L5862:             case Op::TX_GATE_OFF:
L5863:                 if (!txc) { bad("TX channel out of range"); break; }
L5864:                 if (!tx_on[e.chan]) { bad("TX gate off while off"); break; }
L5865:                 if (e.t - tx_since[e.chan] > ticks(lim.tx_max_pulse_s)) bad("TX pulse longer than the hardware limit");
L5866:                 tx_total += e.t - tx_since[e.chan];
L5867:                 tx_on[e.chan] = false;
L5868:                 last_tx_off = e.t;
L5869:                 any_tx_yet = true;
L5870:                 break;
L5871:             case Op::RX_BLANK:
L5872:                 if (!rxc) { bad("RX channel out of range"); break; }
L5873:                 if (acq[e.chan]) bad("receiver blanked inside an acquisition window");
L5874:                 if (!rx_blanked[e.chan]) blank_since[e.chan] = e.t;
L5875:                 rx_blanked[e.chan] = true;
L5876:                 break;
L5877:             case Op::RX_ENABLE:
L5878:                 if (!rxc) { bad("RX channel out of range"); break; }
L5879:                 for (int c = 0; c < kTxChannels; ++c)
L5880:                     if (tx_on[c]) bad("receiver enabled while TX is on");
L5881:                 if (any_tx_yet && e.t - last_tx_off < ticks(lim.dead_time_s)) bad("receiver enabled inside the dead time");
L5882:                 rx_blanked[e.chan] = false;
L5883:                 break;
L5884:             case Op::ACQ_ON:
L5885:                 if (!rxc) { bad("RX channel out of range"); break; }
L5886:                 if (rx_blanked[e.chan]) bad("acquisition window opened on a blanked receiver");
L5887:                 if (acq[e.chan]) bad("acquisition window already open");
L5888:                 acq[e.chan] = true;
L5889:                 break;
L5890:             case Op::ACQ_OFF:
L5891:                 if (!rxc) { bad("RX channel out of range"); break; }
L5892:                 if (!acq[e.chan]) bad("acquisition window closed while closed");
L5893:                 acq[e.chan] = false;
L5894:                 break;
L5895:             case Op::SYNC_WAIT:
L5896:                 if (!lim.backend_has_sync_input) bad("SYNC_WAIT but the backend has no sync input");
L5897:                 break;
L5898:             case Op::END:
L5899:                 for (int c = 0; c < kTxChannels; ++c)
L5900:                     if (tx_on[c]) bad("program ends with TX on");
L5901:                 for (int r = 0; r < kRxChannels; ++r)
L5902:                     if (acq[r]) bad("program ends inside an acquisition window");
L5903:                 if (e.t > 0 && static_cast<double>(tx_total) / static_cast<double>(e.t) > lim.tx_max_duty)
L5904:                     bad("TX duty cycle above the limit");
L5905:                 break;
L5906:             default:
L5907:                 break;
L5908:         }
L5909:     }
L5910:     return d;
L5911: }

L5913: static void put(std::vector<uint8_t>& b, const void* p, size_t n) {
L5914:     const auto* c = static_cast<const uint8_t*>(p);
L5915:     b.insert(b.end(), c, c + n);   // little-endian hosts only (ESP32, x86, wasm): asserted below
L5916: }
L5917: static_assert(sizeof(double) == 8, "binary format assumes IEEE-754 binary64");
L5918: 
L5919: std::vector<uint8_t> encode(const Program& p) {
L5920:     std::vector<uint8_t> b;
L5921:     const char magic[4] = {'N', 'M', 'R', 'P'};
L5922:     const uint16_t ver = 1, res = 0;
L5923:     const uint32_t n = static_cast<uint32_t>(p.code.size());
L5924:     const uint32_t crc = crc32(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(p.code.data()), n * sizeof(Instr));
L5925:     put(b, magic, 4);
L5926:     put(b, &ver, 2);
L5927:     put(b, &res, 2);
L5928:     put(b, &p.tick_hz, 8);
L5929:     put(b, &n, 4);
L5930:     put(b, &crc, 4);
L5931:     put(b, p.code.data(), n * sizeof(Instr));
L5932:     return b;
L5933: }
L5934: 
L5935: bool decode(const uint8_t* d, size_t n, Program& out, std::string& err) {
L5936:     if (n < 24 || std::memcmp(d, "NMRP", 4) != 0) { err = "bad magic"; return false; }
L5937:     uint16_t ver;
L5938:     std::memcpy(&ver, d + 4, 2);
L5939:     if (ver != 1) { err = "unsupported version"; return false; }
L5940:     uint32_t cnt, crc;
L5941:     std::memcpy(&out.tick_hz, d + 8, 8);
L5942:     std::memcpy(&cnt, d + 16, 4);
L5943:     std::memcpy(&crc, d + 20, 4);
L5944:     if (cnt > kMaxInstructions || n != 24 + static_cast<size_t>(cnt) * 8) { err = "bad size"; return false; }
L5945:     if (crc32(d + 24, cnt * 8u) != crc) { err = "CRC mismatch"; return false; }
L5946:     out.code.resize(cnt);
L5947:     std::memcpy(out.code.data(), d + 24, cnt * 8u);
L5948:     return true;
L5949: }
L5950: 
L5951: std::string disassemble(const Program& p) {
L5952:     std::ostringstream s;
L5953:     uint64_t t = 0;
L5954:     for (size_t i = 0; i < p.code.size(); ++i) {
L5955:         const Instr& in = p.code[i];
L5956:         s << i << "\t" << (static_cast<double>(t) / p.tick_hz * 1e6) << " us\t" << op_name(in.op) << " ch" << int(in.chan)
L5957:           << " " << in.arg << "\n";
L5958:         if (in.op == Op::WAIT) t += in.arg;   // loops not unrolled here
L5959:     }
L5960:     return s.str();
L5961: }

L5981: uint32_t phase_units(double turns) {
L5982:     double f = turns - std::floor(turns);
L5983:     uint32_t u = static_cast<uint32_t>(std::llround(f * kTurn));
L5984:     return u % kTurn;
L5985: }

L5989: bool compile(const std::vector<Scan>& scans, const Timing& tm, Program& out, std::vector<Diagnostic>& diag) {
L5990:     diag.clear();
L5991:     const auto ticks = [&](double s) { return static_cast<uint64_t>(std::llround(s * tm.tick_hz)); };
L5992:     std::vector<TimedInstr> ev;
L5993:     uint32_t seq = 0;
L5994:     const auto emit = [&](uint64_t t, Op op, uint8_t ch, uint32_t arg) { ev.push_back({t, seq++, Instr{op, ch, 0, arg}}); };
L5995: 
L5996:     const uint64_t tr = ticks(tm.repetition_s), pre = ticks(tm.pre_blank_s), dead = ticks(tm.dead_time_s);
L5997:     emit(0, Op::RX_BLANK, 0, 0);
L5998:     emit(0, Op::SET_FREQ, 0, static_cast<uint32_t>(std::llround(tm.tx_freq_hz * 1000.0)));
L5999:     emit(0, Op::SET_AMPLITUDE, 0, kTurn);
L6000:     uint64_t end_all = 0;
L6001:     for (size_t k = 0; k < scans.size(); ++k) {
L6002:         const uint64_t t0 = k * tr;
L6003:         uint64_t t = t0 + pre;   // the first pulse can come once the receiver has been blanked for pre_blank
L6004:         bool rx_enabled = false, acq_open = false;
L6005:         uint64_t last_tx_off = 0, last_event = t0;
L6006:         bool tx_yet = false;
L6007:         uint32_t amp = kTurn;
L6008:         emit(t0, Op::SCAN_BEGIN, 0, static_cast<uint32_t>(k));
L6009:         emit(t0, Op::RX_PHASE, 0, phase_units(scans[k].rx_phase_turns));
L6010:         for (size_t j = 0; j < scans[k].elements.size(); ++j) {
L6011:             const Element& e = scans[k].elements[j];
L6012:             const uint64_t d = ticks(e.duration_s);
L6013:             const auto fail = [&](const std::string& m) { diag.push_back({j, "scan " + std::to_string(k) + ": " + m}); };
L6014:             if (e.kind == Element::Pulse) {
L6015:                 if (d == 0) { fail("pulse rounds to zero ticks"); continue; }
L6016:                 if (acq_open) { fail("pulse inside an open acquisition window"); continue; }
L6017:                 if (rx_enabled) {
L6018:                     if (t < last_event + pre) { fail("too little time before the pulse to blank the receiver"); continue; }
L6019:                     emit(t - pre, Op::RX_BLANK, 0, 0);
L6020:                     rx_enabled = false;
L6021:                 }
L6022:                 const uint32_t a = static_cast<uint32_t>(std::llround(std::clamp(e.amplitude, 0.0, 1.0) * kTurn));
L6023:                 if (a != amp) { emit(t - pre, Op::SET_AMPLITUDE, 0, a); amp = a; }
L6024:                 emit(t - pre, Op::SET_PHASE, 0, phase_units(e.phase_turns));
L6025:                 if (e.marker) emit(t, Op::MARKER, 0, e.marker);
L6026:                 emit(t, Op::TX_GATE_ON, 0, 0);
L6027:                 t += d;
L6028:                 emit(t, Op::TX_GATE_OFF, 0, 0);
L6029:                 last_tx_off = t;
L6030:                 tx_yet = true;
L6031:                 last_event = t;
L6032:             } else if (e.kind == Element::Delay) {
L6033:                 t += d;
L6034:             } else {  // Acquire
L6035:                 if (d == 0) { fail("acquisition window rounds to zero ticks"); continue; }
L6036:                 if (tx_yet && t < last_tx_off + dead) { fail("acquisition starts inside the receiver dead time"); continue; }
L6037:                 if (!rx_enabled) {
L6038:                     emit(tx_yet ? last_tx_off + dead : t, Op::RX_ENABLE, 0, 0);
L6039:                     rx_enabled = true;
L6040:                 }
L6041:                 emit(t, Op::ACQ_ON, 0, e.window);
L6042:                 t += d;
L6043:                 emit(t, Op::ACQ_OFF, 0, e.window);
L6044:                 last_event = t;
L6045:             }
L6046:         }
L6047:         if (rx_enabled) emit(t, Op::RX_BLANK, 0, 0);
L6048:         emit(t, Op::SCAN_END, 0, static_cast<uint32_t>(k));
L6049:         if (k + 1 < scans.size() && t + pre > (k + 1) * tr)
L6050:             diag.push_back({0, "scan " + std::to_string(k) + " is longer than the repetition time"});
L6051:         end_all = std::max(end_all, t);
L6052:     }
L6053:     if (!diag.empty()) return false;
L6054:     std::stable_sort(ev.begin(), ev.end(), [](const TimedInstr& a, const TimedInstr& b) {
L6055:         return a.t != b.t ? a.t < b.t : a.seq < b.seq;
L6056:     });
L6057:     out.tick_hz = tm.tick_hz;
L6058:     out.code.clear();
L6059:     uint64_t now = 0;
L6060:     for (const auto& x : ev) {
L6061:         while (x.t > now) {
L6062:             const uint64_t w = std::min<uint64_t>(x.t - now, 0xFFFFFFFFull);
L6063:             out.code.push_back({Op::WAIT, 0, 0, static_cast<uint32_t>(w)});
L6064:             now += w;
L6065:         }
L6066:         out.code.push_back(x.in);
L6067:     }
L6068:     out.code.push_back({Op::END, 0, 0, 0});
L6069:     if (out.code.size() > kMaxInstructions) {
L6070:         diag.push_back({0, "compiled program exceeds kMaxInstructions"});
L6071:         return false;
L6072:     }
L6073:     return true;
L6074: }

L6076: namespace {
L6077: double cyc(int k, Cycle c) { return c == Cycle::Cyclops ? 0.25 * (k % 4) : 0.0; }
L6078: Element P(double d, double ph, uint32_t marker = 0) { Element e; e.kind = Element::Pulse; e.duration_s = d; e.phase_turns = ph; e.marker = marker; return e; }
L6079: Element D(double d) { Element e; e.kind = Element::Delay; e.duration_s = d; return e; }
L6080: Element A(double d, uint32_t w = 0) { Element e; e.kind = Element::Acquire; e.duration_s = d; e.window = w; return e; }

L6092: std::vector<Scan> hahn_echo(double t90, double t180, double tau, double acq_len, int n_avg, Cycle c) {
L6093:     // centres: 90 at 0, 180 at tau, echo at 2 tau; 180 phase +90 degrees (90x - 180y)
L6094:     std::vector<Scan> s;
L6095:     for (int k = 0; k < n_avg; ++k) {
L6096:         const double p90 = cyc(k, c), p180 = cyc(k, c) + 0.25;
L6097:         s.push_back({{P(t90, p90, kMarkerExcitation), D(tau - t90 / 2 - t180 / 2), P(t180, p180, kMarkerRefocus),
L6098:                       D(tau - t180 / 2 - acq_len / 2), A(acq_len)},
L6099:                      2 * p180 - p90});
L6100:     }
L6101:     return s;
L6102: }

L6114: std::vector<Scan> cpmg(double t90, double t180, double tau, int n_echoes, double acq_len, int n_avg, Cycle c) {
L6115:     std::vector<Scan> s;
L6116:     for (int k = 0; k < n_avg; ++k) {

L6118:         Scan sc;
L6119:         sc.rx_phase_turns = 2 * p180 - p90;
L6120:         sc.elements.push_back(P(t90, p90, kMarkerExcitation));
L6121:         sc.elements.push_back(D(tau - t90 / 2 - t180 / 2));
L6122:         for (int n = 0; n < n_echoes; ++n) {
L6123:             sc.elements.push_back(P(t180, p180, kMarkerRefocus));
L6124:             sc.elements.push_back(D(tau - t180 / 2 - acq_len / 2));
L6125:             sc.elements.push_back(A(acq_len, static_cast<uint32_t>(n)));
L6126:             sc.elements.push_back(D(tau - acq_len / 2 - t180 / 2));
L6127:         }
L6128:         s.push_back(sc);
L6129:     }
L6130:     return s;
L6131: }

L6217: static constexpr double kPi = 3.14159265358979323846;
L6218: 
L6219: size_t next_pow2(size_t n) {
L6220:     size_t p = 1;
L6221:     while (p < n) p <<= 1;
L6222:     return p;
L6223: }
L6224: 
L6225: FftPlan::FftPlan(size_t n) : n_(n), tw_(n / 2), rev_(n) {
L6226:     for (size_t k = 0; k < n / 2; ++k) tw_[k] = std::polar(1.0, -2.0 * kPi * static_cast<double>(k) / static_cast<double>(n));
L6227:     size_t bits = 0;
L6228:     while ((size_t{1} << bits) < n) ++bits;
L6229:     for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
L6230:         size_t r = 0;
L6231:         for (size_t b = 0; b < bits; ++b)
L6232:             if (i & (size_t{1} << b)) r |= size_t{1} << (bits - 1 - b);
L6233:         rev_[i] = r;
L6234:     }
L6235: }
L6236: 
L6237: void FftPlan::execute(cplx* a, bool inverse) const {
L6238:     for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i)
L6239:         if (i < rev_[i]) std::swap(a[i], a[rev_[i]]);
L6240:     for (size_t len = 2; len <= n_; len <<= 1) {
L6241:         const size_t half = len / 2, step = n_ / len;
L6242:         for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; i += len)
L6243:             for (size_t j = 0; j < half; ++j) {
L6244:                 cplx w = tw_[j * step];
L6245:                 if (inverse) w = std::conj(w);
L6246:                 const cplx u = a[i + j], v = a[i + j + half] * w;
L6247:                 a[i + j] = u + v;
L6248:                 a[i + j + half] = u - v;
L6249:             }
L6250:     }
L6251: }
L6252: 
L6253: void dft_reference(const cplx* in, cplx* out, size_t n) {
L6254:     for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
L6255:         cplx s = 0;
L6256:         for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j)
L6257:             s += in[j] * std::polar(1.0, -2.0 * kPi * static_cast<double>((k * j) % n) / static_cast<double>(n));
L6258:         out[k] = s;
L6259:     }
L6260: }
L6261: 
L6262: FirDecimator::FirDecimator(double fc, double fs, size_t taps, size_t decim) : h_(taps), decim_(decim) {
L6263:     const double m = static_cast<double>(taps) - 1.0, w = 2.0 * fc / fs;
L6264:     double sum = 0;
L6265:     for (size_t k = 0; k < taps; ++k) {
L6266:         const double x = static_cast<double>(k) - m / 2.0;
L6267:         const double sinc = x == 0 ? w : std::sin(kPi * w * x) / (kPi * x);
L6268:         const double bl = 0.42 - 0.5 * std::cos(2 * kPi * k / m) + 0.08 * std::cos(4 * kPi * k / m);
L6269:         h_[k] = sinc * bl;
L6270:         sum += h_[k];
L6271:     }
L6272:     for (double& v : h_) v /= sum;   // unity DC gain
L6273: }
L6274: 
L6275: size_t FirDecimator::process(const cplx* x, size_t n, cplx* out, size_t cap) const {
L6276:     const size_t t = h_.size();
L6277:     if (n < t) return 0;
L6278:     size_t m = 0;
L6279:     for (size_t s = 0; s + t <= n && m < cap; s += decim_, ++m) {
L6280:         cplx acc = 0;
L6281:         for (size_t k = 0; k < t; ++k) acc += h_[k] * x[s + k];
L6282:         out[m] = acc;
L6283:     }
L6284:     return m;
L6285: }
L6286: 
L6287: void mix_down(const double* i, const double* q, size_t n, cplx off, double f_if, double fs, double t0, cplx* out) {
L6288:     for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
L6289:         const double ph = 2.0 * kPi * f_if * (t0 + static_cast<double>(k) / fs);
L6290:         out[k] = (cplx(i[k], q[k]) - off) * std::polar(1.0, -ph);
L6291:     }
L6292: }
L6293: 
L6294: cplx mean(const cplx* x, size_t n) {
L6295:     cplx s = 0;
L6296:     for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) s += x[k];
L6297:     return n ? s / static_cast<double>(n) : s;
L6298: }
L6299: 
L6300: void rotate(cplx* x, size_t n, double rad) {
L6301:     const cplx r = std::polar(1.0, -rad);
L6302:     for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) x[k] *= r;
L6303: }
L6304: 
L6305: void accumulate(cplx* acc, const cplx* x, size_t n) {
L6306:     for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) acc[k] += x[k];
L6307: }
L6308: 
L6309: static double wval(Window w, size_t k, size_t n, double fs, double tc) {
L6310:     const double nn = static_cast<double>(n), kk = static_cast<double>(k);
L6311:     switch (w) {
L6312:         case Window::Rect: return 1.0;
L6313:         case Window::Hann: return 0.5 - 0.5 * std::cos(2 * kPi * kk / (nn - 1));
L6314:         case Window::Exponential: return std::exp(-kk / fs / tc);
L6315:         case Window::HalfHann: return 0.5 + 0.5 * std::cos(kPi * kk / nn);
L6316:     }
L6317:     return 1.0;
L6318: }
L6319: 
L6320: void apply_window(cplx* x, size_t n, Window w, double fs, double tc) {
L6321:     for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) x[k] *= wval(w, k, n, fs, tc);
L6322: }
L6323: 
L6324: Spectrum spectrum(const cplx* x, size_t n, double fs, Window w, double tc, int pad) {
L6325:     const size_t nf = next_pow2(n * static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, pad)));
L6326:     std::vector<cplx> buf(nf, cplx(0));
L6327:     double wsum = 0;
L6328:     for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
L6329:         const double v = wval(w, k, n, fs, tc);
L6330:         buf[k] = x[k] * v;
L6331:         wsum += v;
L6332:     }
L6333:     FftPlan(nf).execute(buf.data());
L6334:     Spectrum s;
L6335:     s.freq_hz.resize(nf);
L6336:     s.value.resize(nf);
L6337:     for (size_t k = 0; k < nf; ++k) {
L6338:         const size_t src = (k + nf / 2) % nf;
L6339:         const double kk = static_cast<double>(k) - static_cast<double>(nf / 2);
L6340:         s.freq_hz[k] = kk * fs / static_cast<double>(nf);
L6341:         s.value[k] = buf[src] / wsum;
L6342:     }
L6343:     return s;
L6344: }
L6345: 
L6346: Peak find_peak(const Spectrum& s, double f_min, double f_max, double guard) {
L6347:     Peak p;
L6348:     size_t best = 0;
L6349:     double bv = -1;
L6350:     for (size_t k = 0; k < s.freq_hz.size(); ++k)
L6351:         if (s.freq_hz[k] >= f_min && s.freq_hz[k] <= f_max && std::abs(s.value[k]) > bv) { bv = std::abs(s.value[k]); best = k; }
L6352:     if (bv < 0) return p;
L6353:     p.amplitude = bv;
L6354:     p.freq_hz = s.freq_hz[best];
L6355:     if (best > 0 && best + 1 < s.value.size()) {
L6356:         const double a = std::abs(s.value[best - 1]), b = bv, c = std::abs(s.value[best + 1]);
L6357:         const double den = a - 2 * b + c;
L6358:         if (den != 0) p.freq_hz += 0.5 * (a - c) / den * (s.freq_hz[1] - s.freq_hz[0]);
L6359:     }
L6360:     double acc = 0;
L6361:     size_t cnt = 0;
L6362:     for (size_t k = 0; k < s.freq_hz.size(); ++k)
L6363:         if (s.freq_hz[k] >= f_min && s.freq_hz[k] <= f_max && std::fabs(s.freq_hz[k] - p.freq_hz) > guard) {
L6364:             acc += std::norm(s.value[k]);
L6365:             ++cnt;
L6366:         }
L6367:     p.noise_rms = cnt ? std::sqrt(acc / cnt / 2.0) : 0;
L6368:     p.snr = p.noise_rms > 0 ? p.amplitude / p.noise_rms : 0;
L6369:     return p;
L6370: }

L6528: bool SampleGeometry::inside(Vec3 p) const {
L6529:     const double x_end = x_cyl_end + cone_length;
L6530:     if (p.x < x_min || p.x > x_end) return false;
L6531:     double r = radius;
L6532:     if (p.x > x_cyl_end) {
L6533:         const double u = (p.x - x_cyl_end) / cone_length;
L6534:         r = radius * (1 - u) + tip_radius * u;
L6535:     }
L6536:     return p.y * p.y + p.z * p.z <= r * r;
L6537: }
L6538: 
L6539: double SampleGeometry::analytic_volume() const {
L6540:     const double pi = 3.14159265358979323846;
L6541:     return pi * radius * radius * (x_cyl_end - x_min) +
L6542:            pi / 3.0 * cone_length * (radius * radius + radius * tip_radius + tip_radius * tip_radius);
L6543: }

L6550:     const int n = static_cast<int>(RF_COIL_TURNS);
L6551:     const int per_layer = static_cast<int>(std::floor(RF_COIL_LENGTH_M / RF_COIL_WIRE_PITCH_M));  // 222
L6552:     pg.rf = layered_solenoid(0, {RF_COIL_LAYER1_RADIUS_M, RF_COIL_LAYER2_RADIUS_M}, {per_layer, n - per_layer},
L6553:                              RF_COIL_WIRE_PITCH_M, -RF_COIL_LENGTH_M / 2.0);
L6554:     return pg;
L6555: }
L6556: 
L6557: SampleGeometry design_sample() {
L6558:     using namespace cfg;
L6559:     return {SAMPLE_RADIUS_M, SAMPLE_X_MIN_M, SAMPLE_X_CYL_END_M, SAMPLE_CONE_LENGTH_M};
L6560: }
L6561: 
L6562: void transverse(Vec3 b0, Vec3 b1, double& b1_perp, double& phi) {
L6563:     const Vec3 n = b0 * (1.0 / b0.norm());
L6564:     Vec3 ex = Vec3{1, 0, 0} - n * n.x;
L6565:     ex = ex * (1.0 / ex.norm());
L6566:     const Vec3 ey = n.cross(ex);
L6567:     const Vec3 bp = b1 - n * b1.dot(n);
L6568:     b1_perp = bp.norm();
L6569:     phi = std::atan2(bp.dot(ey), bp.dot(ex));
L6570: }
L6571: 
L6572: std::vector<Voxel> voxelize(const ProbeGeometry& pg, const SampleGeometry& sg, double g) {
L6573:     std::vector<Voxel> out;
L6574:     const double x1 = sg.x_cyl_end + sg.cone_length, r = sg.radius;
L6575:     // centred grid: points at k*g + g/2 symmetric about the sample axis; along x aligned to x_min
L6576:     const int nyz = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(r / g));
L6577:     for (double x = sg.x_min + g / 2; x < x1; x += g)
L6578:         for (int iy = -nyz; iy < nyz; ++iy)
L6579:             for (int iz = -nyz; iz < nyz; ++iz) {
L6580:                 const Vec3 p{x, (iy + 0.5) * g, (iz + 0.5) * g};
L6581:                 if (!sg.inside(p)) continue;
L6582:                 const Vec3 b0 = pg.b0.field_per_amp(p, pg.mu0), b1 = pg.rf.field_per_amp(p, pg.mu0);
L6583:                 Voxel v;
L6584:                 v.p = p;
L6585:                 v.volume = g * g * g;
L6586:                 v.b0_per_amp = b0.norm();
L6587:                 v.b0_hat = b0 * (1.0 / v.b0_per_amp);
L6588:                 transverse(b0, b1, v.b1_perp, v.b1_phi);
L6589:                 out.push_back(v);
L6590:             }
L6591:     return out;
L6592: }

L6598: // The digital twin: executes a compiled pulse program (the same one the firmware runs) on the voxel model and
L6599: // produces what the ADC would record: I and Q codes on the free-running ADC sample grid, per acquisition window,
L6600: // with the per-scan metadata the hardware will timestamp (excitation marker time, receiver phase, TX-LO beat phase).
L6601: //
L6602: // Model chain (docs/physics.md has the equations and their sources):
L6603: //   voxel magnetization  -> exact pulse rotations / free precession with T1,T2 (bloch.hpp)
L6604: //   reciprocity emf      -> emf_v = omega_v * B1perp_v/I * M0_v * dV   [V per unit transverse M/M0]
L6605: //   tank                 -> H(f) = 1 / (1 - (f/f0)^2 + j (f/f0)/Q)   (|H(f0)| = Q)
L6606: //   receiver             -> real gain G, single-pole IF response, quadrature mix to the IF with the TX-LO beat phase
L6607: //   ADC                  -> + Gaussian noise (density at the tank node x G, noise bandwidth pi/2 x IF pole), offsets,
L6608: //                           16-bit quantisation and clipping at +-full scale
L6609: #pragma once
L6610: 
L6611: #include <cstdint>
L6612: #include <vector>
L6613: 
L6614: #include "nmr/probe.hpp"
L6615: #include "pulse/program.hpp"

L6619: struct InstrumentModel {
L6620:     // physics
L6621:     double gamma_bar = 0, b0_current = 0, t1 = 0, t2 = 0, proton_density = 0, temperature = 0, hbar = 0, k_b = 0;
L6622:     Vec3 earth_field{};                 // [T] added to the B0 pair's field
L6623:     // transmit
L6624:     double tx_coil_current = 0;         // [A pk] in the RF coil at full amplitude
L6625:     // receive
L6626:     double tank_f0 = 0, tank_q = 0;     // [Hz], [-]
L6627:     double rx_gain = 0;                 // tank node -> ADC input, real [V/V] (LNA x mixer x IF)
L6628:     double if_pole_hz = 0;
L6629:     double noise_density = 0;           // [V/sqrt(Hz)] at the tank node
L6630:     double lo_hz = 0;
L6631:     double adc_full_scale = 0;          // [V] (+-)
L6632:     int adc_bits = 16;
L6633:     double adc_rate = 0;                // raw samples/s per channel
L6634:     double adc_t0 = 0;                  // phase of the free-running ADC grid [s]
L6635:     double offset_i = 0, offset_q = 0;  // [V] at the ADC
L6636:     // coherence (ADR-0006)
L6637:     bool lo_coherent = true;            // false: each scan starts at a random TX-LO beat phase
L6638:     double beat_measurement_sigma = 0;  // [rad] error of the per-scan beat-phase measurement (timestamps)
L6639:     uint64_t seed = 1;
L6640:     // numerics
L6641:     int interp = 8;                     // fast path: evaluate the baseband every `interp` ADC samples (1 = reference)

L6683: constexpr double kPi = 3.14159265358979323846;
L6684: using cplx = std::complex<double>;
L6685: 
L6686: struct Rng {   // xoshiro256** + Box-Muller: reproducible across native and wasm
L6687:     uint64_t s[4];
L6688:     explicit Rng(uint64_t seed) {
L6689:         uint64_t z = seed + 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15ull;
L6690:         for (auto& x : s) {
L6691:             z += 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15ull;
L6692:             uint64_t y = z;
L6693:             y = (y ^ (y >> 30)) * 0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9ull;
L6694:             y = (y ^ (y >> 27)) * 0x94D049BB133111EBull;
L6695:             x = y ^ (y >> 31);
L6696:         }
L6697:     }
L6698:     static uint64_t rotl(uint64_t x, int k) { return (x << k) | (x >> (64 - k)); }
L6699:     uint64_t next() {
L6700:         const uint64_t r = rotl(s[1] * 5, 7) * 9, t = s[1] << 17;
L6701:         s[2] ^= s[0]; s[3] ^= s[1]; s[1] ^= s[2]; s[0] ^= s[3]; s[2] ^= t; s[3] = rotl(s[3], 45);
L6702:         return r;
L6703:     }
L6704:     double uniform() { return (next() >> 11) * (1.0 / 9007199254740992.0); }
L6705:     double gauss() {
L6706:         double u = uniform();
L6707:         while (u <= 0) u = uniform();
L6708:         return std::sqrt(-2.0 * std::log(u)) * std::cos(2 * kPi * uniform());
L6709:     }
L6710: };
L6711: 
L6712: }  // namespace

L6719:     m.t1 = SAMPLE_T1_S;
L6720:     m.t2 = SAMPLE_T2_S;
L6721:     m.proton_density = SAMPLE_PROTON_DENSITY_PER_M3;
L6722:     m.temperature = SAMPLE_TEMPERATURE_K;

L6727:     m.tank_q = RF_COIL_QUALITY_FACTOR;
L6728:     m.rx_gain = RECEIVER_LNA1_GAIN * RECEIVER_LNA2_GAIN * RECEIVER_MIXER_CONVERSION_GAIN * RECEIVER_IF_GAIN;
L6729:     m.if_pole_hz = RECEIVER_IF_BANDWIDTH_HZ;

L6738: SimResult simulate(const pulse::Program& prog, const std::vector<Voxel>& vox, const InstrumentModel& m) {
L6739:     SimResult res;
L6740:     std::vector<pulse::Event> ev;
L6741:     pulse::Diagnostic d;
L6742:     if (!pulse::expand(prog, ev, d)) return res;
L6743:     const size_t nv = vox.size();
L6744:     Rng rng(m.seed);
L6745: 
L6746:     // per-voxel invariants
L6747:     std::vector<double> bmag(nv), m0(nv);
L6748:     for (size_t v = 0; v < nv; ++v) {
L6749:         const Vec3 b = vox[v].b0_hat * (vox[v].b0_per_amp * m.b0_current) + m.earth_field;
L6750:         bmag[v] = b.norm();
L6751:         m0[v] = curie_magnetization(m.proton_density, m.gamma_bar, bmag[v], m.temperature, m.hbar, m.k_b);
L6752:     }
L6753:     std::vector<Vec3> mag(nv, Vec3{0, 0, 1});
L6754:     std::vector<double> df(nv);
L6755:     std::vector<cplx> amp(nv);   // complex weight: emf x tank x IF response, for conj(m) e^{+i phi}
L6756: 
L6757:     double f_tx = 0, rf_phase = 0, rf_amp = 1.0, rx_phase = 0, t_exc = 0;
L6758:     bool tx_on = false;
L6759:     struct Win { bool open = false; uint32_t id = 0; double t0 = 0; };
L6760:     Win win;
L6761:     uint32_t scan = 0;
L6762:     double beat_true = 0, beat_meas = 0;
L6763:     const double tick = prog.tick_hz;
L6764:     const double dt_adc = 1.0 / m.adc_rate;
L6765:     const double lsb = m.adc_full_scale / std::ldexp(1.0, m.adc_bits - 1);
L6766:     const double sigma = m.noise_density * m.rx_gain * std::sqrt(0.5 * kPi * m.if_pole_hz);
L6767:     size_t clipped = 0, total = 0;
L6768: 
L6769:     const auto refresh = [&]() {   // offsets and receive weights depend on f_tx
L6770:         for (size_t v = 0; v < nv; ++v) {
L6771:             df[v] = m.gamma_bar * bmag[v] - f_tx;
L6772:             const double f = f_tx + df[v], omega = 2 * kPi * f;
L6773:             const double emf = omega * vox[v].b1_perp * m0[v] * vox[v].volume;
L6774:             const double r = f / m.tank_f0;
L6775:             const cplx tank = 1.0 / cplx(1.0 - r * r, r / m.tank_q);
L6776:             const double fif = f - m.lo_hz;
L6777:             const cplx ifr = 1.0 / cplx(1.0, fif / m.if_pole_hz);
L6778:             amp[v] = emf * tank * ifr * m.rx_gain * std::polar(1.0, vox[v].b1_phi);
L6779:         }
L6780:     };
L6781: 
L6782:     std::vector<cplx> zt(nv), step(nv);
L6783:     // baseband phasor P(t) = sum_v amp_v conj(m_xy,v(t)) at a time t within a free-precession interval starting at ta
L6784:     const auto precess_all = [&](double dtv) {
L6785:         for (size_t v = 0; v < nv; ++v) mag[v] = precess(mag[v], df[v], dtv, m.t1, m.t2);
L6786:     };
L6787:     const auto sample_interval = [&](double ta, double tb, Record& rec) {
L6788:         // ADC grid points t_n = adc_t0 + n dt in [ta, tb)
L6789:         const double n0 = std::ceil((ta - m.adc_t0) / dt_adc - 1e-9), n1 = std::ceil((tb - m.adc_t0) / dt_adc - 1e-9);
L6790:         if (n1 <= n0) return;
L6791:         const size_t ns = static_cast<size_t>(n1 - n0);
L6792:         const double t_first = m.adc_t0 + n0 * dt_adc;
L6793:         const int k = std::max(1, m.interp);
L6794:         const double dg = k * dt_adc;
L6795:         const size_t ng = (ns + k - 1) / k + 3;   // coarse grid covering the interval, one point before, two after
L6796:         std::vector<cplx> pg(ng);
L6797:         // coarse grid times: t_first + (j-1) dg; state at ta
L6798:         for (size_t v = 0; v < nv; ++v) {
L6799:             const double rate_re = -1.0 / m.t2, rate_im = -2 * kPi * df[v];
L6800:             const cplx lam(rate_re, rate_im);
L6801:             const double tstart = t_first - dg - ta;
L6802:             zt[v] = cplx(mag[v].x, mag[v].y) * std::exp(lam * tstart);
L6803:             step[v] = std::exp(lam * dg);
L6804:         }
L6805:         for (size_t j = 0; j < ng; ++j) {
L6806:             cplx p = 0;
L6807:             for (size_t v = 0; v < nv; ++v) {
L6808:                 p += amp[v] * std::conj(zt[v]);
L6809:                 zt[v] *= step[v];
L6810:             }
L6811:             pg[j] = p;
L6812:             res.emf_peak = std::max(res.emf_peak, std::abs(p));
L6813:         }
L6814:         rec.i.reserve(rec.i.size() + ns);
L6815:         rec.q.reserve(rec.q.size() + ns);
L6816:         for (size_t s = 0; s < ns; ++s) {
L6817:             const double t = t_first + s * dt_adc;
L6818:             // Catmull-Rom between coarse points (exact when k == 1)
L6819:             const double u = static_cast<double>(s) / k + 1.0;
L6820:             const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(u);
L6821:             const double f = u - j;
L6822:             cplx p;
L6823:             if (k == 1) p = pg[j];
L6824:             else {
L6825:                 const cplx p0 = pg[j - 1], p1 = pg[j], p2 = pg[j + 1], p3 = pg[j + 2];
L6826:                 p = 0.5 * ((2.0 * p1) + (p2 - p0) * f + (2.0 * p0 - 5.0 * p1 + 4.0 * p2 - p3) * (f * f) +
L6827:                            (3.0 * p1 - p0 - 3.0 * p2 + p3) * (f * f * f));
L6828:             }
L6829:             const cplx z = p * std::polar(1.0, 2 * kPi * (f_tx - m.lo_hz) * t + beat_true);
L6830:             const double vi = z.real() + m.offset_i + sigma * rng.gauss(), vq = z.imag() + m.offset_q + sigma * rng.gauss();
L6831:             const auto code = [&](double vlt) {
L6832:                 double c = std::round(vlt / lsb);
L6833:                 const double lim = std::ldexp(1.0, m.adc_bits - 1);
L6834:                 if (c > lim - 1 || c < -lim) ++clipped;
L6835:                 return static_cast<int16_t>(std::clamp(c, -lim, lim - 1));
L6836:             };
L6837:             rec.i.push_back(code(vi));
L6838:             rec.q.push_back(code(vq));
L6839:             total += 2;
L6840:         }
L6841:     };
L6842: 
L6843:     double tc = 0;
L6844:     Record* cur = nullptr;
L6845:     for (const auto& e : ev) {
L6846:         const double te = static_cast<double>(e.t) / tick;
L6847:         if (te > tc) {
L6848:             if (tx_on) {
L6849:                 for (size_t v = 0; v < nv; ++v) {
L6850:                     const double b1 = 0.5 * vox[v].b1_perp * m.tx_coil_current * rf_amp;
L6851:                     mag[v] = pulse(mag[v], m.gamma_bar, b1, rf_phase + vox[v].b1_phi, df[v], te - tc);
L6852:                 }
L6853:             } else {
L6854:                 if (win.open && cur) sample_interval(tc, te, *cur);
L6855:                 precess_all(te - tc);
L6856:             }
L6857:             tc = te;
L6858:         }
L6859:         switch (e.op) {
L6860:             case pulse::Op::SET_FREQ: f_tx = e.arg / 1000.0; refresh(); break;
L6861:             case pulse::Op::SET_PHASE: rf_phase = 2 * kPi * e.arg / pulse::kTurn; break;
L6862:             case pulse::Op::SET_AMPLITUDE: rf_amp = static_cast<double>(e.arg) / pulse::kTurn; break;
L6863:             case pulse::Op::TX_GATE_ON: tx_on = true; break;
L6864:             case pulse::Op::TX_GATE_OFF: tx_on = false; break;
L6865:             case pulse::Op::RX_PHASE: rx_phase = static_cast<double>(e.arg) / pulse::kTurn; break;
L6866:             case pulse::Op::MARKER: if (e.arg == pulse::kMarkerExcitation) t_exc = te; break;
L6867:             case pulse::Op::SCAN_BEGIN:
L6868:                 scan = e.arg;
L6869:                 beat_true = m.lo_coherent ? 0.0 : 2 * kPi * rng.uniform();
L6870:                 beat_meas = beat_true + m.beat_measurement_sigma * rng.gauss();
L6871:                 break;
L6872:             case pulse::Op::ACQ_ON: {
L6873:                 win = {true, e.arg, te};
L6874:                 Record r;
L6875:                 r.scan = scan;
L6876:                 r.window = e.arg;
L6877:                 r.t_excitation = t_exc;
L6878:                 r.rx_phase_turns = rx_phase;
L6879:                 r.beat_phase_true = beat_true;
L6880:                 r.beat_phase_measured = beat_meas;
L6881:                 r.f_tx = f_tx;
L6882:                 const double n0 = std::ceil((te - m.adc_t0) / dt_adc - 1e-9);
L6883:                 r.t_first = m.adc_t0 + n0 * dt_adc;
L6884:                 res.records.push_back(std::move(r));
L6885:                 cur = &res.records.back();
L6886:                 break;
L6887:             }
L6888:             case pulse::Op::ACQ_OFF: win.open = false; cur = nullptr; break;
L6889:             default: break;
L6890:         }
L6891:     }
L6892:     res.adc_clip_fraction = total ? static_cast<double>(clipped) / total : 0;
L6893:     return res;
L6894: }

L6914: // The receive-processing chain, one implementation for firmware, host tools and the browser:
L6915: //   ADC codes -> volts -> DC offset removal -> complex IF (I + jQ) -> digital mix to baseband (absolute time, so the
L6916: //   TX-LO beat is accounted for) -> FIR low-pass + decimation -> per-scan phase correction (receiver phase of the
L6917: //   phase cycle + measured beat phase, ADR-0006) -> coherent average -> (window -> FFT -> peak/SNR: dsp.hpp)
L6918: #pragma once
L6919: 
L6920: #include <cstdint>
L6921: #include <vector>
L6922: 
L6923: #include "nmr/dsp.hpp"
L6924: 
L6925: namespace nmr {
L6926: 
L6927: struct RecordView {               // what the hardware (or the simulator) delivers per acquisition window
L6928:     uint32_t scan = 0, window = 0;
L6929:     double t_first = 0;           // [s] time of the first sample, program clock
L6930:     double t_excitation = 0;      // [s] excitation marker time, same clock
L6931:     double rx_phase_turns = 0;    // phase-cycle receiver phase
L6932:     double beat_phase = 0;        // [rad] measured TX-LO beat phase at the scan start (0 if the hardware is coherent)
L6933:     double f_if = 0;              // [Hz] f_tx - f_lo for this record
L6934:     const int16_t* i = nullptr;
L6935:     const int16_t* q = nullptr;
L6936:     size_t n = 0;
L6937: };
L6938: 
L6939: struct PipelineConfig {
L6940:     double adc_rate = 0, adc_full_scale = 0;
L6941:     int adc_bits = 16;
L6942:     size_t decimation = 4, fir_taps = 63;
L6943:     double fir_cutoff_hz = 0;     // 0: 0.4 x output rate
L6944:     double offset_tail = 0.1;     // DC offset from the last fraction of each record (0 = none)
L6945:     bool correct_beat = true;     // apply the measured beat phase
L6946: };
L6947: 
L6948: struct Processed {
L6949:     double fs = 0;                // output sample rate [Hz]
L6950:     double t0 = 0;                // time of output sample 0 after the excitation marker [s]
L6951:     std::vector<dsp::cplx> average;
L6952:     std::vector<std::vector<dsp::cplx>> scans;   // per scan, after phase correction (for diagnostics)
L6953:     size_t n_scans = 0;
L6954:     bool aligned = true;          // all scans had the same sample timing relative to excitation
L6955: };

L6964: #include <cmath>

L6966: namespace nmr {

L6968: Processed process(const std::vector<RecordView>& recs, const PipelineConfig& c) {
L6969:     Processed out;
L6970:     if (recs.empty()) return out;
L6971:     const double lsb = c.adc_full_scale / std::ldexp(1.0, c.adc_bits - 1);
L6972:     const double fs_out = c.adc_rate / static_cast<double>(c.decimation);
L6973:     const double fc = c.fir_cutoff_hz > 0 ? c.fir_cutoff_hz : 0.4 * fs_out;
L6974:     const dsp::FirDecimator fir(fc, c.adc_rate, c.fir_taps, c.decimation);
L6975:     out.fs = fs_out;
L6976:     size_t n_out = 0;
L6977:     std::vector<double> vi, vq;
L6978:     std::vector<dsp::cplx> bb, dec;
L6979:     double t_rel0 = 0;
L6980:     for (size_t r = 0; r < recs.size(); ++r) {
L6981:         const RecordView& rv = recs[r];
L6982:         vi.resize(rv.n);
L6983:         vq.resize(rv.n);
L6984:         for (size_t k = 0; k < rv.n; ++k) { vi[k] = rv.i[k] * lsb; vq[k] = rv.q[k] * lsb; }
L6985:         dsp::cplx off = 0;
L6986:         if (c.offset_tail > 0) {
L6987:             // the IF tone averages out over the tail; what remains is the DC offset of each channel
L6988:             const size_t n0 = rv.n - static_cast<size_t>(rv.n * c.offset_tail);
L6989:             double si = 0, sq = 0;
L6990:             for (size_t k = n0; k < rv.n; ++k) { si += vi[k]; sq += vq[k]; }
L6991:             off = dsp::cplx(si, sq) / static_cast<double>(rv.n - n0);
L6992:         }
L6993:         bb.resize(rv.n);
L6994:         dsp::mix_down(vi.data(), vq.data(), rv.n, off, rv.f_if, c.adc_rate, rv.t_first, bb.data());
L6995:         dec.resize(rv.n / c.decimation + 1);
L6996:         const size_t m = fir.process(bb.data(), rv.n, dec.data(), dec.size());
L6997:         dec.resize(m);
L6998:         const double phase = 2.0 * 3.14159265358979323846 * rv.rx_phase_turns + (c.correct_beat ? rv.beat_phase : 0.0);
L6999:         dsp::rotate(dec.data(), m, phase);
L7000:         const double t_rel = rv.t_first - rv.t_excitation + fir.group_delay_samples() / c.adc_rate;
L7001:         if (r == 0) {
L7002:             t_rel0 = t_rel;
L7003:             n_out = m;
L7004:             out.average.assign(m, dsp::cplx(0));
L7005:         } else {
L7006:             if (std::fabs(t_rel - t_rel0) > 1e-3 / c.adc_rate) out.aligned = false;
L7007:             if (m < n_out) { n_out = m; out.average.resize(m); }
L7008:         }
L7009:         dsp::accumulate(out.average.data(), dec.data(), n_out);
L7010:         out.scans.push_back(dec);
L7011:     }
L7012:     for (auto& v : out.average) v /= static_cast<double>(recs.size());
L7013:     out.n_scans = recs.size();
L7014:     out.t0 = t_rel0;
L7015:     return out;
L7016: }
L7017: 
L7018: }  // namespace nmr

L7208: int32_t nmr_compile(const nmr_timing* t, const nmr_sequence* s, uint8_t* out, int32_t cap) {
L7209:     const pulse::Cycle cyc = s->cyclops ? pulse::Cycle::Cyclops : pulse::Cycle::None;
L7210:     std::vector<pulse::Scan> scans;
L7211:     switch (s->kind) {
L7212:         case 0: scans = pulse::fid(s->t90, s->acq_start, s->acq_len, s->n_avg, cyc); break;
L7213:         case 1: scans = pulse::hahn_echo(s->t90, s->t180, s->tau, s->acq_len, s->n_avg, cyc); break;
L7214:         case 2: scans = pulse::inversion_recovery(s->t90, s->t180, s->ti, s->acq_start, s->acq_len, s->n_avg, cyc); break;
L7215:         case 3: scans = pulse::cpmg(s->t90, s->t180, s->tau, s->n_echoes, s->acq_len, s->n_avg, cyc); break;
L7216:         default: g_err = "unknown sequence kind"; return -1;
L7217:     }
L7218:     pulse::Timing tm{t->tick_hz, t->tx_freq_hz, t->pre_blank_s, t->dead_time_s, t->repetition_s};
L7219:     pulse::Program p;
L7220:     std::vector<pulse::Diagnostic> d;
L7221:     if (!pulse::compile(scans, tm, p, d)) {
L7222:         g_err.clear();
L7223:         for (auto& x : d) g_err += x.message + "\n";
L7224:         return -2;
L7225:     }
L7226:     const auto bytes = pulse::encode(p);
L7227:     if (static_cast<int32_t>(bytes.size()) > cap) { g_err = "output buffer too small"; return -3; }
L7228:     std::memcpy(out, bytes.data(), bytes.size());
L7229:     return static_cast<int32_t>(bytes.size());
L7230: }

L7234:     return true;

L7243:     lim.tx_max_freq_hz = l->tx_max_freq_hz; lim.backend_has_sync_input = l->has_sync_input != 0;
L7244:     const auto d = pulse::validate(p, lim);

L7275: int32_t nmr_voxels(double* o, int32_t cap) {

L7285: void nmr_fields_at(double x, double y, double z, double* b0, double* b1) {
L7286:     static const nmr::ProbeGeometry pg = nmr::design_probe();
L7287:     const nmr::Vec3 a = pg.b0.field_per_amp({x, y, z}, pg.mu0), b = pg.rf.field_per_amp({x, y, z}, pg.mu0);
L7288:     b0[0] = a.x; b0[1] = a.y; b0[2] = a.z;
L7289:     b1[0] = b.x; b1[1] = b.y; b1[2] = b.z;
L7290: }

L7292: int32_t nmr_simulate(const uint8_t* b, int32_t n, const nmr_model* c) {
L7293:     pulse::Program p;
L7294:     if (!load_prog(b, n, p)) return -1;
L7295:     if (g_vox.empty()) { g_err = "no voxels: call nmr_voxelize first"; return -2; }
L7296:     g_sim = nmr::simulate(p, g_vox, to_model(c));
L7297:     g_use_loaded = false;
L7298:     return static_cast<int32_t>(g_sim.records.size());
L7299: }
L7300: 
L7301: int32_t nmr_record(int32_t idx, nmr_record_meta* m, int16_t* i, int16_t* q, int32_t cap) {
L7302:     const auto& rs = records();
L7303:     if (idx < 0 || idx >= static_cast<int32_t>(rs.size())) { g_err = "record index out of range"; return -1; }
L7304:     const auto& r = rs[idx];
L7305:     m->scan = r.scan; m->window = r.window; m->t_first = r.t_first; m->t_excitation = r.t_excitation;
L7306:     m->rx_phase_turns = r.rx_phase_turns; m->beat_phase_true = r.beat_phase_true;
L7307:     m->beat_phase_measured = r.beat_phase_measured; m->f_tx = r.f_tx;
L7308:     m->n = static_cast<int32_t>(r.i.size());
L7309:     if (i && q) {
L7310:         const int32_t k = std::min<int32_t>(cap, m->n);
L7311:         std::memcpy(i, r.i.data(), k * sizeof(int16_t));
L7312:         std::memcpy(q, r.q.data(), k * sizeof(int16_t));
L7313:     }
L7314:     return m->n;
L7315: }

L7318: double nmr_sim_clip_fraction(void) { return g_sim.adc_clip_fraction; }

L7320: void nmr_clear_records(void) { g_loaded.clear(); g_use_loaded = true; }
L7321: 
L7322: int32_t nmr_load_record(const nmr_record_meta* m, const int16_t* i, const int16_t* q) {
L7323:     nmr::Record r;
L7324:     r.scan = m->scan; r.window = m->window; r.t_first = m->t_first; r.t_excitation = m->t_excitation;
L7325:     r.rx_phase_turns = m->rx_phase_turns; r.beat_phase_measured = m->beat_phase_measured; r.f_tx = m->f_tx;
L7326:     r.i.assign(i, i + m->n);
L7327:     r.q.assign(q, q + m->n);
L7328:     g_loaded.push_back(std::move(r));
L7329:     g_use_loaded = true;
L7330:     return static_cast<int32_t>(g_loaded.size());
L7331: }
L7332: 
L7333: void nmr_default_pipeline(nmr_pipeline* p) {
L7334:     using namespace nmr::cfg;
L7335:     std::memset(p, 0, sizeof *p);
L7336:     p->adc_rate = ACQUISITION_RAW_RATE_HZ;
L7337:     p->adc_full_scale = RECEIVER_ADC_FULL_SCALE_V;
L7338:     p->adc_bits = static_cast<int32_t>(RECEIVER_ADC_BITS);
L7339:     p->decimation = static_cast<int32_t>(ACQUISITION_DECIMATION);
L7340:     p->fir_taps = 63;
L7341:     p->offset_tail = 0.1;
L7342:     p->lo_hz = LO_FREQUENCY_HZ;
L7343:     p->correct_beat = 1;
L7344: }
L7345: 
L7346: int32_t nmr_process(const nmr_pipeline* p, double* out, int32_t cap, double* fs, double* t0) {
L7347:     std::vector<nmr::RecordView> v;
L7348:     for (const auto& r : records()) {
L7349:         if (static_cast<int32_t>(r.window) != p->window_id) continue;
L7350:         nmr::RecordView rv;
L7351:         rv.scan = r.scan; rv.window = r.window; rv.t_first = r.t_first; rv.t_excitation = r.t_excitation;
L7352:         rv.rx_phase_turns = r.rx_phase_turns; rv.beat_phase = r.beat_phase_measured; rv.f_if = r.f_tx - p->lo_hz;
L7353:         rv.i = r.i.data(); rv.q = r.q.data(); rv.n = r.i.size();
L7354:         v.push_back(rv);
L7355:     }
L7356:     if (v.empty()) { g_err = "no records for this window"; return -1; }
L7357:     nmr::PipelineConfig c;
L7358:     c.adc_rate = p->adc_rate; c.adc_full_scale = p->adc_full_scale; c.adc_bits = p->adc_bits;
L7359:     c.decimation = static_cast<size_t>(p->decimation); c.fir_taps = static_cast<size_t>(p->fir_taps);
L7360:     c.fir_cutoff_hz = p->fir_cutoff_hz; c.offset_tail = p->offset_tail; c.correct_beat = p->correct_beat != 0;
L7361:     const nmr::Processed pr = nmr::process(v, c);
L7362:     if (!pr.aligned) { g_err = "scans are not sample-aligned relative to excitation"; return -2; }
L7363:     const int32_t n = std::min<int32_t>(cap, static_cast<int32_t>(pr.average.size()));
L7364:     for (int32_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) { out[2 * k] = pr.average[k].real(); out[2 * k + 1] = pr.average[k].imag(); }
L7365:     *fs = pr.fs;
L7366:     *t0 = pr.t0;
L7367:     return n;
L7368: }
L7369: 
L7370: int32_t nmr_spectrum(const double* x, int32_t n, double fs, int32_t w, double tc, int32_t pad, double* freq,
L7371:                      double* val, int32_t cap) {
L7372:     std::vector<nmr::dsp::cplx> z(n);
L7373:     for (int32_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) z[k] = {x[2 * k], x[2 * k + 1]};
L7374:     const auto s = nmr::dsp::spectrum(z.data(), n, fs, static_cast<nmr::dsp::Window>(w), tc, pad);
L7375:     const int32_t m = static_cast<int32_t>(s.value.size());
L7376:     if (m > cap) { g_err = "output buffer too small"; return -1; }
L7377:     for (int32_t k = 0; k < m; ++k) { freq[k] = s.freq_hz[k]; val[2 * k] = s.value[k].real(); val[2 * k + 1] = s.value[k].imag(); }
L7378:     return m;
L7379: }
L7380: 
L7381: int32_t nmr_peak(const double* freq, const double* val, int32_t n, double f_min, double f_max, double guard, double* o) {
L7382:     nmr::dsp::Spectrum s;
L7383:     s.freq_hz.assign(freq, freq + n);
L7384:     s.value.resize(n);
L7385:     for (int32_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) s.value[k] = {val[2 * k], val[2 * k + 1]};
L7386:     const auto p = nmr::dsp::find_peak(s, f_min, f_max, guard);
L7387:     o[0] = p.freq_hz; o[1] = p.amplitude; o[2] = p.noise_rms; o[3] = p.snr;
L7388:     return 0;
L7389: }
L7390: 
L7391: int32_t nmr_fft(double* x, int32_t n, int32_t inverse) {
L7392:     if (n <= 0 || (n & (n - 1))) { g_err = "FFT size must be a power of two"; return -1; }
L7393:     nmr::dsp::FftPlan(static_cast<size_t>(n)).execute(reinterpret_cast<nmr::dsp::cplx*>(x), inverse != 0);
L7394:     return 0;
L7395: }

L7448: // Minimal test harness: every check prints the model quantity, expected, actual and tolerance on failure.
L7449: #pragma once
L7450: 
L7451: #include <cmath>
L7452: #include <cstdio>
L7453: #include <string>
L7454: 
L7455: inline int g_fail = 0, g_pass = 0;
L7456: 
L7457: #define CHECK(cond, what)                                                                   \
L7458:     do {                                                                                    \
L7459:         if (cond) ++g_pass;                                                                 \
L7460:         else { ++g_fail; std::printf("FAIL %s:%d  %s\n", __FILE__, __LINE__, what); }      \
L7461:     } while (0)
L7462: 
L7463: // |actual - expected| <= abs_tol + rel_tol * |expected|
L7464: #define CHECK_NEAR(actual, expected, abs_tol, rel_tol, what)                                                     \
L7465:     do {                                                                                                         \
L7466:         const double a_ = (actual), e_ = (expected);                                                             \
L7467:         const double tol_ = (abs_tol) + (rel_tol) * std::fabs(e_);                                               \
L7468:         if (std::fabs(a_ - e_) <= tol_ && std::isfinite(a_)) ++g_pass;                                           \
L7469:         else { ++g_fail; std::printf("FAIL %s:%d  %s: expected %.10g, actual %.10g, tolerance %.3g\n", __FILE__, \
L7470:                                      __LINE__, what, e_, a_, tol_); }                                            \
L7471:     } while (0)
L7472: 
L7473: inline int finish(const char* name) {
L7474:     std::printf("%s: %d passed, %d failed\n", name, g_pass, g_fail);
L7475:     return g_fail ? 1 : 0;
L7476: }

L7479: // Bloch dynamics against analytic results and an independent RK4 integration of the Bloch equation.
L7480: #include <random>
L7481: 
L7482: #include "check.hpp"
L7483: #include "instrument_config.hpp"
L7484: #include "nmr/bloch.hpp"
L7485: 
L7486: using namespace nmr;
L7487: static const double G = cfg::NUCLEUS_GAMMA_BAR_HZ_PER_T;   // 1H, from the canonical config
L7488: static const double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
L7489: 
L7490: // Independent reference: dM/dt = 2 pi G M x B_eff, B_eff = (B1 cos phi, B1 sin phi, df / G), RK4.
L7491: static Vec3 rk4(Vec3 m, double b1, double phi, double df, double t, int steps) {
L7492:     const Vec3 b{b1 * std::cos(phi), b1 * std::sin(phi), df / G};
L7493:     const auto f = [&](Vec3 x) { return x.cross(b) * (2 * PI * G); };
L7494:     const double h = t / steps;
L7495:     for (int i = 0; i < steps; ++i) {
L7496:         const Vec3 k1 = f(m), k2 = f(m + k1 * (h / 2)), k3 = f(m + k2 * (h / 2)), k4 = f(m + k3 * h);
L7497:         m = m + (k1 + k2 * 2 + k3 * 2 + k4) * (h / 6);
L7498:     }
L7499:     return m;
L7500: }
L7501: 
L7502: int main() {
L7503:     const Vec3 z{0, 0, 1};
L7504:     // equilibrium is a fixed point of free precession with relaxation
L7505:     {
L7506:         const Vec3 m = precess(z, 123.0, 0.37, 2.5, 2.0);
L7507:         CHECK_NEAR(m.x, 0, 1e-15, 0, "equilibrium Mx"); CHECK_NEAR(m.y, 0, 1e-15, 0, "equilibrium My");
L7508:         CHECK_NEAR(m.z, 1, 1e-15, 0, "equilibrium Mz");
L7509:     }
L7510:     // ideal on-resonance 90 and 180 degree pulses (phi = 0): rotation about -x' takes +z to +y', then to -z

L7615:     // loop on its axis: B = mu0 I a^2 / (2 (a^2 + z^2)^1.5)
L7616:     {

L7673: // DSP primitives: FFT against the O(N^2) reference, tone location/amplitude, FIR response, mixing, SNR.
L7674: #include <random>

L7683: int main() {
L7684:     std::mt19937_64 rng(3);
L7685:     std::normal_distribution<double> g(0, 1);
L7686:     // FFT == DFT for random data; inverse round trip
L7687:     for (size_t n : {8u, 64u, 1024u}) {
L7688:         std::vector<cplx> x(n), a(n), b(n);
L7689:         for (auto& v : x) v = {g(rng), g(rng)};
L7690:         a = x;
L7691:         FftPlan(n).execute(a.data());
L7692:         dft_reference(x.data(), b.data(), n);
L7693:         double err = 0;
L7694:         for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) err = std::max(err, std::abs(a[k] - b[k]));
L7695:         CHECK_NEAR(err, 0, 1e-10 * std::sqrt(static_cast<double>(n)), 0, "FFT vs DFT reference (max abs error)");
L7696:         FftPlan(n).execute(a.data(), true);
L7697:         double rt = 0;
L7698:         for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) rt = std::max(rt, std::abs(a[k] / static_cast<double>(n) - x[k]));
L7699:         CHECK_NEAR(rt, 0, 1e-12, 0, "FFT inverse round trip");
L7700:     }
L7701:     // a complex tone: peak at its frequency (parabolic interpolation), |S| = amplitude with the rect window on-bin
L7702:     {

L7704:         const size_t n = 4096;

L7710:         CHECK_NEAR(p.amplitude, A, 0, 0.02, "tone amplitude |S| (Hann, scaled by sum(w))");
L7711:         // negative frequency maps to the negative half of the shifted axis

L7714:         CHECK_NEAR(q.freq_hz, -f, 0.5, 0, "negative-frequency tone");
L7715:     }
L7716:     // FIR: unity DC gain, passband flat to 0.1 dB at 0.25 fs_out, stopband below -60 dB beyond fs_out/2 + transition
L7717:     {
L7718:         const double fs = 100000;
L7719:         const FirDecimator fir(0.4 * 25000, fs, 63, 4);
L7720:         double sum = 0;
L7721:         for (double h : fir.taps()) sum += h;
L7722:         CHECK_NEAR(sum, 1.0, 1e-12, 0, "FIR DC gain");

L7724:             cplx r = 0;
L7725:             for (size_t k = 0; k < fir.taps().size(); ++k) r += fir.taps()[k] * std::polar(1.0, -2 * PI * f * k / fs);
L7726:             return std::abs(r);
L7727:         };

L7729:         CHECK(20 * std::log10(resp(30000)) < -60, "FIR stopband at 30 kHz below -60 dB");
L7730:     }
L7731:     // mix_down moves a tone at f_if (absolute time origin t0) to DC with the right phase
L7732:     {

L7915: static const double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;

L7917: static pulse::Program compile_or_die(const std::vector<pulse::Scan>& s, double f_tx) {
L7918:     pulse::Timing tm{TIMING_TICK_HZ, f_tx, TIMING_PRE_BLANK_S, RECEIVER_DEAD_TIME_S, ACQUISITION_REPETITION_TIME_S};
L7919:     pulse::Program p;
L7920:     std::vector<pulse::Diagnostic> d;
L7921:     if (!pulse::compile(s, tm, p, d)) { std::printf("compile failed: %s\n", d[0].message.c_str()); std::exit(2); }
L7922:     return p;
L7923: }
L7924: 
L7925: static Processed run_pipeline(const SimResult& r, const InstrumentModel& m, bool correct_beat, uint32_t window = 0) {
L7926:     std::vector<RecordView> v;
L7927:     for (const auto& x : r.records) {
L7928:         if (x.window != window) continue;
L7929:         RecordView rv;
L7930:         rv.scan = x.scan; rv.window = x.window; rv.t_first = x.t_first; rv.t_excitation = x.t_excitation;
L7931:         rv.rx_phase_turns = x.rx_phase_turns; rv.beat_phase = x.beat_phase_measured; rv.f_if = x.f_tx - m.lo_hz;
L7932:         rv.i = x.i.data(); rv.q = x.q.data(); rv.n = x.i.size();
L7933:         v.push_back(rv);
L7934:     }
L7935:     PipelineConfig c;
L7936:     c.adc_rate = m.adc_rate; c.adc_full_scale = m.adc_full_scale; c.adc_bits = m.adc_bits;
L7937:     c.decimation = static_cast<size_t>(ACQUISITION_DECIMATION); c.fir_taps = 63; c.offset_tail = 0; c.correct_beat = correct_beat;
L7938:     return process(v, c);
L7939: }

L7941: int main() {
L7942:     const ProbeGeometry pg = design_probe();
L7943:     const Vec3 b0c = pg.b0.field_per_amp({0, 0, 0}, pg.mu0), b1c = pg.rf.field_per_amp({0, 0, 0}, pg.mu0);
L7944:     InstrumentModel m = design_model();
L7945:     m.noise_density = 0;
L7946:     const double t90 = TX_T90_CALCULATED_S;   // exact pi/2 at the coil centre
L7947: 
L7948:     // ---- one voxel at the centre, 100 mL: amplitude, frequency sign, T2 decay
L7949:     Voxel v0;
L7950:     v0.p = {0, 0, 0}; v0.volume = 1e-4; v0.b0_per_amp = b0c.norm(); v0.b0_hat = b0c * (1 / b0c.norm());
L7951:     transverse(b0c, b1c, v0.b1_perp, v0.b1_phi);
L7952:     const double f_line = m.gamma_bar * v0.b0_per_amp * m.b0_current;
L7953:     {
L7954:         const double offset = 37.0;            // transmit 37 Hz below the line
L7955:         InstrumentModel mm = m;
L7956:         mm.tank_f0 = f_line;
L7957:         mm.t2 = 0.05;
L7958:         const auto prog = compile_or_die(pulse::fid(t90, ACQUISITION_START_DELAY_S, 0.2, 1, pulse::Cycle::None), f_line - offset);
L7959:         const SimResult r = simulate(prog, {v0}, mm);
L7960:         CHECK(r.records.size() == 1, "one record");
L7961:         const Processed p = run_pipeline(r, mm, true);
L7962:         // expected amplitude at the ADC, from the model equations (docs/physics.md)
L7963:         const double m0 = curie_magnetization(mm.proton_density, mm.gamma_bar, v0.b0_per_amp * mm.b0_current, mm.temperature, mm.hbar, mm.k_b);
L7964:         const double emf = 2 * PI * f_line * v0.b1_perp * m0 * v0.volume;
L7965:         const double rr = f_line / mm.tank_f0;
L7966:         const double tank = 1.0 / std::abs(std::complex<double>(1 - rr * rr, rr / mm.tank_q));
L7967:         const double ifr = 1.0 / std::abs(std::complex<double>(1, (f_line - mm.lo_hz) / mm.if_pole_hz));
L7968:         const double expect = emf * tank * ifr * mm.rx_gain;
L7969:         // the flip is exactly 90 degrees only on resonance; at 37 Hz offset the transverse magnitude is ~1 - O(1e-3)
L7970:         const double a0 = std::abs(p.average[0]) * std::exp(p.t0 / mm.t2);
L7971:         CHECK_NEAR(a0, expect, 0, 0.01, "single-voxel FID amplitude at the ADC [V] (model equation)");
L7972:         const double a1 = std::abs(p.average[2000]) * std::exp((p.t0 + 2000 / p.fs) / mm.t2);
L7973:         CHECK_NEAR(a1, a0, 0, 0.005, "T2 decay: amplitude * exp(t/T2) constant");
L7974:         const auto s = dsp::spectrum(p.average.data(), p.average.size(), p.fs, dsp::Window::Rect, 0, 8);
L7975:         const auto pk = dsp::find_peak(s, -200, 200, 10);
L7976:         CHECK_NEAR(pk.freq_hz, offset, 0.2, 0, "line 37 Hz above f_tx appears at +37 Hz (I/Q sign convention)");
L7977:         CHECK(r.adc_clip_fraction == 0, "no ADC clipping");
L7978:     }
L7979:     // ---- CYCLOPS averages coherently; an unsynchronised LO destroys the average; the measured beat phase restores it
L7980:     {
L7981:         InstrumentModel mm = m;
L7982:         mm.tank_f0 = f_line;
L7983:         const auto one = compile_or_die(pulse::fid(t90, ACQUISITION_START_DELAY_S, 0.1, 1, pulse::Cycle::None), f_line);
L7984:         const auto eight = compile_or_die(pulse::fid(t90, ACQUISITION_START_DELAY_S, 0.1, 8, pulse::Cycle::Cyclops), f_line);
L7985:         const double a1 = std::abs(run_pipeline(simulate(one, {v0}, mm), mm, true).average[10]);
L7986:         const double a8 = std::abs(run_pipeline(simulate(eight, {v0}, mm), mm, true).average[10]);
L7987:         CHECK_NEAR(a8, a1, 0, 0.005, "CYCLOPS x8 average = single scan (coherent LO)");
L7988:         mm.lo_coherent = false;
L7989:         mm.seed = 11;
L7990:         const SimResult inc = simulate(eight, {v0}, mm);
L7991:         const double raw = std::abs(run_pipeline(inc, mm, false).average[10]);
L7992:         const double fixed = std::abs(run_pipeline(inc, mm, true).average[10]);
L7993:         CHECK(raw < 0.7 * a1, "random TX-LO beat phase per scan: uncorrected average collapses");
L7994:         CHECK_NEAR(fixed, a1, 0, 0.005, "measured beat phase restores the coherent average");
L7995:         mm.beat_measurement_sigma = 0.1;   // 5.7 degrees rms measurement error
L7996:         const double noisy = std::abs(run_pipeline(simulate(eight, {v0}, mm), mm, true).average[10]);
L7997:         CHECK_NEAR(noisy, a1 * std::exp(-0.005), 0, 0.01, "beat measurement error sigma costs exp(-sigma^2/2)");
L7998:     }
L7999:     // ---- design sample: reference path vs fast path, line position, noise level
L8000:     {
L8001:         const std::vector<Voxel> vox = voxelize(pg, design_sample(), 0.005);
L8002:         InstrumentModel mm = m;
L8003:         const auto prog = compile_or_die(pulse::fid(t90, ACQUISITION_START_DELAY_S, 0.05, 1, pulse::Cycle::None), mm.tank_f0);
L8004:         mm.interp = 1;
L8005:         const SimResult ref = simulate(prog, vox, mm);
L8006:         mm.interp = 8;
L8007:         const SimResult fast = simulate(prog, vox, mm);
L8008:         int worst = 0;
L8009:         for (size_t k = 0; k < ref.records[0].i.size(); ++k)
L8010:             worst = std::max({worst, std::abs(ref.records[0].i[k] - fast.records[0].i[k]), std::abs(ref.records[0].q[k] - fast.records[0].q[k])});
L8011:         CHECK(worst <= 2, "fast path (interp 8) within 2 LSB of the reference path");
L8012:         std::printf("  fast vs reference: worst difference %d LSB\n", worst);
L8013:         // line: the voxels' Larmor frequencies relative to f_tx, weighted median vs the spectrum peak
L8014:         const Processed p = run_pipeline(fast, mm, true);
L8015:         const auto s = dsp::spectrum(p.average.data(), p.average.size(), p.fs, dsp::Window::Rect, 0, 8);
L8016:         const auto pk = dsp::find_peak(s, -400, 400, 10);
L8017:         CHECK_NEAR(pk.freq_hz, m.gamma_bar * b0c.norm() * m.b0_current - mm.tank_f0, 25, 0, "design-sample line near the centre Larmor offset");
L8018:         std::printf("  design sample: line at %+.1f Hz from f_tx (centre voxel %+.1f Hz)\n", pk.freq_hz, m.gamma_bar * b0c.norm() * m.b0_current - mm.tank_f0);
L8019:     }
L8020:     {
L8021:         InstrumentModel mm = design_model();
L8022:         mm.proton_density = 0;   // no signal: noise only
L8023:         const auto prog = compile_or_die(pulse::fid(t90, ACQUISITION_START_DELAY_S, 0.2, 1, pulse::Cycle::None), mm.tank_f0);
L8024:         const SimResult r = simulate(prog, {v0}, mm);
L8025:         double s2 = 0;
L8026:         for (auto c : r.records[0].i) s2 += code_to_volts(c, mm) * code_to_volts(c, mm);
L8027:         const double sd = std::sqrt(s2 / r.records[0].i.size());

L8065: static double time_ms(const std::function<void()>& f, int reps) {
L8066:     std::vector<double> t;
L8067:     for (int i = 0; i < reps; ++i) {
L8068:         const auto a = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
L8069:         f();
L8070:         t.push_back(std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - a).count());
L8071:     }
L8072:     std::sort(t.begin(), t.end());
L8073:     return t[t.size() / 2];
L8074: }

L8352:         return self.L.nmr_last_error().decode()

L8355:         return self.L.nmr_config_sha256().decode()

L8357:     # ---- programs
L8358:     def default_timing(self) -> Timing:
L8359:         t = Timing(); self.L.nmr_default_timing(C.byref(t)); return t
L8360: 
L8361:     def default_sequence(self, kind: int) -> Sequence:
L8362:         s = Sequence(); self.L.nmr_default_sequence(C.byref(s), kind); return s
L8363: 
L8364:     def default_limits(self) -> Limits:
L8365:         lim = Limits(); self.L.nmr_default_limits(C.byref(lim)); return lim
L8366: 
L8367:     def compile_sequence(self, kind: int = 0, timing: Timing | None = None, **seq) -> bytes:
L8368:         s = self.default_sequence(kind)
L8369:         for k, v in seq.items():
L8370:             setattr(s, k, v)
L8371:         t = timing or self.default_timing()
L8372:         buf = (C.c_uint8 * (8 * 8192 + 64))()
L8373:         n = self.L.nmr_compile(C.byref(t), C.byref(s), buf, len(buf))
L8374:         if n < 0:
L8375:             raise ValueError(self.err())
L8376:         return bytes(buf[:n])
L8377: 
L8378:     def validate(self, prog: bytes, limits: Limits | None = None) -> list[str]:
L8379:         lim = limits or self.default_limits()
L8380:         n = self.L.nmr_validate(prog, len(prog), C.byref(lim))
L8381:         if n < 0:
L8382:             raise ValueError(self.err())
L8383:         return [x for x in self.err().splitlines() if x]
L8384: 
L8385:     def disassemble(self, prog: bytes) -> str:
L8386:         buf = C.create_string_buffer(1 << 20)
L8387:         if self.L.nmr_disassemble(prog, len(prog), buf, len(buf)) < 0:
L8388:             raise ValueError(self.err())
L8389:         return buf.value.decode()
L8390: 
L8391:     # ---- fields and voxels
L8392:     def fields_at(self, x, y, z):
L8393:         b0 = (C.c_double * 3)(); b1 = (C.c_double * 3)()
L8394:         self.L.nmr_fields_at(x, y, z, b0, b1)
L8395:         return np.array(b0[:]), np.array(b1[:])
L8396: 
L8397:     def voxelize(self, grid_m: float) -> np.ndarray:
L8398:         n = self.L.nmr_voxelize(grid_m)
L8399:         out = np.zeros((n, 7))
L8400:         self.L.nmr_voxels(out.ctypes.data, n)
L8401:         return out   # x, y, z, volume, |B0|/I, |B1perp|/I, phi
L8402: 
L8403:     # ---- simulation and processing
L8404:     def default_model(self) -> Model:
L8405:         m = Model(); self.L.nmr_default_model(C.byref(m)); return m
L8406: 
L8407:     def simulate(self, prog: bytes, model: Model | None = None) -> list[dict]:
L8408:         m = model or self.default_model()
L8409:         n = self.L.nmr_simulate(prog, len(prog), C.byref(m))
L8410:         if n < 0:
L8411:             raise ValueError(self.err())
L8412:         return [self.record(k) for k in range(n)]
L8413: 
L8414:     def record(self, k: int) -> dict:
L8415:         meta = RecordMeta()
L8416:         n = self.L.nmr_record(k, C.byref(meta), None, None, 0)
L8417:         i = np.zeros(n, np.int16); q = np.zeros(n, np.int16)
L8418:         self.L.nmr_record(k, C.byref(meta), i.ctypes.data, q.ctypes.data, n)
L8419:         d = {f: getattr(meta, f) for f, _ in RecordMeta._fields_}
L8420:         d["i"], d["q"] = i, q
L8421:         return d
L8422: 
L8423:     def load_records(self, recs: list[dict]) -> None:
L8424:         self.L.nmr_clear_records()
L8425:         for r in recs:
L8426:             meta = RecordMeta(**{f: r[f] for f, _ in RecordMeta._fields_ if f in r and f != "n"})
L8427:             meta.n = len(r["i"])
L8428:             i = np.ascontiguousarray(r["i"], np.int16); q = np.ascontiguousarray(r["q"], np.int16)
L8429:             self.L.nmr_load_record(C.byref(meta), i.ctypes.data, q.ctypes.data)
L8430: 
L8431:     def default_pipeline(self) -> Pipeline:
L8432:         p = Pipeline(); self.L.nmr_default_pipeline(C.byref(p)); return p
L8433: 
L8434:     def process(self, pipeline: Pipeline | None = None, window: int = 0):
L8435:         p = pipeline or self.default_pipeline()
L8436:         p.window_id = window
L8437:         cap = 1 << 22
L8438:         out = np.zeros(2 * cap)
L8439:         fs = C.c_double(); t0 = C.c_double()
L8440:         n = self.L.nmr_process(C.byref(p), out.ctypes.data, cap, C.byref(fs), C.byref(t0))
L8441:         if n < 0:
L8442:             raise ValueError(self.err())
L8443:         z = out[:2 * n:2] + 1j * out[1:2 * n:2]
L8444:         return z, fs.value, t0.value
L8445: 
L8446:     def spectrum(self, z: np.ndarray, fs: float, window: int = 0, tc: float = 0.0, pad: int = 2):
L8447:         x = np.empty(2 * len(z)); x[0::2] = z.real; x[1::2] = z.imag
L8448:         cap = 1 << 23
L8449:         f = np.zeros(cap); v = np.zeros(2 * cap)
L8450:         n = self.L.nmr_spectrum(x.ctypes.data, len(z), fs, window, tc, pad, f.ctypes.data, v.ctypes.data, cap)
L8451:         if n < 0:
L8452:             raise ValueError(self.err())
L8453:         return f[:n], v[:2 * n:2] + 1j * v[1:2 * n:2]
L8454: 
L8455:     def peak(self, f: np.ndarray, s: np.ndarray, f_min: float, f_max: float, guard: float) -> dict:
L8456:         v = np.empty(2 * len(s)); v[0::2] = s.real; v[1::2] = s.imag
L8457:         o = np.zeros(4)
L8458:         self.L.nmr_peak(np.ascontiguousarray(f).ctypes.data, v.ctypes.data, len(s), f_min, f_max, guard, o.ctypes.data)
L8459:         return dict(zip(("freq_hz", "amplitude", "noise_rms", "snr"), o))

L8479: core = nmrpy.core
L8480: LEG = json.loads((GOLD / "legacy-constants.json").read_text())

L8482: 
L8483: def transverse(b0, b1):
L8484:     """the legacy frame: x' = coil axis (x) projected perpendicular to B0, y' = B0_hat x x'"""
L8485:     n = b0 / np.linalg.norm(b0)
L8486:     ex = np.array([1.0, 0, 0]) - n[0] * n
L8487:     ex /= np.linalg.norm(ex)
L8488:     ey = np.cross(n, ex)
L8489:     bp = b1 - np.dot(b1, n) * n
L8490:     return np.linalg.norm(bp), np.arctan2(np.dot(bp, ey), np.dot(bp, ex))
L8491: 
L8492: 
L8493: @pytest.mark.parametrize("sample", ["tube", "bottle"])
L8494: def test_fieldmap_every_voxel(sample):
L8495:     d = json.loads((GOLD / f"fieldmap-{sample}.json").read_text())
L8496:     p = np.array(d["p"]) / 1000.0
L8497:     df_err = b1_err = phi_err = 0.0
L8498:     for k in range(len(p)):
L8499:         b0, b1 = core.fields_at(*p[k])
L8500:         df = LEG["gamma_bar_hz_per_t"] * np.linalg.norm(b0) * LEG["b0_current_a"] - LEG["f_tx_hz"]
L8501:         b1p, phi = transverse(b0, b1)
L8502:         df_err = max(df_err, abs(df - d["df"][k]))
L8503:         b1_err = max(b1_err, abs(b1p / d["b1"][k] - 1))
L8504:         phi_err = max(phi_err, abs(phi - d["phi"][k]))
L8505:     # tolerances: the golden data's own rounding (df 1 mHz, b1 6 s.f., phi 1e-4 rad) plus 1 unit
L8506:     print(f"{sample}: {len(p)} voxels; max |d df| {df_err:.2e} Hz, max rel b1 {b1_err:.2e}, max |d phi| {phi_err:.2e}")
L8507:     assert df_err <= 2e-3
L8508:     assert b1_err <= 1e-5
L8509:     assert phi_err <= 1.5e-4
L8510:     b0c, b1c = core.fields_at(0, 0, 0)
L8511:     assert np.linalg.norm(b0c) * 1.5 * 1e3 == pytest.approx(d["B0_centre_mT_at_1.5A"], rel=1e-9)
L8512:     assert b1c[0] == pytest.approx(d["centre"]["b1"], rel=1e-9)
L8513:

L8520:     assert abs(v[:, 1].mean()) < 1e-12 and abs(v[:, 2].mean()) < 1e-12, "grid centred on the sample axis"

L8534: The field code matches the legacy Mathematica map exactly across all 8065 voxels, but the volume check is off by 2.4% due to staircasing at the curved surface from coarse grid resolution. I'll fix this by weighting boundary voxels using 4×4×4 sub-samples to better approximate their fraction inside the sample.
L8535: Bash Weight boundary voxels by volume fraction, retest
L8536: IN
L8537: py - <<'EOF'
L8538: p='physics/src/probe.cpp'; s=open(p,encoding='utf-8').read()
L8539: a='''                const Vec3 p{x, (iy + 0.5) * g, (iz + 0.5) * g};
L8540:                 if (!sg.inside(p)) continue;'''
L8541: b='''                const Vec3 p{x, (iy + 0.5) * g, (iz + 0.5) * g};
L8542:                 // boundary voxels carry the fraction of their volume inside the sample (4 x 4 x 4 sub-samples),
L8543:                 // so the curved surface is not a staircase (whole-voxel counting is 2.4 % low at a 2.5 mm pitch)
L8544:                 int in = 0;
L8545:                 for (int a = 0; a < 4; ++a)
L8546:                     for (int b = 0; b < 4; ++b)
L8547:                         for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
L8548:                             in += sg.inside({p.x + (a - 1.5) * g / 4, p.y + (b - 1.5) * g / 4, p.z + (c - 1.5) * g / 4});
L8549:                 if (in == 0) continue;'''
L8550: assert a in s; s=s.replace(a,b,1)
L8551: a2='''                v.volume = g * g * g;'''
L8552: b2='''                v.volume = g * g * g * in / 64.0;'''
L8553: assert a2 in s; s=s.replace(a2,b2,1)
L8554: open(p,'w',encoding='utf-8',newline='\n').write(s)
L8555: EOF

L8802: // JavaScript access to the C++ NMR core compiled to WebAssembly (simulator/wasm/build.py).
L8803: // Thin marshalling only: every number is computed by the same C++ the native tests and the Python tools use.
L8804: // Works in the browser and in Node (tests/wasm_parity.mjs).

L8806: const LAYOUT = {          // byte sizes, checked against nmr_abi_layout() at load time
L8807:   timing: 40, sequence: 72, limits: 64, model: 200, record: 64, pipeline: 64,
L8808: };
L8809: const F = {               // field offsets (wasm32: double 8-aligned)
L8810:   timing: { tick_hz: 0, tx_freq_hz: 8, pre_blank_s: 16, dead_time_s: 24, repetition_s: 32 },
L8811:   sequence: { kind: [0, 'i32'], t90: 8, t180: 16, tau: 24, ti: 32, acq_start: 40, acq_len: 48,
L8812:               n_echoes: [56, 'i32'], n_avg: [60, 'i32'], cyclops: [64, 'i32'] },
L8813:   model: { gamma_bar: 0, b0_current: 8, t1: 16, t2: 24, proton_density: 32, temperature: 40, earth_x: 48,
L8814:            earth_y: 56, earth_z: 64, tx_coil_current: 72, tank_f0: 80, tank_q: 88, rx_gain: 96, if_pole_hz: 104,
L8815:            noise_density: 112, lo_hz: 120, adc_full_scale: 128, adc_rate: 136, adc_t0: 144, offset_i: 152,
L8816:            offset_q: 160, adc_bits: [168, 'i32'], lo_coherent: [172, 'i32'], interp: [176, 'i32'],
L8817:            beat_measurement_sigma: 184, seed: [192, 'u64'] },
L8818:   record: { scan: [0, 'u32'], window: [4, 'u32'], t_first: 8, t_excitation: 16, rx_phase_turns: 24,
L8819:             beat_phase_true: 32, beat_phase_measured: 40, f_tx: 48, n: [56, 'i32'] },
L8820:   pipeline: { adc_rate: 0, adc_full_scale: 8, fir_cutoff_hz: 16, offset_tail: 24, lo_hz: 32, adc_bits: [40, 'i32'],
L8821:               decimation: [44, 'i32'], fir_taps: [48, 'i32'], correct_beat: [52, 'i32'], window_id: [56, 'i32'] },
