L8823: 
L8824: function wasiStubs(getMem, log) {
L8825:   const ENOSYS = 52, EBADF = 8;
L8826:   return {
L8827:     environ_sizes_get: (pc, ps) => { const v = new DataView(getMem().buffer); v.setUint32(pc, 0, true); v.setUint32(ps, 0, true); return 0; },
L8828:     environ_get: () => 0,
L8829:     fd_prestat_get: () => EBADF,
L8830:     fd_prestat_dir_name: () => EBADF,
L8831:     fd_close: () => 0,
L8832:     fd_seek: () => ENOSYS,
L8833:     fd_write: (fd, iovs, n, pw) => {
L8834:       const v = new DataView(getMem().buffer); let w = 0, s = '';
L8835:       for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
L8836:         const p = v.getUint32(iovs + 8 * k, true), l = v.getUint32(iovs + 8 * k + 4, true);
L8837:         s += new TextDecoder().decode(new Uint8Array(getMem().buffer, p, l)); w += l;
L8838:       }
L8839:       v.setUint32(pw, w, true); log(s); return 0;
L8840:     },
L8841:     proc_exit: (c) => { throw new Error(`nmrcore exited with ${c}`); },
L8842:   };
L8843: }
L8844: 
L8845: export async function loadCore(source, { log = () => {} } = {}) {
L8846:   let mem;
L8847:   const imports = { wasi_snapshot_preview1: wasiStubs(() => mem, log) };
L8848:   const bytes = source instanceof ArrayBuffer || ArrayBuffer.isView(source) ? source : await (await fetch(source)).arrayBuffer();
L8849:   const { instance } = await WebAssembly.instantiate(bytes, imports);
L8850:   const X = instance.exports;
L8851:   mem = X.memory;
L8852:   X._initialize();
L8853:   return new Core(X);
L8854: }
L8855: 
L8856: class Core {
L8857:   constructor(X) {
L8858:     this.X = X;
L8859:     const p = X.malloc(24);
L8860:     X.nmr_abi_layout(p);
L8861:     const s = new Int32Array(X.memory.buffer, p, 6);
L8862:     const want = [LAYOUT.timing, LAYOUT.sequence, LAYOUT.limits, LAYOUT.model, LAYOUT.record, LAYOUT.pipeline];
L8863:     if (want.some((w, k) => w !== s[k])) throw new Error(`ABI layout mismatch: wasm ${[...s]} vs js ${want}`);
L8864:     X.free(p);
L8865:   }
L8866:   get dv() { return new DataView(this.X.memory.buffer); }
L8867:   str(p) { const b = new Uint8Array(this.X.memory.buffer, p); let n = 0; while (b[n]) n++; return new TextDecoder().decode(b.subarray(0, n)); }
L8868:   error() { return this.str(this.X.nmr_last_error()); }
L8869:   configSha256() { return this.str(this.X.nmr_config_sha256()); }
L8870: 
L8871:   // struct <-> object
L8872:   read(kind, p) {
L8873:     const o = {}, v = this.dv;
L8874:     for (const [k, d] of Object.entries(F[kind])) {
L8875:       const [off, t] = Array.isArray(d) ? d : [d, 'f64'];
L8876:       o[k] = t === 'i32' ? v.getInt32(p + off, true) : t === 'u32' ? v.getUint32(p + off, true)
L8877:         : t === 'u64' ? Number(v.getBigUint64(p + off, true)) : v.getFloat64(p + off, true);
L8878:     }
L8879:     return o;
L8880:   }
L8881:   write(kind, p, o) {
L8882:     const v = this.dv;
L8883:     for (const [k, d] of Object.entries(F[kind])) {
L8884:       if (!(k in o)) continue;
L8885:       const [off, t] = Array.isArray(d) ? d : [d, 'f64'];
L8886:       if (t === 'i32') v.setInt32(p + off, o[k], true);
L8887:       else if (t === 'u32') v.setUint32(p + off, o[k], true);
L8888:       else if (t === 'u64') v.setBigUint64(p + off, BigInt(o[k]), true);
L8889:       else v.setFloat64(p + off, o[k], true);
L8890:     }
L8891:   }
L8892:   withStruct(kind, init, fn) {
L8893:     const p = this.X.malloc(LAYOUT[kind]);
L8894:     try { new Uint8Array(this.X.memory.buffer, p, LAYOUT[kind]).fill(0); init(p); return fn(p); } finally { this.X.free(p); }
L8895:   }
L8896:   defaults(kind, fnName, arg) {
L8897:     return this.withStruct(kind, (p) => (arg === undefined ? this.X[fnName](p) : this.X[fnName](p, arg)), (p) => this.read(kind, p));
L8898:   }
L8899:   defaultTiming() { return this.defaults('timing', 'nmr_default_timing'); }
L8900:   defaultSequence(kind) { return this.defaults('sequence', 'nmr_default_sequence', kind); }
L8901:   defaultModel() { return this.defaults('model', 'nmr_default_model'); }
L8902:   defaultPipeline() { return this.defaults('pipeline', 'nmr_default_pipeline'); }
L8903: 
L8904:   compile(sequence, timing = this.defaultTiming()) {
L8905:     const X = this.X, cap = 8 * 8192 + 64, out = X.malloc(cap);
L8906:     try {
L8907:       const n = this.withStruct('timing', (pt) => this.write('timing', pt, timing),
L8908:         (pt) => this.withStruct('sequence', (ps) => this.write('sequence', ps, sequence), (ps) => X.nmr_compile(pt, ps, out, cap)));
L8909:       if (n < 0) throw new Error(this.error());
L8910:       return new Uint8Array(X.memory.buffer, out, n).slice();
L8911:     } finally { X.free(out); }
L8912:   }
L8913:   withBytes(bytes, fn) {
L8914:     const p = this.X.malloc(bytes.length);
L8915:     try { new Uint8Array(this.X.memory.buffer, p, bytes.length).set(bytes); return fn(p); } finally { this.X.free(p); }
L8916:   }
L8917:   disassemble(prog) {
L8918:     const cap = 1 << 20, out = this.X.malloc(cap);
L8919:     try { return this.withBytes(prog, (p) => (this.X.nmr_disassemble(p, prog.length, out, cap) < 0 ? this.error() : this.str(out))); }
L8920:     finally { this.X.free(out); }
L8921:   }
L8922:   voxelize(grid) { return this.X.nmr_voxelize(grid); }
L8923:   voxels() {
L8924:     const n = this.X.nmr_voxelize ? null : 0;
L8925:     const cap = 20000, p = this.X.malloc(cap * 56);
L8926:     try { const k = this.X.nmr_voxels(p, cap); return new Float64Array(this.X.memory.buffer, p, 7 * k).slice(); } finally { this.X.free(p); }
L8927:   }
L8928:   simulate(prog, model = this.defaultModel()) {
L8929:     const n = this.withBytes(prog, (pp) => this.withStruct('model', (pm) => this.write('model', pm, model), (pm) => this.X.nmr_simulate(pp, prog.length, pm)));
L8930:     if (n < 0) throw new Error(this.error());
L8931:     const recs = [];
L8932:     for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) recs.push(this.record(k));
L8933:     return recs;
L8934:   }
L8935:   record(k) {
L8936:     return this.withStruct('record', () => {}, (pm) => {
L8937:       const n = this.X.nmr_record(k, pm, 0, 0, 0);
L8938:       const pi = this.X.malloc(2 * n), pq = this.X.malloc(2 * n);
L8939:       try {
L8940:         this.X.nmr_record(k, pm, pi, pq, n);
L8941:         const meta = this.read('record', pm);
L8942:         meta.i = new Int16Array(this.X.memory.buffer, pi, n).slice();
L8943:         meta.q = new Int16Array(this.X.memory.buffer, pq, n).slice();
L8944:         return meta;
L8945:       } finally { this.X.free(pi); this.X.free(pq); }
L8946:     });
L8947:   }
L8948:   loadRecords(recs) {   // measured data, same format as simulate()'s output
L8949:     this.X.nmr_clear_records();
L8950:     for (const r of recs) {
L8951:       this.withStruct('record', (pm) => this.write('record', pm, { ...r, n: r.i.length }), (pm) => {
L8952:         const pi = this.X.malloc(2 * r.i.length), pq = this.X.malloc(2 * r.q.length);
L8953:         try {
L8954:           new Int16Array(this.X.memory.buffer, pi, r.i.length).set(r.i);
L8955:           new Int16Array(this.X.memory.buffer, pq, r.q.length).set(r.q);
L8956:           this.X.nmr_load_record(pm, pi, pq);
L8957:         } finally { this.X.free(pi); this.X.free(pq); }
L8958:       });
L8959:     }
L8960:   }
L8961:   process(pipeline = this.defaultPipeline(), window = 0) {
L8962:     const cap = 1 << 20, out = this.X.malloc(16 * cap), pf = this.X.malloc(16);
L8963:     try {
L8964:       const n = this.withStruct('pipeline', (pp) => this.write('pipeline', pp, { ...pipeline, window_id: window }),
L8965:         (pp) => this.X.nmr_process(pp, out, cap, pf, pf + 8));
L8966:       if (n < 0) throw new Error(this.error());
L8967:       const re = new Float64Array(this.X.memory.buffer, out, 2 * n).slice();
L8968:       return { reim: re, fs: this.dv.getFloat64(pf, true), t0: this.dv.getFloat64(pf + 8, true) };
L8969:     } finally { this.X.free(out); this.X.free(pf); }
L8970:   }
L8971:   spectrum(reim, fs, window = 0, tc = 0, pad = 2) {
L8972:     const n = reim.length / 2, cap = 1 << 21;
L8973:     const px = this.X.malloc(8 * reim.length), pf = this.X.malloc(8 * cap), pv = this.X.malloc(16 * cap);
L8974:     try {
L8975:       new Float64Array(this.X.memory.buffer, px, reim.length).set(reim);
L8976:       const m = this.X.nmr_spectrum(px, n, fs, window, tc, pad, pf, pv, cap);
L8977:       if (m < 0) throw new Error(this.error());
L8978:       return { freq: new Float64Array(this.X.memory.buffer, pf, m).slice(), reim: new Float64Array(this.X.memory.buffer, pv, 2 * m).slice() };
L8979:     } finally { this.X.free(px); this.X.free(pf); this.X.free(pv); }
L8980:   }
L8981:   peak(spec, fMin, fMax, guard) {
L8982:     const m = spec.freq.length, pf = this.X.malloc(8 * m), pv = this.X.malloc(16 * m), po = this.X.malloc(32);
L8983:     try {
L8984:       new Float64Array(this.X.memory.buffer, pf, m).set(spec.freq);
L8985:       new Float64Array(this.X.memory.buffer, pv, 2 * m).set(spec.reim);
L8986:       this.X.nmr_peak(pf, pv, m, fMin, fMax, guard, po);
L8987:       const o = new Float64Array(this.X.memory.buffer, po, 4);
L8988:       return { freq_hz: o[0], amplitude: o[1], noise_rms: o[2], snr: o[3] };
L8989:     } finally { this.X.free(pf); this.X.free(pv); this.X.free(po); }
L8990:   }
L8991: }

L8999: // Runs one experiment on the WASM core and prints JSON for tests/test_wasm_parity.py to compare with native.

L9035: def test_wasm_matches_native():
L9036:     from nmrpy import core
L9037:     w = json.loads(subprocess.run(["node", str(ROOT / "tests/wasm_parity.mjs")], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout)
L9038:     assert w["sha"] == core.config_sha256(), "both builds from the same generated config"

L9046:     di = np.abs(np.array(w["i0"]) - recs[0]["i"][:2000]).max()
L9047:     dq = np.abs(np.array(w["q3"]) - recs[3]["q"][:2000]).max()
L9048:     print(f"max ADC code difference native vs wasm: I {di}, Q {dq} LSB")

L9055:     pk = core.peak(f, sp, -400, 400, 10)

L9057:     assert w["peak"]["amplitude"] == pytest.approx(pk["amplitude"], rel=1e-4)

L9217: 65 lines
L9218: // Backend planning: one scan of a validated program -> gate waveforms + acquisition windows + markers.
L9219: //
L9220: // This is the boundary between the hardware-neutral IR and a timing backend. The ESP32 backend packs the segments into
L9221: // RMT symbols; an FPGA backend would load the same segments into its sequencer. Fixed capacities: no allocation, so
L9222: // a plan can be built right before a scan runs.
L9223: //
L9224: // Gate channels (logic levels at the pins, 1 = asserted):
L9225: //   kTxGate  : TX_EN            (1 = transmit)
L9226: //   kRxOpen  : RX_BLANK pin     (1 = receive, 0 = blanked; the board's polarity)
L9227: //   kPhaseSel: DDS PSELECT      (0 = phase register 0, 1 = phase register 1)
L9228: #pragma once
L9229: 
L9230: #include <cstdint>
L9231: #include <vector>
L9232: 
L9233: #include "pulse/program.hpp"
L9234: 
L9235: namespace pulse {
L9236: 
L9237: inline constexpr int kGateChannels = 3;
L9238: inline constexpr int kTxGate = 0, kRxOpen = 1, kPhaseSel = 2;
L9239: inline constexpr int kMaxSegments = 96;
L9240: inline constexpr int kMaxWindows = 64;
L9241: 
L9242: struct Segment {
L9243:     uint8_t level;
L9244:     uint32_t ticks;   // > 0
L9245: };
L9246: 
L9247: struct Window {
L9248:     uint32_t id;
L9249:     uint64_t start, end;   // ticks from the scan start
L9250: };
L9251: 
L9252: struct ScanPlan {
L9253:     uint32_t scan = 0;
L9254:     uint64_t start = 0;        // absolute program tick of the scan start (SCAN_BEGIN)
L9255:     uint64_t length = 0;       // ticks to SCAN_END
L9256:     uint8_t initial[kGateChannels] = {};
L9257:     Segment seg[kGateChannels][kMaxSegments];
L9258:     uint16_t nseg[kGateChannels] = {};
L9259:     uint32_t phase_reg[2] = {}; // DDS phase-register contents (1/65536 turn) selected by kPhaseSel
L9260:     uint8_t nphase = 0;
L9261:     uint32_t freq_mhz = 0, amplitude = kTurn;
L9262:     uint32_t rx_phase = 0;      // RX_PHASE operand
L9263:     Window win[kMaxWindows];
L9264:     uint8_t nwin = 0;
L9265:     bool has_excitation = false;
L9266:     uint64_t excitation = 0;    // marker 1, ticks from the scan start
L9267: };
L9268: 
L9269: struct BackendCaps {
L9270:     int phase_registers = 2;    // AD9834: PHASE0/PHASE1 selected by the PSELECT pin
L9271:     uint32_t max_segment_ticks = 32767u * 2u;   // informative; packing splits longer segments
L9272: };
L9273: 
L9274: // Number of scans in an expanded program (SCAN_BEGIN count).
L9275: size_t count_scans(const std::vector<Event>& ev);
L9276: 
L9277: // Plan scan k (0-based). Fails if the scan needs more phase values than the backend has registers, exceeds a fixed
L9278: // capacity, or is malformed.
L9279: bool plan_scan(const std::vector<Event>& ev, size_t k, const BackendCaps& caps, ScanPlan& out, Diagnostic& diag);
L9280: 
L9281: }  // namespace pulse
L9282: Write C:\Users\Liang\tigp-2026\nmr-instrument\pulse\src\backend.cpp

L9284: #include "pulse/backend.hpp"
L9285: 
L9286: namespace pulse {
L9287: 
L9288: size_t count_scans(const std::vector<Event>& ev) {
L9289:     size_t n = 0;
L9290:     for (const auto& e : ev) n += e.op == Op::SCAN_BEGIN;
L9291:     return n;
L9292: }
L9293: 
L9294: bool plan_scan(const std::vector<Event>& ev, size_t k, const BackendCaps& caps, ScanPlan& p, Diagnostic& diag) {
L9295:     p = ScanPlan{};
L9296:     // state carried in from before the scan (frequency, amplitude, the last phase)
L9297:     size_t i = 0, scans = 0;
L9298:     uint32_t phase_before = 0;
L9299:     for (; i < ev.size(); ++i) {
L9300:         const Event& e = ev[i];
L9301:         if (e.op == Op::SCAN_BEGIN && scans++ == k) break;
L9302:         if (e.op == Op::SET_FREQ && e.chan == 0) p.freq_mhz = e.arg;
L9303:         if (e.op == Op::SET_AMPLITUDE && e.chan == 0) p.amplitude = e.arg;
L9304:         if (e.op == Op::SET_PHASE && e.chan == 0) phase_before = e.arg;
L9305:     }
L9306:     if (i == ev.size()) { diag = {i, "scan index out of range"}; return false; }
L9307:     p.scan = ev[i].arg;
L9308:     p.start = ev[i].t;
L9309:     p.initial[kTxGate] = 0;
L9310:     p.initial[kRxOpen] = 0;      // compiler-generated scans start blanked
L9311:     p.initial[kPhaseSel] = 0;
L9312:     p.phase_reg[0] = phase_before;
L9313:     p.nphase = 1;
L9314:     uint8_t level[kGateChannels] = {0, 0, 0};
L9315:     uint64_t last[kGateChannels] = {p.start, p.start, p.start};
L9316:     bool phase0_used = false;
L9317:     const auto change = [&](int ch, uint8_t v, uint64_t t) -> bool {
L9318:         if (v == level[ch]) return true;
L9319:         if (t > last[ch]) {
L9320:             if (p.nseg[ch] >= kMaxSegments) { diag = {0, "gate channel exceeds kMaxSegments"}; return false; }
L9321:             p.seg[ch][p.nseg[ch]++] = {level[ch], static_cast<uint32_t>(t - last[ch])};
L9322:         }
L9323:         level[ch] = v;
L9324:         last[ch] = t;
L9325:         return true;
L9326:     };
L9327:     for (++i; i < ev.size(); ++i) {
L9328:         const Event& e = ev[i];
L9329:         const uint64_t rel = e.t - p.start;
L9330:         switch (e.op) {
L9331:             case Op::TX_GATE_ON: if (!change(kTxGate, 1, e.t)) return false; break;
L9332:             case Op::TX_GATE_OFF: if (!change(kTxGate, 0, e.t)) return false; break;
L9333:             case Op::RX_ENABLE: if (!change(kRxOpen, 1, e.t)) return false; break;
L9334:             case Op::RX_BLANK: if (!change(kRxOpen, 0, e.t)) return false; break;
L9335:             case Op::SET_PHASE: {
L9336:                 // map phase values onto the registers in order of first use
L9337:                 int reg = -1;
L9338:                 for (int r = 0; r < p.nphase; ++r)
L9339:                     if (p.phase_reg[r] == e.arg) reg = r;
L9340:                 if (reg < 0 && !phase0_used) { p.phase_reg[0] = e.arg; reg = 0; }
L9341:                 if (reg < 0) {
L9342:                     if (p.nphase >= caps.phase_registers) { diag = {i, "scan uses more RF phases than the backend has phase registers"}; return false; }
L9343:                     p.phase_reg[p.nphase] = e.arg;
L9344:                     reg = p.nphase++;
L9345:                 }
L9346:                 if (reg == 0) phase0_used = true;
L9347:                 if (!change(kPhaseSel, static_cast<uint8_t>(reg), e.t)) return false;
L9348:                 break;
L9349:             }
L9350:             case Op::SET_FREQ:
L9351:                 if (e.arg != p.freq_mhz) { diag = {i, "frequency change inside a scan is not supported by this backend"}; return false; }
L9352:                 break;
L9353:             case Op::SET_AMPLITUDE:
L9354:                 if (e.arg != p.amplitude) { diag = {i, "amplitude change inside a scan is not supported by this backend"}; return false; }
L9355:                 break;
L9356:             case Op::RX_PHASE: p.rx_phase = e.arg; break;
L9357:             case Op::MARKER:
L9358:                 if (e.arg == kMarkerExcitation && !p.has_excitation) { p.has_excitation = true; p.excitation = rel; }
L9359:                 break;
L9360:             case Op::ACQ_ON:
L9361:                 if (p.nwin >= kMaxWindows) { diag = {i, "too many acquisition windows in a scan"}; return false; }
L9362:                 p.win[p.nwin++] = {e.arg, rel, rel};
L9363:                 break;
L9364:             case Op::ACQ_OFF:
L9365:                 for (int w = p.nwin - 1; w >= 0; --w)
L9366:                     if (p.win[w].id == e.arg && p.win[w].end == p.win[w].start) { p.win[w].end = rel; break; }
L9367:                 break;
L9368:             case Op::SCAN_END:
L9369:                 p.length = rel;
L9370:                 for (int ch = 0; ch < kGateChannels; ++ch)
L9371:                     if (e.t > last[ch]) {
L9372:                         if (p.nseg[ch] >= kMaxSegments) { diag = {i, "gate channel exceeds kMaxSegments"}; return false; }
L9373:                         p.seg[ch][p.nseg[ch]++] = {level[ch], static_cast<uint32_t>(e.t - last[ch])};
L9374:                         last[ch] = e.t;
L9375:                     }
L9376:                 if (level[kTxGate]) { diag = {i, "scan ends with TX on"}; return false; }
L9377:                 return true;
L9378:             case Op::SCAN_BEGIN:
L9379:             case Op::END:
L9380:                 diag = {i, "scan without SCAN_END"};
L9381:                 return false;
L9382:             default: break;
L9383:         }
L9384:     }
L9385:     diag = {ev.size(), "scan without SCAN_END"};
L9386:     return false;
L9387: }
L9388: 
L9389: }  // namespace pulse

L9402: static uint32_t ticks(double s) { return static_cast<uint32_t>(std::llround(s * TIMING_TICK_HZ)); }

L9404: static std::vector<Event> build(const std::vector<Scan>& s) {
L9405:     Timing tm{TIMING_TICK_HZ, TX_FREQUENCY_HZ, TIMING_PRE_BLANK_S, RECEIVER_DEAD_TIME_S, ACQUISITION_REPETITION_TIME_S};

L9409:     std::vector<Event> ev;

L9412:     return ev;
L9413: }

L9415: int main() {
L9416:     // FID, CYCLOPS: scan 2 has phase 180 degrees
L9417:     {
L9418:         const auto ev = build(fid(TX_T90_S, ACQUISITION_START_DELAY_S, 0.1, 4, Cycle::Cyclops));
L9419:         CHECK(count_scans(ev) == 4, "four scans");
L9420:         ScanPlan p;
L9421:         Diagnostic d;
L9422:         CHECK(plan_scan(ev, 2, BackendCaps{}, p, d), "plan scan 2");
L9423:         CHECK(p.scan == 2 && p.start == 2ull * ticks(ACQUISITION_REPETITION_TIME_S), "scan start time");
L9424:         // TX: low for pre_blank, high for t90, low for the rest
L9425:         CHECK(p.nseg[kTxGate] == 3, "TX gate: three segments");
L9426:         CHECK(p.seg[kTxGate][0].level == 0 && p.seg[kTxGate][0].ticks == ticks(TIMING_PRE_BLANK_S), "TX: pre-blank");
L9427:         CHECK(p.seg[kTxGate][1].level == 1 && p.seg[kTxGate][1].ticks == ticks(TX_T90_S), "TX: pulse length");
L9428:         // RX: blanked until pulse end + dead time, then open until the window closes, then blanked
L9429:         CHECK(p.seg[kRxOpen][0].level == 0 && p.seg[kRxOpen][0].ticks == ticks(TIMING_PRE_BLANK_S) + ticks(TX_T90_S) + ticks(RECEIVER_DEAD_TIME_S), "RX: blanked through the dead time");
L9430:         CHECK(p.seg[kRxOpen][1].level == 1, "RX: then open");
L9431:         CHECK(p.nwin == 1 && p.win[0].start == ticks(TIMING_PRE_BLANK_S) + ticks(TX_T90_S) + ticks(ACQUISITION_START_DELAY_S), "window start");
L9432:         CHECK(p.win[0].end - p.win[0].start == ticks(0.1), "window length");
L9433:         CHECK(p.has_excitation && p.excitation == ticks(TIMING_PRE_BLANK_S), "excitation marker at the pulse start");
L9434:         CHECK(p.nphase == 1 && p.phase_reg[0] == kTurn / 2, "one phase register holding 180 degrees");
L9435:         CHECK(p.rx_phase == kTurn / 2, "receiver phase 180 degrees");
L9436:         uint64_t sum = 0;
L9437:         for (int s = 0; s < p.nseg[kTxGate]; ++s) sum += p.seg[kTxGate][s].ticks;
L9438:         CHECK(sum == p.length, "TX segments cover the whole scan");
L9439:     }
L9440:     // Hahn echo: 90x then 180y -> two phase registers, PSELECT switches between the pulses

L9455:         a.duration_s = b.duration_s = c.duration_s = 1e-4;

L9591: Now I'll build the gate engine core: RMT channels driven by a ScanPlan, started together via the sync manager, with edges timestamped through MCPWM capture over GPIO loopback, using a fixed-size ISR array and compile-time buffer sizing.
L9592: Write C:\Users\Liang\tigp-2026\nmr-instrument\firmware\components\engine\include\engine.hpp
L9593: 78 lines
L9594: // Hard-real-time gate engine (ADR-0005).
L9595: //
L9596: // Each scan's gate waveforms (TX_EN, RX_BLANK, DDS PSELECT; pulse/backend.hpp) are packed into RMT symbols and
L9597: // started on three RMT TX channels at the same APB clock edge through an RMT sync manager. After start the CPU does
L9598: // nothing for the gates: the RMT peripheral produces every edge. All gate pins (and optional reference inputs) are
L9599: // timestamped by MCPWM capture channels on one 80 MHz capture timer, so the actual timing is measured, not assumed.
L9600: //
L9601: // Real-time rules: no allocation after init() (fixed symbol and edge buffers), no logging or locks in the ISR, and
L9602: // nothing here touches Wi-Fi, JSON or the filesystem.
L9603: #pragma once
L9604: 
L9605: #include <cstddef>
L9606: #include <cstdint>
L9607: 
L9608: #include "esp_err.h"
L9609: #include "pulse/backend.hpp"
L9610: 
L9611: namespace fw {
L9612: 
L9613: inline constexpr int kMaxSymbols = 512;       // per gate channel and scan
L9614: inline constexpr int kMaxEdges = 256;         // timestamped edges per scan, all inputs together
L9615: 
L9616: enum Input : uint8_t { kInTxGate = 0, kInRxOpen = 1, kInPhaseSel = 2, kInDdsSign = 3, kInLoI = 4, kInWs = 5, kInputs = 6 };
L9617: 
L9618: struct Edge {
L9619:     uint32_t ticks;   // capture timer (80 MHz) count
L9620:     uint8_t input;    // Input
L9621:     uint8_t rising;
L9622: };
L9623: 
L9624: struct EngineConfig {
L9625:     int pin_tx_gate, pin_rx_open, pin_phase_sel;
L9626:     int pin_dds_sign = -1, pin_lo_i = -1, pin_ws = -1;   // -1 = not wired (ECO-1 / ADC ws)
L9627:     uint32_t tick_hz;                                    // must equal the pulse-program tick
L9628: };
L9629: 
L9630: class GateEngine {
L9631: public:
L9632:     esp_err_t init(const EngineConfig& c);
L9633:     // Pack a plan into RMT symbols (fixed buffers). Fails if a channel needs more than kMaxSymbols.
L9634:     esp_err_t load(const pulse::ScanPlan& p);
L9635:     // Arm the capture log, then start the three gate channels on the same clock edge.
L9636:     esp_err_t start();
L9637:     esp_err_t wait_done(uint32_t timeout_ms);
L9638:     // Capture log of the last scan (valid after wait_done).
L9639:     const Edge* edges() const { return edges_; }
L9640:     size_t edge_count() const { return nedges_ > kMaxEdges ? kMaxEdges : nedges_; }
L9641:     bool edges_overflowed() const { return nedges_ > kMaxEdges; }
L9642:     uint32_t capture_hz() const { return cap_hz_; }
L9643:     // One-shot capture of the next edge on an input (e.g. the first ADC frame sync after I2S starts).
L9644:     void arm_single(Input in);
L9645: 
L9646:     // ISR side (public only for the C callback trampoline)
L9647:     void on_capture(Input in, uint32_t t, bool rising);
L9648: 
L9649: private:
L9650:     EngineConfig cfg_{};
L9651:     void* rmt_ch_[pulse::kGateChannels] = {};
L9652:     void* rmt_enc_ = nullptr;
L9653:     void* rmt_sync_ = nullptr;
L9654:     void* cap_timer_ = nullptr;
L9655:     void* cap_ch_[kInputs] = {};
L9656:     uint32_t sym_[pulse::kGateChannels][kMaxSymbols] = {};
L9657:     size_t nsym_[pulse::kGateChannels] = {};
L9658:     uint8_t eot_[pulse::kGateChannels] = {};
L9659:     Edge edges_[kMaxEdges];
L9660:     volatile size_t nedges_ = 0;
L9661:     volatile uint32_t single_mask_ = 0;   // inputs that log exactly one more edge
L9662:     volatile uint32_t enabled_mask_ = 0;  // inputs that log every edge
L9663:     uint32_t cap_hz_ = 0;
L9664: };
L9665: 
L9666: // Pack a gate channel's segments into RMT symbol words (15-bit durations; long segments split; an odd count is
L9667: // completed by splitting the last segment). Exposed for host tests. Returns the symbol count, 0 on overflow.
L9668: size_t pack_segments(const pulse::Segment* seg, size_t n, uint32_t* out, size_t cap);
L9669: 
L9670: }  // namespace fw

L9673: #include "engine.hpp"

L9681: namespace {
L9682: 
L9683: constexpr uint32_t kMaxDur = 32767;
L9684: 
L9685: inline uint32_t word(uint32_t d0, uint32_t l0, uint32_t d1, uint32_t l1) {
L9686:     return (d0 & 0x7FFF) | ((l0 & 1u) << 15) | ((d1 & 0x7FFF) << 16) | ((l1 & 1u) << 31);
L9687: }
L9688: 
L9689: struct CapCtx {
L9690:     GateEngine* eng;
L9691:     Input in;
L9692: };
L9693: CapCtx g_ctx[kInputs];
L9694: 
L9695: bool IRAM_ATTR on_cap(mcpwm_cap_channel_handle_t, const mcpwm_capture_event_data_t* d, void* user) {
L9696:     auto* c = static_cast<CapCtx*>(user);
L9697:     c->eng->on_capture(c->in, d->cap_value, d->cap_edge == MCPWM_CAP_EDGE_POS);
L9698:     return false;
L9699: }
L9700: 
L9701: }  // namespace
L9702: 
L9703: size_t pack_segments(const pulse::Segment* seg, size_t n, uint32_t* out, size_t cap) {
L9704:     // flatten into (level, duration <= kMaxDur) halves
L9705:     size_t halves = 0;
L9706:     for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) halves += (seg[i].ticks + kMaxDur - 1) / kMaxDur;
L9707:     if (halves % 2) ++halves;   // the last segment is split once more so the symbol count is whole
L9708:     if (halves / 2 > cap) return 0;
L9709:     size_t h = 0, w = 0;
L9710:     uint32_t pend_d = 0, pend_l = 0;
L9711:     bool have = false;
L9712:     for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
L9713:         uint32_t left = seg[i].ticks;
L9714:         const bool last = i + 1 == n;
L9715:         size_t parts = (left + kMaxDur - 1) / kMaxDur;
L9716:         const size_t remaining_after = halves - h - parts;   // halves still to come after this segment's
L9717:         if (last && remaining_after == 1) ++parts;           // the odd-count fix-up
L9718:         for (size_t k = 0; k < parts; ++k) {
L9719:             const uint32_t d = (k + 1 == parts) ? left : (parts - k == 1 ? left : (left / (parts - k)));
L9720:             left -= d;
L9721:             if (!have) { pend_d = d; pend_l = seg[i].level; have = true; }
L9722:             else { out[w++] = word(pend_d, pend_l, d, seg[i].level); have = false; }
L9723:             ++h;
L9724:         }
L9725:     }
L9726:     return w;
L9727: }
L9728: 
L9729: esp_err_t GateEngine::init(const EngineConfig& c) {
L9730:     cfg_ = c;
L9731:     // capture first (it configures the pads as inputs), then RMT (output, with input kept enabled by io_loop_back)
L9732:     mcpwm_capture_timer_config_t tc = {};
L9733:     tc.group_id = 0;
L9734:     tc.clk_src = MCPWM_CAPTURE_CLK_SRC_DEFAULT;
L9735:     tc.resolution_hz = 80 * 1000 * 1000;
L9736:     mcpwm_cap_timer_handle_t timer;
L9737:     ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR_SAFE:;
L9738:     esp_err_t e = mcpwm_new_capture_timer(&tc, &timer);
L9739:     if (e != ESP_OK) return e;
L9740:     cap_timer_ = timer;
L9741:     mcpwm_capture_timer_get_resolution(timer, &cap_hz_);
L9742:     const int pins[kInputs] = {c.pin_tx_gate, c.pin_rx_open, c.pin_phase_sel, c.pin_dds_sign, c.pin_lo_i, c.pin_ws};
L9743:     for (int i = 0; i < kInputs; ++i) {
L9744:         if (pins[i] < 0) continue;
L9745:         if (i >= 3 && i != kInWs) {
L9746:             // MCPWM group 0 has three capture channels: the gate loopbacks. Reference inputs go to group 1.
L9747:         }
L9748:         mcpwm_capture_channel_config_t cc = {};
L9749:         cc.gpio_num = pins[i];
L9750:         cc.prescale = 1;
L9751:         cc.flags.pos_edge = true;
L9752:         cc.flags.neg_edge = true;
L9753:         mcpwm_cap_channel_handle_t ch;
L9754:         e = mcpwm_new_capture_channel(timer, &cc, &ch);
L9755:         if (e != ESP_OK) break;   // more inputs than this group's channels: the rest stay unlogged
L9756:         g_ctx[i] = {this, static_cast<Input>(i)};
L9757:         mcpwm_capture_event_callbacks_t cb = {};
L9758:         cb.on_cap = on_cap;
L9759:         mcpwm_capture_channel_register_event_callbacks(ch, &cb, &g_ctx[i]);
L9760:         mcpwm_capture_channel_enable(ch);
L9761:         cap_ch_[i] = ch;
L9762:     }
L9763:     mcpwm_capture_timer_enable(timer);
L9764:     mcpwm_capture_timer_start(timer);
L9765: 
L9766:     const int gpins[pulse::kGateChannels] = {c.pin_tx_gate, c.pin_rx_open, c.pin_phase_sel};
L9767:     for (int i = 0; i < pulse::kGateChannels; ++i) {
L9768:         rmt_tx_channel_config_t rc = {};
L9769:         rc.gpio_num = static_cast<gpio_num_t>(gpins[i]);
L9770:         rc.clk_src = RMT_CLK_SRC_DEFAULT;
L9771:         rc.resolution_hz = c.tick_hz;
L9772:         rc.mem_block_symbols = 48;
L9773:         rc.trans_queue_depth = 2;
L9774:         rc.flags.io_loop_back = true;   // keep the pad's input path on for the capture loopback
L9775:         rmt_channel_handle_t ch;
L9776:         e = rmt_new_tx_channel(&rc, &ch);
L9777:         if (e != ESP_OK) return e;
L9778:         rmt_enable(ch);
L9779:         rmt_ch_[i] = ch;
L9780:     }
L9781:     rmt_copy_encoder_config_t ec = {};
L9782:     rmt_encoder_handle_t enc;
L9783:     e = rmt_new_copy_encoder(&ec, &enc);
L9784:     if (e != ESP_OK) return e;
L9785:     rmt_enc_ = enc;
L9786:     rmt_sync_manager_config_t sc = {};
L9787:     sc.tx_channel_array = reinterpret_cast<rmt_channel_handle_t*>(rmt_ch_);
L9788:     sc.array_size = pulse::kGateChannels;
L9789:     rmt_sync_manager_handle_t sync;
L9790:     e = rmt_new_sync_manager(&sc, &sync);
L9791:     if (e != ESP_OK) return e;
L9792:     rmt_sync_ = sync;
L9793:     return ESP_OK;
L9794: }
L9795: 
L9796: esp_err_t GateEngine::load(const pulse::ScanPlan& p) {
L9797:     for (int ch = 0; ch < pulse::kGateChannels; ++ch) {
L9798:         // trailing segment at the channel's final level need not be transmitted: the end-of-transmission level holds it
L9799:         size_t n = p.nseg[ch];
L9800:         uint8_t final_level = n ? p.seg[ch][n - 1].level : p.initial[ch];
L9801:         if (n > 1 && p.seg[ch][n - 1].level == final_level) --n;
L9802:         if (n == 0) {   // constant level for the whole scan: one short symbol at that level
L9803:             const pulse::Segment s[1] = {{final_level, 2}};
L9804:             nsym_[ch] = pack_segments(s, 1, sym_[ch], kMaxSymbols);
L9805:         } else {
L9806:             nsym_[ch] = pack_segments(p.seg[ch], n, sym_[ch], kMaxSymbols);
L9807:         }
L9808:         if (nsym_[ch] == 0) return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
L9809:         eot_[ch] = final_level;
L9810:     }
L9811:     return ESP_OK;
L9812: }
L9813: 
L9814: esp_err_t GateEngine::start() {
L9815:     nedges_ = 0;
L9816:     enabled_mask_ = (1u << kInTxGate) | (1u << kInRxOpen) | (1u << kInPhaseSel);
L9817:     rmt_sync_reset(static_cast<rmt_sync_manager_handle_t>(rmt_sync_));
L9818:     for (int ch = 0; ch < pulse::kGateChannels; ++ch) {
L9819:         rmt_transmit_config_t tc = {};
L9820:         tc.loop_count = 0;
L9821:         tc.flags.eot_level = eot_[ch];
L9822:         const esp_err_t e = rmt_transmit(static_cast<rmt_channel_handle_t>(rmt_ch_[ch]),
L9823:                                          static_cast<rmt_encoder_handle_t>(rmt_enc_), sym_[ch], nsym_[ch] * 4, &tc);
L9824:         if (e != ESP_OK) return e;   // with a sync manager nothing has started until all three are queued
L9825:     }
L9826:     return ESP_OK;
L9827: }
L9828: 
L9829: esp_err_t GateEngine::wait_done(uint32_t timeout_ms) {
L9830:     for (int ch = 0; ch < pulse::kGateChannels; ++ch) {
L9831:         const esp_err_t e = rmt_tx_wait_all_done(static_cast<rmt_channel_handle_t>(rmt_ch_[ch]), static_cast<int>(timeout_ms));
L9832:         if (e != ESP_OK) return e;
L9833:     }
L9834:     enabled_mask_ = 0;
L9835:     return ESP_OK;
L9836: }
L9837: 
L9838: void GateEngine::arm_single(Input in) { single_mask_ = single_mask_ | (1u << in); }
L9839: 
L9840: void IRAM_ATTR GateEngine::on_capture(Input in, uint32_t t, bool rising) {
L9841:     const uint32_t bit = 1u << in;
L9842:     if (!(enabled_mask_ & bit)) {
L9843:         if (!(single_mask_ & bit)) return;
L9844:         single_mask_ = single_mask_ & ~bit;
L9845:     }
L9846:     const size_t k = nedges_;
L9847:     if (k < kMaxEdges) edges_[k] = {t, static_cast<uint8_t>(in), static_cast<uint8_t>(rising)};
L9848:     nedges_ = k + 1;
L9849: }

L9851: }  // namespace fw

L10197: inline uint32_t word(uint32_t d0, uint32_t l0, uint32_t d1, uint32_t l1) {

L10207: bool IRAM_ATTR on_cap(mcpwm_cap_channel_handle_t, const mcpwm_capture_event_data_t* d, void* user) {
L10208:     auto* c = static_cast<CapCtx*>(user);

L10215: size_t pack_segments(const pulse::Segment* seg, size_t n, uint32_t* out, size_t cap) {
L10216:     // halves of (level, duration <= kMaxDur); the total must be even (two halves per symbol word)
L10217:     size_t halves = 0;
L10218:     for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) halves += (seg[i].ticks + kMaxDur - 1) / kMaxDur;
L10219:     const bool fixup = halves % 2;
L10220:     if (fixup) ++halves;
L10221:     if (halves / 2 > cap) return 0;
L10222:     size_t w = 0;
L10223:     uint32_t pend_d = 0, pend_l = 0;
L10224:     bool have = false;
L10225:     for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
L10226:         uint32_t left = seg[i].ticks;
L10227:         size_t parts = (left + kMaxDur - 1) / kMaxDur;
L10228:         if (fixup && i + 1 == n) ++parts;   // split the last segment once more
L10229:         for (size_t k = 0; k < parts; ++k) {
L10230:             const uint32_t d = left / static_cast<uint32_t>(parts - k);   // even split, every part >= 1 tick
L10231:             left -= d;
L10232:             if (!have) { pend_d = d; pend_l = seg[i].level; have = true; }
L10233:             else { out[w++] = word(pend_d, pend_l, d, seg[i].level); have = false; }
L10234:         }
L10235:     }
L10236:     return w;
L10237: }

L10239: esp_err_t GateEngine::init(const EngineConfig& c) {

L10241:     esp_err_t e;
L10242:     // 1. RMT gate outputs
L10243:     const int gpins[pulse::kGateChannels] = {c.pin_tx_gate, c.pin_rx_open, c.pin_phase_sel};
L10244:     for (int i = 0; i < pulse::kGateChannels; ++i) {

L10246:         rc.gpio_num = static_cast<gpio_num_t>(gpins[i]);
L10247:         rc.clk_src = RMT_CLK_SRC_DEFAULT;
L10248:         rc.resolution_hz = c.tick_hz;

L10256:     }
L10257:     rmt_copy_encoder_config_t ec = {};

L10259:     if ((e = rmt_new_copy_encoder(&ec, &enc)) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10260:     rmt_enc_ = enc;

L10267: 
L10268:     // 2. Capture: group 0 = the three gate loopbacks, group 1 = beat references and the ADC frame sync. Both timers
L10269:     //    at 80 MHz, phase-reset by one GPIO sync edge so every timestamp is on the same timebase.

L10272:         tc.group_id = g;
L10273:         tc.clk_src = MCPWM_CAPTURE_CLK_SRC_DEFAULT;
L10274:         tc.resolution_hz = 80 * 1000 * 1000;
L10275:         mcpwm_cap_timer_handle_t timer;
L10276:         if ((e = mcpwm_new_capture_timer(&tc, &timer)) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10277:         cap_timer_[g] = timer;
L10278:         if (c.pin_sync >= 0) {
L10279:             mcpwm_gpio_sync_src_config_t ssc = {};
L10280:             ssc.group_id = g;
L10281:             ssc.gpio_num = c.pin_sync;
L10282:             mcpwm_sync_handle_t s;
L10283:             if ((e = mcpwm_new_gpio_sync_src(&ssc, &s)) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10284:             cap_sync_[g] = s;
L10285:             mcpwm_capture_timer_sync_phase_config_t ph = {};
L10286:             ph.sync_src = s;
L10287:             ph.count_value = 0;
L10288:             ph.direction = MCPWM_TIMER_DIRECTION_UP;
L10289:             if ((e = mcpwm_capture_timer_set_phase_on_sync(timer, &ph)) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10290:         }
L10291:     }
L10292:     mcpwm_capture_timer_get_resolution(static_cast<mcpwm_cap_timer_handle_t>(cap_timer_[0]), &cap_hz_);
L10293:     const int pins[kInputs] = {c.pin_tx_gate, c.pin_rx_open, c.pin_phase_sel, c.pin_dds_sign, c.pin_lo_i, c.pin_ws};
L10294:     for (int i = 0; i < kInputs; ++i) {
L10295:         if (pins[i] < 0) continue;
L10296:         mcpwm_capture_channel_config_t cc = {};
L10297:         cc.gpio_num = pins[i];
L10298:         cc.prescale = 1;
L10299:         cc.flags.pos_edge = true;
L10300:         cc.flags.neg_edge = true;
L10301:         mcpwm_cap_channel_handle_t ch;
L10302:         if ((e = mcpwm_new_capture_channel(static_cast<mcpwm_cap_timer_handle_t>(cap_timer_[i < 3 ? 0 : 1]), &cc, &ch)) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10303:         g_ctx[i] = {this, static_cast<Input>(i)};
L10304:         mcpwm_capture_event_callbacks_t cb = {};
L10305:         cb.on_cap = on_cap;
L10306:         if ((e = mcpwm_capture_channel_register_event_callbacks(ch, &cb, &g_ctx[i])) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10307:         if ((e = mcpwm_capture_channel_enable(ch)) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10308:         cap_ch_[i] = ch;
L10309:         // the gate pins are RMT outputs: keep their input path on so the capture sees the pad
L10310:         gpio_input_enable(static_cast<gpio_num_t>(pins[i]));
L10311:     }
L10312:     for (int g = 0; g < 2; ++g) {
L10313:         if ((e = mcpwm_capture_timer_enable(static_cast<mcpwm_cap_timer_handle_t>(cap_timer_[g]))) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10314:         if ((e = mcpwm_capture_timer_start(static_cast<mcpwm_cap_timer_handle_t>(cap_timer_[g]))) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10315:     }
L10316:     if (c.pin_sync >= 0) {   // one rising edge: both timers restart from 0 together
L10317:         const auto p = static_cast<gpio_num_t>(c.pin_sync);
L10318:         gpio_set_direction(p, GPIO_MODE_INPUT_OUTPUT);
L10319:         gpio_set_level(p, 0);
L10320:         gpio_set_level(p, 1);
L10321:         gpio_set_level(p, 0);
L10322:     }
L10323:     return ESP_OK;
L10324: }
L10325: 
L10326: esp_err_t GateEngine::load(const pulse::ScanPlan& p) {
L10327:     for (int ch = 0; ch < pulse::kGateChannels; ++ch) {
L10328:         // a trailing segment need not be transmitted: the end-of-transmission level holds it
L10329:         size_t n = p.nseg[ch];
L10330:         const uint8_t final_level = n ? p.seg[ch][n - 1].level : p.initial[ch];
L10331:         if (n > 1) --n;
L10332:         if (n == 0) {
L10333:             const pulse::Segment s[1] = {{final_level, 2}};
L10334:             nsym_[ch] = pack_segments(s, 1, sym_[ch], kMaxSymbols);
L10335:         } else {
L10336:             nsym_[ch] = pack_segments(p.seg[ch], n, sym_[ch], kMaxSymbols);
L10337:         }
L10338:         if (nsym_[ch] == 0) return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
L10339:         eot_[ch] = final_level;
L10340:     }
L10341:     return ESP_OK;
L10342: }
L10343: 
L10344: esp_err_t GateEngine::start() {
L10345:     nedges_ = 0;
L10346:     enabled_mask_ = (1u << kInTxGate) | (1u << kInRxOpen) | (1u << kInPhaseSel) | (1u << kInDdsSign) | (1u << kInLoI);
L10347:     esp_err_t e = rmt_sync_reset(static_cast<rmt_sync_manager_handle_t>(rmt_sync_));
L10348:     if (e != ESP_OK) return e;
L10349:     for (int ch = 0; ch < pulse::kGateChannels; ++ch) {
L10350:         rmt_transmit_config_t tc = {};
L10351:         tc.loop_count = 0;
L10352:         tc.flags.eot_level = eot_[ch];
L10353:         // with a sync manager nothing starts until all three channels are queued; then they start on one clock edge
L10354:         if ((e = rmt_transmit(static_cast<rmt_channel_handle_t>(rmt_ch_[ch]), static_cast<rmt_encoder_handle_t>(rmt_enc_),
L10355:                               sym_[ch], nsym_[ch] * 4, &tc)) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10356:     }
L10357:     return ESP_OK;
L10358: }
L10359: 
L10360: esp_err_t GateEngine::wait_done(uint32_t timeout_ms) {
L10361:     for (int ch = 0; ch < pulse::kGateChannels; ++ch) {
L10362:         const esp_err_t e = rmt_tx_wait_all_done(static_cast<rmt_channel_handle_t>(rmt_ch_[ch]), static_cast<int>(timeout_ms));
L10363:         if (e != ESP_OK) return e;
L10364:     }
L10365:     enabled_mask_ = 0;
L10366:     return ESP_OK;
L10367: }

L10371: void IRAM_ATTR GateEngine::on_capture(Input in, uint32_t t, bool rising) {
L10372:     const uint32_t bit = 1u << in;
L10373:     if (!(enabled_mask_ & bit)) {
L10374:         if (!(single_mask_ & bit)) return;
L10375:         single_mask_ = single_mask_ & ~bit;
L10376:     }
L10377:     const size_t k = nedges_;
L10378:     if (k < kMaxEdges) edges_[k] = {t, static_cast<uint8_t>(in), static_cast<uint8_t>(rising)};
L10379:     nedges_ = k + 1;
L10380: }

L10384: Write C:\Users\Liang\tigp-2026\nmr-instrument\firmware\components\adc\include\adc_stream.hpp

L10386: // Hardware-paced ADS8688 acquisition (ADR-0009).
L10387: //
L10388: // The ADS8688 is configured once over SPI (ranges, AUTO_SEQ on the I and Q channels, AUTO_RST), then the same four
L10389: // bus pins are handed to the I2S peripheral in full-duplex master mode: BCLK = SCLK, WS = /CS (low for the 32-bit
L10390: // left slot, high for the right slot), DOUT = SDI (zeros = NO_OP keeps the auto sequence running), DIN = SDO.
L10391: // One conversion per I2S frame at a crystal-derived rate (160 MHz / 10 = 16 MHz SCLK, 64 bits per frame =
L10392: // 250 kframes/s = 125 kS/s per channel), moved by DMA. The CPU only copies finished DMA buffers into a preallocated
L10393: // buffer; nothing paces samples in software. DMA queue overflows are counted, never silent.
L10394: #pragma once
L10395: 
L10396: #include <cstddef>
L10397: #include <cstdint>
L10398: 
L10399: #include "esp_err.h"
L10400: 
L10401: namespace fw {
L10402: 
L10403: struct AdcPins { int sclk, mosi, miso, cs; };
L10404: 
L10405: struct AdcConfig {
L10406:     AdcPins pins;
L10407:     uint32_t frame_rate;     // I2S frames per second = conversions per second (all channels)
L10408:     uint8_t ch_i, ch_q;      // ADS8688 channel numbers (I and Q)
L10409:     uint8_t range_code;      // ADS8688 range register value (1 = +-1.25 Vref = +-5.12 V)
L10410:     size_t capacity_frames;  // preallocated (PSRAM) buffer, frames
L10411: };
L10412: 
L10413: class AdcStream {
L10414: public:
L10415:     esp_err_t init(const AdcConfig& c);
L10416:     // Program the ADC over SPI (bus owned by SPI during the call, then released).
L10417:     esp_err_t configure_adc();
L10418:     // Start I2S streaming into the buffer from frame 0. The first WS falling edge after start is frame 0's /CS edge.
L10419:     esp_err_t start();
L10420:     // Copy DMA data until `frames` frames have been taken or the timeout passes. Returns frames taken.
L10421:     size_t collect(size_t frames, uint32_t timeout_ms);
L10422:     esp_err_t stop();
L10423:     // Raw 16-bit codes in conversion order (the auto sequence alternates I/Q; see parity in the docs).
L10424:     const int16_t* data() const { return buf_; }
L10425:     size_t frames() const { return taken_; }
L10426:     uint32_t overflows() const { return ovf_; }
L10427:     double frame_period_s() const { return 1.0 / cfg_.frame_rate; }
L10428:     void on_overflow() { ++ovf_; }
L10429: private:
L10430:     AdcConfig cfg_{};
L10431:     void* tx_ = nullptr;
L10432:     void* rx_ = nullptr;
L10433:     int16_t* buf_ = nullptr;   // capacity_frames entries
L10434:     uint32_t* dma_tmp_ = nullptr;
L10435:     size_t taken_ = 0;
L10436:     volatile uint32_t ovf_ = 0;
L10437:     bool running_ = false;
L10438: };

L10479: esp_err_t AdcStream::configure_adc() {
L10480:     spi_bus_config_t bus = {};
L10481:     bus.sclk_io_num = cfg_.pins.sclk;
L10482:     bus.mosi_io_num = cfg_.pins.mosi;
L10483:     bus.miso_io_num = cfg_.pins.miso;
L10484:     bus.quadwp_io_num = -1;
L10485:     bus.quadhd_io_num = -1;
L10486:     esp_err_t e = spi_bus_initialize(SPI2_HOST, &bus, SPI_DMA_DISABLED);
L10487:     if (e != ESP_OK) return e;
L10488:     spi_device_interface_config_t dev = {};
L10489:     dev.mode = 1;                       // ADS8688: CPOL 0, CPHA 1
L10490:     dev.clock_speed_hz = 4 * 1000 * 1000;
L10491:     dev.spics_io_num = cfg_.pins.cs;
L10492:     dev.queue_size = 1;
L10493:     spi_device_handle_t h;
L10494:     if ((e = spi_bus_add_device(SPI2_HOST, &dev, &h)) != ESP_OK) { spi_bus_free(SPI2_HOST); return e; }
L10495:     const auto xfer = [&](uint16_t cmd) {
L10496:         uint8_t tx[4] = {static_cast<uint8_t>(cmd >> 8), static_cast<uint8_t>(cmd), 0, 0}, rx[4];
L10497:         spi_transaction_t t = {};
L10498:         t.length = 32;
L10499:         t.tx_buffer = tx;
L10500:         t.rx_buffer = rx;
L10501:         return spi_device_polling_transmit(h, &t);
L10502:     };
L10503:     e = xfer(kRst);
L10504:     const uint8_t mask = static_cast<uint8_t>((1u << cfg_.ch_i) | (1u << cfg_.ch_q));
L10505:     if (e == ESP_OK) e = xfer(prog_write(0x01, mask));                       // AUTO_SEQ_EN
L10506:     if (e == ESP_OK) e = xfer(prog_write(0x02, static_cast<uint8_t>(~mask))); // power down the others
L10507:     if (e == ESP_OK) e = xfer(prog_write(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x05 + cfg_.ch_i), cfg_.range_code));
L10508:     if (e == ESP_OK) e = xfer(prog_write(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x05 + cfg_.ch_q), cfg_.range_code));
L10509:     if (e == ESP_OK) e = xfer(kAutoRst);
L10510:     spi_bus_remove_device(h);
L10511:     spi_bus_free(SPI2_HOST);
L10512:     return e;
L10513: }
L10514: 
L10515: esp_err_t AdcStream::start() {
L10516:     taken_ = 0;
L10517:     ovf_ = 0;
L10518:     i2s_chan_config_t cc = I2S_CHANNEL_DEFAULT_CONFIG(I2S_NUM_0, I2S_ROLE_MASTER);
L10519:     cc.dma_desc_num = kDmaBufs;
L10520:     cc.dma_frame_num = kDmaFrames;
L10521:     cc.auto_clear_before_cb = true;   // TX sends zeros = NO_OP commands
L10522:     i2s_chan_handle_t tx, rx;
L10523:     esp_err_t e = i2s_new_channel(&cc, &tx, &rx);
L10524:     if (e != ESP_OK) return e;
L10525:     i2s_std_config_t sc = {};
L10526:     sc.clk_cfg.sample_rate_hz = cfg_.frame_rate;
L10527:     sc.clk_cfg.clk_src = I2S_CLK_SRC_PLL_160M;
L10528:     sc.clk_cfg.mclk_multiple = I2S_MCLK_MULTIPLE_128;   // MCLK = 32 MHz = 160/5, BCLK = 16 MHz: integer dividers
L10529:     sc.slot_cfg = I2S_STD_MSB_SLOT_DEFAULT_CONFIG(I2S_DATA_BIT_WIDTH_32BIT, I2S_SLOT_MODE_STEREO);
L10530:     sc.gpio_cfg.mclk = I2S_GPIO_UNUSED;
L10531:     sc.gpio_cfg.bclk = static_cast<gpio_num_t>(cfg_.pins.sclk);
L10532:     sc.gpio_cfg.ws = static_cast<gpio_num_t>(cfg_.pins.cs);
L10533:     sc.gpio_cfg.dout = static_cast<gpio_num_t>(cfg_.pins.mosi);
L10534:     sc.gpio_cfg.din = static_cast<gpio_num_t>(cfg_.pins.miso);
L10535:     // ADS8688 is SPI mode 1: SDI sampled and SDO changed... the inverted bit clock makes the I2S transmitter change
L10536:     // on the external rising edge and the receiver sample on the external falling edge (VERIFY at bring-up, T-ADC-1)
L10537:     sc.gpio_cfg.invert_flags.bclk_inv = true;
L10538:     if ((e = i2s_channel_init_std_mode(tx, &sc)) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10539:     if ((e = i2s_channel_init_std_mode(rx, &sc)) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10540:     i2s_event_callbacks_t cb = {};
L10541:     cb.on_recv_q_ovf = on_ovf;
L10542:     i2s_channel_register_event_callback(rx, &cb, this);
L10543:     tx_ = tx;
L10544:     rx_ = rx;
L10545:     if ((e = i2s_channel_enable(tx)) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10546:     if ((e = i2s_channel_enable(rx)) != ESP_OK) return e;
L10547:     running_ = true;
L10548:     return ESP_OK;
L10549: }
L10550: 
L10551: size_t AdcStream::collect(size_t frames, uint32_t timeout_ms) {
L10552:     if (!running_) return 0;
L10553:     const TickType_t t_end = xTaskGetTickCount() + pdMS_TO_TICKS(timeout_ms);
L10554:     while (taken_ < frames && taken_ < cfg_.capacity_frames && xTaskGetTickCount() < t_end) {
L10555:         size_t got = 0;
L10556:         if (i2s_channel_read(static_cast<i2s_chan_handle_t>(rx_), dma_tmp_, kTmpWords * 4, &got, 20) != ESP_OK && got == 0) continue;
L10557:         const size_t nf = got / 8;
L10558:         for (size_t k = 0; k < nf && taken_ < cfg_.capacity_frames; ++k) {
L10559:             // left slot (CS low): 16 command clocks then the 16-bit result, MSB first -> low half of the 32-bit word
L10560:             buf_[taken_++] = static_cast<int16_t>(static_cast<uint16_t>(dma_tmp_[2 * k] & 0xFFFF) ^ 0x8000);
L10561:         }
L10562:     }
L10563:     return taken_;
L10564: }
L10565: 
L10566: esp_err_t AdcStream::stop() {
L10567:     if (!running_) return ESP_OK;
L10568:     auto tx = static_cast<i2s_chan_handle_t>(tx_), rx = static_cast<i2s_chan_handle_t>(rx_);
L10569:     i2s_channel_disable(rx);
L10570:     i2s_channel_disable(tx);
L10571:     i2s_del_channel(rx);
L10572:     i2s_del_channel(tx);
L10573:     running_ = false;
L10574:     return ESP_OK;
L10575: }

L10622: uint32_t dds_ftw(double mclk, double f, double* actual) {
L10623:     const uint32_t w = static_cast<uint32_t>(std::llround(f / mclk * 268435456.0)) & 0x0FFFFFFF;
L10624:     if (actual) *actual = w * mclk / 268435456.0;
L10625:     return w;
L10626: }
L10627: 
L10628: esp_err_t dds_program(const DdsPins& p, double mclk, double f, uint32_t ph0, uint32_t ph1) {
L10629:     spi_bus_config_t bus = {};
L10630:     bus.sclk_io_num = p.sclk;
L10631:     bus.mosi_io_num = p.mosi;
L10632:     bus.miso_io_num = -1;
L10633:     bus.quadwp_io_num = -1;
L10634:     bus.quadhd_io_num = -1;
L10635:     esp_err_t e = spi_bus_initialize(SPI2_HOST, &bus, SPI_DMA_DISABLED);
L10636:     if (e != ESP_OK) return e;
L10637:     spi_device_interface_config_t dev = {};
L10638:     dev.mode = 2;                        // AD9834: data clocked on SCLK falling edge, SCLK idles high
L10639:     dev.clock_speed_hz = 10 * 1000 * 1000;
L10640:     dev.spics_io_num = p.fsync;
L10641:     dev.queue_size = 1;
L10642:     spi_device_handle_t h;
L10643:     if ((e = spi_bus_add_device(SPI2_HOST, &dev, &h)) != ESP_OK) { spi_bus_free(SPI2_HOST); return e; }
L10644:     const auto w16 = [&](uint16_t v) {
L10645:         uint8_t b[2] = {static_cast<uint8_t>(v >> 8), static_cast<uint8_t>(v)};
L10646:         spi_transaction_t t = {};
L10647:         t.length = 16;
L10648:         t.tx_buffer = b;
L10649:         return spi_device_polling_transmit(h, &t);
L10650:     };
L10651:     constexpr uint16_t B28 = 1u << 13, PINSW = 1u << 9, RESET = 1u << 8, OPBITEN = 1u << 5, DIV2 = 1u << 3;
L10652:     const uint32_t ftw = dds_ftw(mclk, f, nullptr);
L10653:     const uint16_t p0 = static_cast<uint16_t>((ph0 >> 4) & 0x0FFF), p1 = static_cast<uint16_t>((ph1 >> 4) & 0x0FFF);
L10654:     const uint16_t seq[] = {
L10655:         static_cast<uint16_t>(B28 | RESET),                          // hold the accumulator while loading
L10656:         static_cast<uint16_t>(0x4000 | (ftw & 0x3FFF)),              // FREQ0 LSBs
L10657:         static_cast<uint16_t>(0x4000 | ((ftw >> 14) & 0x3FFF)),      // FREQ0 MSBs
L10658:         static_cast<uint16_t>(0xC000 | p0),                          // PHASE0
L10659:         static_cast<uint16_t>(0xE000 | p1),                          // PHASE1
L10660:         static_cast<uint16_t>(B28 | PINSW | OPBITEN | DIV2),         // run; FSELECT/PSELECT/RESET from the pins
L10661:     };
L10662:     for (uint16_t v : seq)
L10663:         if ((e = w16(v)) != ESP_OK) break;
L10664:     spi_bus_remove_device(h);
L10665:     spi_bus_free(SPI2_HOST);
L10666:     return e;
L10667: }
L10668: 
L10669: namespace {
L10670: struct Ms { uint32_t p1, p2, p3; };
L10671: Ms ms_params(uint32_t a, uint32_t b, uint32_t c) {   // AN619 eq. for multisynth / PLL feedback a + b/c
L10672:     const uint32_t f = static_cast<uint32_t>(128ull * b / c);
L10673:     return {128 * a + f - 512, 128 * b - c * f, c};
L10674: }
L10675: void pack(uint8_t* r, Ms m, uint8_t extra) {
L10676:     r[0] = static_cast<uint8_t>(m.p3 >> 8); r[1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(m.p3);
L10677:     r[2] = static_cast<uint8_t>(((m.p1 >> 16) & 0x03) | extra); r[3] = static_cast<uint8_t>(m.p1 >> 8);
L10678:     r[4] = static_cast<uint8_t>(m.p1); r[5] = static_cast<uint8_t>(((m.p3 >> 12) & 0xF0) | ((m.p2 >> 16) & 0x0F));
L10679:     r[6] = static_cast<uint8_t>(m.p2 >> 8); r[7] = static_cast<uint8_t>(m.p2);
L10680: }
L10681: }  // namespace
L10682: 
L10683: esp_err_t si5351_program(int sda, int scl, uint8_t addr, double xtal, double clk0, double f_lo, double* actual_lo) {
L10684:     i2c_master_bus_config_t bc = {};
L10685:     bc.i2c_port = I2C_NUM_0;
L10686:     bc.sda_io_num = static_cast<gpio_num_t>(sda);
L10687:     bc.scl_io_num = static_cast<gpio_num_t>(scl);
L10688:     bc.clk_source = I2C_CLK_SRC_DEFAULT;
L10689:     bc.glitch_ignore_cnt = 7;
L10690:     bc.flags.enable_internal_pullup = false;   // 4.7 k on the board
L10691:     i2c_master_bus_handle_t bus;
L10692:     esp_err_t e = i2c_new_master_bus(&bc, &bus);
L10693:     if (e != ESP_OK) return e;
L10694:     if ((e = i2c_master_probe(bus, addr, 50)) != ESP_OK) { i2c_del_master_bus(bus); return e; }   // absent: report
L10695:     i2c_device_config_t dc = {};
L10696:     dc.dev_addr_length = I2C_ADDR_BIT_LEN_7;
L10697:     dc.device_address = addr;
L10698:     dc.scl_speed_hz = 400000;
L10699:     i2c_master_dev_handle_t dev;
L10700:     if ((e = i2c_master_bus_add_device(bus, &dc, &dev)) != ESP_OK) { i2c_del_master_bus(bus); return e; }
L10701:     const auto wr = [&](uint8_t reg, const uint8_t* d, size_t n) {
L10702:         uint8_t b[10];
L10703:         b[0] = reg;
L10704:         for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) b[1 + i] = d[i];
L10705:         return i2c_master_transmit(dev, b, n + 1, 50);
L10706:     };
L10707:     const auto w1 = [&](uint8_t reg, uint8_t v) { return wr(reg, &v, 1); };
L10708:     uint8_t r[8];
L10709:     w1(3, 0xFF);                               // outputs off while programming
L10710:     // PLLA = 36 x 25 MHz = 900 MHz; MS0 = 18 (integer) -> 50 MHz
L10711:     const uint32_t pa = static_cast<uint32_t>(std::lround(clk0 * 18 / xtal));
L10712:     pack(r, ms_params(pa, 0, 1), 0); wr(26, r, 8);
L10713:     pack(r, ms_params(static_cast<uint32_t>(std::lround(xtal * pa / clk0)), 0, 1), 0); wr(42, r, 8);
L10714:     // CLK1 = 4 f_lo: R divider so the multisynth output stays >= 500 kHz, even integer MS1, fractional PLLB
L10715:     const double f1 = 4 * f_lo;
L10716:     uint8_t rdiv = 0;
L10717:     double out = f1;
L10718:     while (out < 500e3 && rdiv < 7) { out *= 2; ++rdiv; }
L10719:     uint32_t ms1 = static_cast<uint32_t>(900e6 / out) & ~1u;
L10720:     if (ms1 > 1800) ms1 = 1800;
L10721:     const double pll_b = out * ms1;
L10722:     const double ratio = pll_b / xtal;
L10723:     const uint32_t a = static_cast<uint32_t>(ratio), c = 1048575, b = static_cast<uint32_t>(std::lround((ratio - a) * c));
L10724:     pack(r, ms_params(a, b, c), 0); wr(34, r, 8);
L10725:     pack(r, ms_params(ms1, 0, 1), static_cast<uint8_t>(rdiv << 4)); wr(50, r, 8);
L10726:     w1(16, 0x4F);                              // CLK0: PLLA, multisynth, 8 mA
L10727:     w1(17, 0x6F);                              // CLK1: PLLB, multisynth, 8 mA
L10728:     w1(177, 0xA0);                             // reset PLLA and PLLB
L10729:     w1(3, 0xFC);                               // CLK0, CLK1 on
L10730:     if (actual_lo) *actual_lo = xtal * (a + static_cast<double>(b) / c) / ms1 / (1u << rdiv) / 4.0;
L10731:     i2c_master_bus_rm_device(dev);
L10732:     i2c_del_master_bus(bus);
L10733:     return ESP_OK;
L10734: }
